`panel/frontend/src/components/EmbedGuide.jsx`:

```jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { API_BASE } from '../apiConfig';

export default function EmbedGuide() {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [buyerName, setBuyerName] = useState('');
  const [buyerEmail, setBuyerEmail] = useState('');
  const [generatedKey, setGeneratedKey] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const panelOrigin = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : 'https://your-panel.onrender.com';

  async function handleDemoGenerate(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!buyerName.trim()) return;
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/admin/licenses/create`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          owner: buyerName,
          email: buyerEmail,
          tier: 'LIFETIME',
          maxServers: 1
        })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success) {
        setGeneratedKey(data.license.key);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      alert('Error connecting to panel backend.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const embedCodeSnippet = `<!-- Nexora AntiCheat Website Key Generator Widget -->
<div id="nexora-license-widget" style="max-width: 480px; background: #12151d; border: 1px solid #ff0000; padding: 24px; border-radius: 12px; color: #fff; font-family: sans-serif;">
  <h3 style="color: #ff0000; margin-bottom: 8px;">🛡️ Get Nexora AntiCheat License</h3>
  <p style="font-size: 13px; color: #8b94a5; margin-bottom: 16px;">Instant license key issuance for your Minecraft server.</p>
  
  <form id="nexora-form" onsubmit="generateKey(event)">
    <input id="nexora-name" type="text" placeholder="Server Name / Owner" required style="width: 100%; padding: 10px; margin-bottom: 10px; background: #0a0c10; border: 1px solid #232836; color: #fff; border-radius: 6px;" />
    <input id="nexora-email" type="email" placeholder="Email Address" required style="width: 100%; padding: 10px; margin-bottom: 14px; background: #0a0c10; border: 1px solid #232836; color: #fff; border-radius: 6px;" />
    <button type="submit" style="width: 100%; background: #ff0000; color: #fff; border: none; padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer;">⚡ Generate Instant License</button>
  </form>

  <div id="nexora-result" style="display: none; margin-top: 16px; padding: 12px; background: #181d27; border-radius: 6px; text-align: center;">
    <div style="font-size: 12px; color: #10b981;">License Activated!</div>
    <div id="nexora-key-display" style="font-family: monospace; font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; color: #ff0000; margin-top: 6px;"></div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
async function generateKey(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const name = document.getElementById('nexora-name').value;
  const email = document.getElementById('nexora-email').value;
  
  const res = await fetch('${panelOrigin}/api/admin/licenses/create', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ owner: name, email: email, tier: 'LIFETIME', maxServers: 1 })
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  if (data.success) {
    document.getElementById('nexora-key-display').innerText = data.license.key;
    document.getElementById('nexora-result').style.display = 'block';
  }
}
</script>`;

  function copyCode() {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(embedCodeSnippet);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2500);
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(360px, 1fr))', gap: '24px' }}>
      {/* Column 1: Live Interactive Widget Demo */}
      <div className="glass-panel" style={{ padding: '28px' }}>
        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#ff0000', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
          🌐 Website Buyer Checkout Form (Live Preview)
        </h3>
        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
          This is the exact form your buyers see on your store/website to get their license key.
        </p>

        <form onSubmit={handleDemoGenerate} style={{ background: '#0a0c10', padding: '20px', borderRadius: '10px', border: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
          <div style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }}>
            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
              SERVER NAME / OWNER NAME
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              className="input-dark"
              placeholder="e.g. PixelMC Network"
              value={buyerName}
              onChange={e => setBuyerName(e.target.value)}
              required
            />
          </div>

          <div style={{ marginBottom: '18px' }}>
            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
              BUYER EMAIL ADDRESS
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              className="input-dark"
              placeholder="buyer@domain.com"
              value={buyerEmail}
              onChange={e => setBuyerEmail(e.target.value)}
              required
            />
          </div>

          <button type="submit" className="btn-red" style={{ width: '100%', justifyContent: 'center' }} disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? 'Activating License...' : '⚡ Generate Instant Key'}
          </button>
        </form>

        {generatedKey && (
          <div style={{
            marginTop: '20px',
            background: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1)',
            border: '1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.4)',
            padding: '16px',
            borderRadius: '8px',
            textAlign: 'center'
          }}>
            <div style={{ color: '#10b981', fontWeight: '700', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              ✓ License Generated Successfully!
            </div>
            <div className="mono" style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#ff0000', marginTop: '8px' }}>
              {generatedKey}
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: '4px' }}>
              Paste this key into your Minecraft server config.yml to begin protection.
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Column 2: Code Snippet to Copy */}
      <div className="glass-panel" style={{ padding: '28px' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '12px' }}>
          <h3 style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#ffffff' }}>
            📋 Copy HTML / React Embed Code
          </h3>
          <button onClick={copyCode} className="btn-secondary" style={{ fontSize: '12px' }}>
            {copied ? '✓ Copied Code!' : '📋 Copy Embed Code'}
          </button>
        </div>

        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
          Drop this HTML / JS snippet into your WordPress, Tebex, CraftingStore, or custom React website:
        </p>

        <pre className="mono" style={{
          background: '#0a0c10',
          border: '1px solid var(--border)',
          padding: '16px',
          borderRadius: '8px',
          fontSize: '11px',
          color: '#38bdf8',
          maxHeight: '340px',
          overflowY: 'auto',
          whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap'
        }}>
          {embedCodeSnippet}
        </pre>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`panel/frontend/src/components/KeyGeneratorModal.jsx`:

```jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { API_BASE } from '../apiConfig';

export default function KeyGeneratorModal({ isOpen, onClose, onCreated }) {
  const [owner, setOwner] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [tier, setTier] = useState('LIFETIME');
  const [durationDays, setDurationDays] = useState('30');
  const [maxServers, setMaxServers] = useState('1');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!owner.trim()) {
      setError('Buyer / Owner name is required.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/admin/licenses/create`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          owner,
          email,
          tier,
          durationDays: tier === 'CUSTOM' ? durationDays : undefined,
          maxServers: Number(maxServers)
        })
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success) {
        onCreated(data.license);
        onClose();
      } else {
        setError(data.error || 'Failed to generate key.');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Cannot connect to panel backend. Ensure backend is running.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div style={{
      position: 'fixed',
      top: 0,
      left: 0,
      right: 0,
      bottom: 0,
      background: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(5px)',
      display: 'flex',
      alignItems: 'center',
      justifyContent: 'center',
      zIndex: 1000,
      padding: '20px'
    }}>
      <div className="glass-panel" style={{
        maxWidth: '520px',
        width: '100%',
        padding: '28px',
        position: 'relative',
        boxShadow: '0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.6)'
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
          <div>
            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: '700', color: '#ff0000', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
              🔑 Generate Nexora License Key
            </h3>
            <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '13px', marginTop: '4px' }}>
              Creates a cryptographically unique key for a server buyer with strict host limits.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} style={{
            background: 'none',
            border: 'none',
            color: 'var(--text-muted)',
            fontSize: '22px',
            cursor: 'pointer'
          }}>✕</button>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div style={{
            background: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
            border: '1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.4)',
            color: '#ef4444',
            padding: '10px 14px',
            borderRadius: '8px',
            fontSize: '13px',
            marginBottom: '16px'
          }}>
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }}>
            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
              BUYER / CUSTOMER NAME *
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              className="input-dark"
              placeholder="e.g. Hypixel Network LLC or player name"
              value={owner}
              onChange={e => setOwner(e.target.value)}
              required
            />
          </div>

          <div style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }}>
            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
              BUYER EMAIL ADDRESS
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              className="input-dark"
              placeholder="e.g. admin@network.net"
              value={email}
              onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '14px', marginBottom: '14px' }}>
            <div>
              <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                LICENSE DURATION / TIER
              </label>
              <select
                className="input-dark"
                value={tier}
                onChange={e => setTier(e.target.value)}
              >
                <option value="LIFETIME">Lifetime (No Expiry)</option>
                <option value="1_YEAR">1 Year (365 Days)</option>
                <option value="30_DAYS">30 Days (Monthly)</option>
                <option value="CUSTOM">Custom Days</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                MAX SERVERS ALLOWED
              </label>
              <select
                className="input-dark"
                value={maxServers}
                onChange={e => setMaxServers(e.target.value)}
              >
                <option value="1">1 Server (Single Hosting)</option>
                <option value="2">2 Servers (Network Dual)</option>
                <option value="5">5 Servers (BungeeCord Fleet)</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {tier === 'CUSTOM' && (
            <div style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
              <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                CUSTOM DURATION IN DAYS
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="1"
                className="input-dark"
                value={durationDays}
                onChange={e => setDurationDays(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: '10px', marginTop: '20px' }}>
            <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="btn-secondary">
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button type="submit" className="btn-red" disabled={loading}>
              {loading ? 'Generating...' : '⚡ Generate Key'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`panel/frontend/src/components/UserPanel.jsx`:

```jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { API_BASE } from '../apiConfig';

export default function UserPanel() {
  // Authentication states
  const [authMode, setAuthMode] = useState('login'); // 'login' or 'signup'
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [licenseKey, setLicenseKey] = useState('');

  // Active session
  const [userSession, setUserSession] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('nexora_user_session');
      return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  });

  const [portalData, setPortalData] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState('');
  const [resetMsg, setResetMsg] = useState('');

  // Ban Player Form States
  const [banPlayerName, setBanPlayerName] = useState('');
  const [banReason, setBanReason] = useState('');
  const [banDuration, setBanDuration] = useState('PERMANENT');
  const [banIp, setBanIp] = useState(false);
  const [banLoading, setBanLoading] = useState(false);
  const [banMsg, setBanMsg] = useState('');

  // Auto-load portal data when session exists
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!userSession) return;
    loadPortalData();
    const interval = setInterval(loadPortalData, 10000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [userSession]);

  async function loadPortalData() {
    if (!userSession?.licenseKey) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/user/portal`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          key: userSession.licenseKey,
          username: userSession.username
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setPortalData(data);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error fetching portal data:', e);
    }
  }

  // Handle Login
  async function handleLogin(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setSuccessMsg('');

    if (!username.trim() || !password || !licenseKey.trim()) {
      setError('Please fill in Username, Password, and License Key.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/auth/login`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          username: username.trim(),
          password,
          licenseKey: licenseKey.trim().toUpperCase()
        })
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.success) {
        setUserSession(data.user);
        localStorage.setItem('nexora_user_session', JSON.stringify(data.user));
        setError('');
      } else {
        setError(data.error || 'Login failed. Please check credentials.');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Cannot connect to panel backend. Ensure the server is running.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  // Handle Signup
  async function handleSignup(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setSuccessMsg('');

    if (!username.trim() || !password || !confirmPassword || !licenseKey.trim()) {
      setError('All fields are required.');
      return;
    }

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError('Passwords do not match.');
      return;
    }

    if (password.length < 6) {
      setError('Password must be at least 6 characters.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/auth/signup`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          username: username.trim(),
          password,
          confirmPassword,
          licenseKey: licenseKey.trim().toUpperCase()
        })
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.success) {
        setSuccessMsg('Account registered successfully! You can now log in.');
        setAuthMode('login');
        setPassword('');
        setConfirmPassword('');
      } else {
        setError(data.error || 'Registration failed.');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Cannot connect to panel backend. Ensure the server is running.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  // Handle Logout
  function handleLogout() {
    setUserSession(null);
    setPortalData(null);
    localStorage.removeItem('nexora_user_session');
  }

  // User bans a player on their server
  async function handleBanPlayer(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!banPlayerName.trim()) return;

    setBanLoading(true);
    setBanMsg('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/user/players/ban`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          licenseKey: userSession.licenseKey,
          username: userSession.username,
          playerName: banPlayerName.trim(),
          reason: banReason.trim(),
          duration: banDuration,
          ipBanned: banIp
        })
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.success) {
        setBanMsg(`✅ ${data.message}`);
        setBanPlayerName('');
        setBanReason('');
        loadPortalData();
      } else {
        setBanMsg(`❌ ${data.error || 'Failed to ban player.'}`);
      }
    } catch {
      setBanMsg('❌ Error connecting to server.');
    } finally {
      setBanLoading(false);
    }
  }

  // User unbans a player
  async function handleUnbanPlayer(banId, pName) {
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to unban "${pName}"?`)) return;

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/user/players/unban`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          licenseKey: userSession.licenseKey,
          banId
        })
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.success) {
        loadPortalData();
      } else {
        alert(data.error || 'Failed to unban player.');
      }
    } catch {
      alert('Error connecting to backend.');
    }
  }

  // User resets their IP binding
  async function handleResetIp() {
    if (!confirm('Are you migrating your Minecraft server to a new IP or host? Click OK to reset binding.')) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/user/reset-ip`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ key: userSession.licenseKey })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        setResetMsg(data.message);
        loadPortalData();
      } else {
        setError(data.error || 'Failed to reset IP binding.');
      }
    } catch {
      setError('Failed to contact backend server.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // VIEW 1: AUTHENTICATION FORM (LOGIN / SIGNUP)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  if (!userSession) {
    return (
      <div style={{ maxWidth: '480px', margin: '40px auto 0' }}>
        <div className="glass-panel" style={{ padding: '32px 28px' }}>
          {/* Header */}
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
            <div style={{
              display: 'inline-flex',
              padding: '12px',
              borderRadius: '14px',
              background: 'rgba(255, 0, 0, 0.1)',
              border: '1px solid rgba(255, 0, 0, 0.25)',
              marginBottom: '12px'
            }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '28px' }}>🛡️</span>
            </div>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#ffffff', letterSpacing: '-0.5px' }}>
              Nexora Client Portal
            </h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#8b94a5', marginTop: '6px' }}>
              Sign in with your license key to manage server protections & player bans
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Tab Selector: Login vs Sign Up */}
          <div style={{
            display: 'flex',
            background: '#0d1017',
            padding: '4px',
            borderRadius: '8px',
            marginBottom: '20px',
            border: '1px solid var(--border)'
          }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { setAuthMode('login'); setError(''); setSuccessMsg(''); }}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                padding: '10px 0',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                border: 'none',
                fontWeight: '600',
                fontSize: '13px',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                background: authMode === 'login' ? 'var(--primary)' : 'transparent',
                color: authMode === 'login' ? '#ffffff' : '#8b94a5',
                transition: 'all 0.15s ease'
              }}
            >
              Sign In
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { setAuthMode('signup'); setError(''); setSuccessMsg(''); }}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                padding: '10px 0',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                border: 'none',
                fontWeight: '600',
                fontSize: '13px',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                background: authMode === 'signup' ? 'var(--primary)' : 'transparent',
                color: authMode === 'signup' ? '#ffffff' : '#8b94a5',
                transition: 'all 0.15s ease'
              }}
            >
              Create Account
            </button>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div style={{
              padding: '10px 14px',
              borderRadius: '8px',
              background: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
              border: '1px solid #ef4444',
              color: '#fca5a5',
              fontSize: '13px',
              marginBottom: '16px'
            }}>
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {successMsg && (
            <div style={{
              padding: '10px 14px',
              borderRadius: '8px',
              background: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15)',
              border: '1px solid #10b981',
              color: '#6ee7b7',
              fontSize: '13px',
              marginBottom: '16px'
            }}>
              {successMsg}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Form */}
          <form onSubmit={authMode === 'login' ? handleLogin : handleSignup}>
            <div style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }}>
              <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#c9d1d9', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                Username
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={username}
                onChange={e => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Enter your username"
                required
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  padding: '11px 14px',
                  borderRadius: '8px',
                  background: '#0a0c10',
                  border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                  color: '#ffffff',
                  fontSize: '14px',
                  outline: 'none',
                  boxSizing: 'border-box'
                }}
              />
            </div>

            <div style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }}>
              <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#c9d1d9', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                License Key
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={licenseKey}
                onChange={e => setLicenseKey(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
                placeholder="NX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX"
                required
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  padding: '11px 14px',
                  borderRadius: '8px',
                  background: '#0a0c10',
                  border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                  color: '#ffffff',
                  fontSize: '14px',
                  fontFamily: 'monospace',
                  letterSpacing: '1px',
                  outline: 'none',
                  boxSizing: 'border-box'
                }}
              />
            </div>

            <div style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }}>
              <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#c9d1d9', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                Password
              </label>
              <input
                type="password"
                value={password}
                onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Enter password"
                required
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  padding: '11px 14px',
                  borderRadius: '8px',
                  background: '#0a0c10',
                  border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                  color: '#ffffff',
                  fontSize: '14px',
                  outline: 'none',
                  boxSizing: 'border-box'
                }}
              />
            </div>

            {authMode === 'signup' && (
              <div style={{ marginBottom: '18px' }}>
                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#c9d1d9', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                  Confirm Password
                </label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  value={confirmPassword}
                  onChange={e => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Repeat your password"
                  required
                  style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    padding: '11px 14px',
                    borderRadius: '8px',
                    background: '#0a0c10',
                    border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                    color: '#ffffff',
                    fontSize: '14px',
                    outline: 'none',
                    boxSizing: 'border-box'
                  }}
                />
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="btn btn-primary"
              style={{
                width: '100%',
                padding: '12px 0',
                fontSize: '14px',
                fontWeight: '700',
                marginTop: '10px'
              }}
            >
              {loading ? 'Please wait...' : authMode === 'login' ? '🔐 Log In' : '✨ Register Account'}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // VIEW 2: LOGGED-IN BUYER DASHBOARD & BAN PLAYER CONTROL
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  const license = portalData?.license;
  const server = portalData?.server;
  const violations = portalData?.violations || [];
  const playerBans = portalData?.playerBans || [];

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Top Session Bar */}
      <div className="glass-panel" style={{
        padding: '16px 20px',
        marginBottom: '24px',
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        alignItems: 'center',
        flexWrap: 'wrap',
        gap: '12px'
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
          <div style={{
            width: '36px',
            height: '36px',
            borderRadius: '50%',
            background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #ff0000 0%, #aa0000 100%)',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            fontSize: '16px'
          }}>
            👤
          </div>
          <div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '15px', fontWeight: '700', color: '#ffffff' }}>
              Welcome back, <span style={{ color: '#ff4444' }}>{userSession.username}</span>
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#8b94a5', fontFamily: 'monospace' }}>
              License: <span style={{ color: '#c9d1d9' }}>{userSession.licenseKey}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={handleLogout}
          className="btn btn-secondary"
          style={{ padding: '8px 14px', fontSize: '12px' }}
        >
          🚪 Sign Out
        </button>
      </div>

      {resetMsg && (
        <div style={{
          padding: '12px 16px',
          borderRadius: '8px',
          background: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15)',
          border: '1px solid #10b981',
          color: '#6ee7b7',
          fontSize: '13px',
          marginBottom: '20px'
        }}>
          {resetMsg}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Grid: Server Status & IP Reset */}
      <div style={{
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr))',
        gap: '20px',
        marginBottom: '24px'
      }}>
        {/* Bound Server Stats */}
        <div className="glass-panel" style={{ padding: '20px' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '14px' }}>
            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '15px', fontWeight: '700', color: '#ffffff' }}>
              🖥️ Bound Minecraft Server
            </h3>
            {server ? (
              <span className={`badge ${server.isOnline ? 'badge-online' : 'badge-offline'}`}>
                {server.isOnline ? 'ONLINE' : 'OFFLINE'}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="badge badge-pending">NOT CONNECTED</span>
            )}
          </div>

          {server ? (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#8b94a5' }}>Server Name:</span>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: '600', color: '#ffffff' }}>{server.serverName}</span>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#8b94a5' }}>Host IP & Port:</span>
                <span style={{ fontFamily: 'monospace', color: '#38bdf8' }}>{server.ip}:{server.port}</span>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#8b94a5' }}>Engine Version:</span>
                <span style={{ color: '#c9d1d9' }}>{server.mcVersion}</span>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#8b94a5' }}>Online Players:</span>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: '700', color: '#10b981' }}>{server.onlinePlayers} players</span>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#8b94a5' }}>Server TPS:</span>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: '700', color: server.tps >= 19 ? '#10b981' : '#f59e0b' }}>
                  {server.tps} TPS
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div style={{ color: '#8b94a5', fontSize: '13px', lineHeight: '1.6' }}>
              No Minecraft server has connected with this license yet. Install Nexora AntiCheat on your server and enter your key in <code>config.yml</code>.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* License & IP Binding Migration */}
        <div className="glass-panel" style={{ padding: '20px' }}>
          <h3 style={{ fontSize: '15px', fontWeight: '700', color: '#ffffff', marginBottom: '14px' }}>
            🔒 License & Hardware Binding
          </h3>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', fontSize: '13px' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
              <span style={{ color: '#8b94a5' }}>Subscription Tier:</span>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: '700', color: '#ff4444' }}>{license?.tier || 'LIFETIME'}</span>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
              <span style={{ color: '#8b94a5' }}>License Status:</span>
              <span className={`badge ${license?.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'badge-online' : 'badge-offline'}`}>
                {license?.status || 'ACTIVE'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
              <span style={{ color: '#8b94a5' }}>Bound Host IP:</span>
              <span style={{ fontFamily: 'monospace', color: license?.boundIp ? '#38bdf8' : '#8b94a5' }}>
                {license?.boundIp || 'Unbound (Ready to lock)'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ marginTop: '18px', paddingTop: '14px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#8b94a5', marginBottom: '10px' }}>
              Moving to a new hosting provider or VPS? Reset your IP binding before starting from your new server:
            </p>
            <button
              onClick={handleResetIp}
              disabled={loading}
              className="btn btn-secondary"
              style={{ width: '100%', fontSize: '13px' }}
            >
              🔄 Reset Server IP Binding
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* SECTION: PLAYER BAN MANAGEMENT */}
      <div className="glass-panel" style={{ padding: '24px', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '18px', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '10px' }}>
          <div>
            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '17px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#ffffff' }}>
              🔨 Player Ban Control & History
            </h3>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#8b94a5', marginTop: '4px' }}>
              Remotely ban cheaters and rule-breakers on your Minecraft server
            </p>
          </div>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#8b94a5', background: '#0a0c10', padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: '6px', border: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
            Active Bans: <strong style={{ color: '#ff4444' }}>{playerBans.filter(b => b.status === 'ACTIVE').length}</strong>
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Ban Form */}
        <form onSubmit={handleBanPlayer} style={{
          display: 'grid',
          gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(180px, 1fr))',
          gap: '12px',
          background: '#0a0c10',
          padding: '16px',
          borderRadius: '10px',
          border: '1px solid var(--border)',
          marginBottom: '20px'
        }}>
          <div>
            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#8b94a5', marginBottom: '4px' }}>
              Player Name
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={banPlayerName}
              onChange={e => setBanPlayerName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g. BadCheater"
              required
              style={{
                width: '100%',
                padding: '9px 12px',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                background: '#12151d',
                border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                color: '#ffffff',
                fontSize: '13px',
                outline: 'none',
                boxSizing: 'border-box'
              }}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#8b94a5', marginBottom: '4px' }}>
              Ban Reason
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={banReason}
              onChange={e => setBanReason(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g. KillAura / Speed Cheating"
              style={{
                width: '100%',
                padding: '9px 12px',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                background: '#12151d',
                border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                color: '#ffffff',
                fontSize: '13px',
                outline: 'none',
                boxSizing: 'border-box'
              }}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#8b94a5', marginBottom: '4px' }}>
              Duration
            </label>
            <select
              value={banDuration}
              onChange={e => setBanDuration(e.target.value)}
              style={{
                width: '100%',
                padding: '9px 12px',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                background: '#12151d',
                border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                color: '#ffffff',
                fontSize: '13px',
                outline: 'none',
                boxSizing: 'border-box'
              }}
            >
              <option value="PERMANENT">Permanent Ban</option>
              <option value="30_DAYS">30 Days</option>
              <option value="7_DAYS">7 Days</option>
              <option value="24_HOURS">24 Hours</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
            <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '12px', color: '#c9d1d9', marginTop: '16px' }}>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={banIp}
                onChange={e => setBanIp(e.target.checked)}
                style={{ accentColor: '#ff0000', cursor: 'pointer' }}
              />
              Also Ban Player IP
            </label>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={banLoading}
              className="btn btn-primary"
              style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 0', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: '700' }}
            >
              {banLoading ? 'Executing...' : '🔨 Ban Player'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>

        {banMsg && (
          <div style={{
            fontSize: '13px',
            marginBottom: '16px',
            padding: '8px 12px',
            borderRadius: '6px',
            background: '#0d1017'
          }}>
            {banMsg}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Banned Players Table */}
        {playerBans.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '24px 0', color: '#8b94a5', fontSize: '13px' }}>
            No players currently banned on this server.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
            <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ textAlign: 'left', color: '#8b94a5', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>Player</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>Reason</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>Duration</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>IP Ban</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>Banned By</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>Status</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px', textAlign: 'right' }}>Action</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {playerBans.map(b => (
                  <tr key={b.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)' }}>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#ffffff' }}>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <img
                          src={`https://mc-heads.net/avatar/${encodeURIComponent(b.playerName)}/20`}
                          alt=""
                          style={{ width: '20px', height: '20px', borderRadius: '4px' }}
                          onError={e => { e.target.style.display = 'none'; }}
                        />
                        {b.playerName}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', color: '#c9d1d9' }}>{b.reason}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', fontFamily: 'monospace', color: '#f59e0b' }}>{b.duration}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>
                      {b.ipBanned ? (
                        <span className="badge badge-offline" style={{ fontSize: '10px' }}>IP BANNED</span>
                      ) : (
                        <span style={{ color: '#8b94a5' }}>No</span>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', color: '#8b94a5' }}>{b.bannedBy}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>
                      <span className={`badge ${b.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'badge-offline' : 'badge-online'}`} style={{ fontSize: '11px' }}>
                        {b.status}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
                      {b.status === 'ACTIVE' && (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleUnbanPlayer(b.id, b.playerName)}
                          className="btn btn-secondary"
                          style={{ padding: '4px 10px', fontSize: '11px' }}
                        >
                          Pardon / Unban
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* SECTION: VIOLATION HISTORY LOG */}
      <div className="glass-panel" style={{ padding: '24px', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '18px' }}>
          <div>
            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '17px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#ffffff' }}>
              🛡️ Live Cheat Violation History
            </h3>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#8b94a5', marginTop: '4px' }}>
              Real-time anti-cheat detections flagged on your Minecraft server
            </p>
          </div>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#8b94a5' }}>
            Total Flags: <strong style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>{violations.length}</strong>
          </span>
        </div>

        {violations.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '30px 0', color: '#8b94a5', fontSize: '13px' }}>
            🎉 No cheat violations detected on your server! Everything is clean.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
            <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ textAlign: 'left', color: '#8b94a5', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>Player</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>Check</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>Violation Level (VL)</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>Details</th>
                  <th style={{ padding: '10px 12px', textAlign: 'right' }}>Time</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {violations.map(v => (
                  <tr key={v.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)' }}>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#ffffff' }}>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <img
                          src={`https://mc-heads.net/avatar/${encodeURIComponent(v.playerName)}/20`}
                          alt=""
                          style={{ width: '20px', height: '20px', borderRadius: '4px' }}
                          onError={e => { e.target.style.display = 'none'; }}
                        />
                        {v.playerName}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px' }}>
                      <span className="badge badge-pending" style={{ fontSize: '11px' }}>
                        {v.checkId}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', fontFamily: 'monospace', color: '#ff4444', fontWeight: '700' }}>
                      VL {v.vl}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', color: '#8b94a5', fontSize: '12px' }}>{v.details}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', textAlign: 'right', color: '#8b94a5', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                      {new Date(v.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* SECTION: MINECRAFT CONFIG SNIPPET */}
      <div className="glass-panel" style={{ padding: '20px' }}>
        <h4 style={{ fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: '700', color: '#ffffff', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
          📋 Server Configuration (`plugins/NexoraAntiCheat/config.yml`)
        </h4>
        <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#8b94a5', marginBottom: '12px' }}>
          Paste this block into your Minecraft server configuration to activate protection:
        </p>
        <pre style={{
          background: '#0a0c10',
          border: '1px solid var(--border)',
          padding: '14px',
          borderRadius: '8px',
          fontSize: '12px',
          color: '#38bdf8',
          overflowX: 'auto'
        }}>
{`license:
  enabled: true
  key: "${userSession.licenseKey}"
  server-name: "${server ? server.serverName : 'My Minecraft Server'}"
  api-url: "${typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : 'https://your-panel.onrender.com'}"
  heartbeat-interval-seconds: 60`}
        </pre>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```

```yaml
      - key: DATABASE_URL
        sync: false
```

`panel/supabase_schema.sql`:

```sql
-- ==========================================================
--     NEXORA ANTICHEAT - SUPABASE POSTGRESQL SCHEMA
-- ==========================================================
-- You can run this script directly in your Supabase SQL Editor:
-- Dashboard -> SQL Editor -> New Query -> Paste & Run!
--
-- Note: The Nexora Panel backend also auto-creates these tables
-- automatically when connected via DATABASE_URL!
-- ==========================================================

-- 1. Licenses Table (cryptographic keys, tiers, owner, bound IP)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_licenses (
    key VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    owner VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(255),
    tier VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'LIFETIME',
    status VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    bound_ip VARCHAR(64),
    max_servers INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at BIGINT NOT NULL,
    expires_at BIGINT,
    ban_reason TEXT
);

-- 2. Registered Users Table (Auth with Username, Password Hash, Salt & License Key)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_users (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(64) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    salt VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    license_key VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES nexora_licenses(key) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    role VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER',
    created_at BIGINT NOT NULL,
    last_login BIGINT
);

-- 3. Minecraft Servers Fleet Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_servers (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    license_key VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES nexora_licenses(key) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    server_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    ip VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    port INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 25565,
    mc_version VARCHAR(128),
    plugin_version VARCHAR(64),
    online_players INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tps DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL DEFAULT 20.0,
    status VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ONLINE',
    last_heartbeat BIGINT NOT NULL,
    first_seen BIGINT NOT NULL
);

-- 4. User Controlled Player Bans Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_player_bans (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    license_key VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES nexora_licenses(key) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    server_name VARCHAR(255),
    player_name VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    duration VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PERMANENT',
    banned_by VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    ip_banned BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    banned_at BIGINT NOT NULL,
    expires_at BIGINT,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
);

-- 5. Telemetry & Cheat Violations Log Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_violations (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    license_key VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES nexora_licenses(key) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    server_name VARCHAR(255),
    player_name VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    check_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    vl DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL DEFAULT 1.0,
    details TEXT,
    timestamp BIGINT NOT NULL
);

-- Performance Indexes
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_username ON nexora_users(username);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_license ON nexora_users(license_key);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_servers_license ON nexora_servers(license_key);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_servers_status ON nexora_servers(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_player_bans_license ON nexora_player_bans(license_key);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_violations_license ON nexora_violations(license_key);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_violations_timestamp ON nexora_violations(timestamp DESC);

```

`pom.xml`:

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0"
         xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
         xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0 http://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>com.nexora.anticheat</groupId>
    <artifactId>nexora-anticheat</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0</version>
    <packaging>jar</packaging>

    <name>NexoraAntiCheat</name>
    <description>Enterprise-grade, lag-compensated anticheat for modern Paper servers</description>

    <properties>
        <java.version>21</java.version>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
        <paper.version>26.2.build.134-stable</paper.version>
        <hikari.version>6.2.1</hikari.version>
        <sqlite.version>3.47.1.0</sqlite.version>
        <junit.version>5.11.3</junit.version>
    </properties>

    <repositories>
        <repository>
            <id>papermc</id>
            <name>PaperMC Public Repository</name>
            <url>https://repo.papermc.io/repository/maven-public/</url>
        </repository>
        <repository>
            <id>sonatype-oss</id>
            <url>https://oss.sonatype.org/content/repositories/snapshots/</url>
        </repository>
    </repositories>

    <dependencies>
        <!-- Paper API -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>io.papermc.paper</groupId>
            <artifactId>paper-api</artifactId>
            <version>${paper.version}</version>
            <scope>provided</scope>
        </dependency>

        <!-- Database Connection Pooling -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>com.zaxxer</groupId>
            <artifactId>HikariCP</artifactId>
            <version>${hikari.version}</version>
            <scope>compile</scope>
        </dependency>

        <!-- SQLite Driver for Local Storage -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.xerial</groupId>
            <artifactId>sqlite-jdbc</artifactId>
            <version>${sqlite.version}</version>
            <scope>compile</scope>
        </dependency>

        <!-- Testing Framework -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.junit.jupiter</groupId>
            <artifactId>junit-jupiter</artifactId>
            <version>${junit.version}</version>
            <scope>test</scope>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>

    <build>
        <defaultGoal>clean package</defaultGoal>
        <resources>
            <resource>
                <directory>src/main/resources</directory>
                <filtering>true</filtering>
            </resource>
        </resources>
        <plugins>
            <!-- Compiler Plugin -->
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
                <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>3.13.0</version>
                <configuration>
                    <release>${java.version}</release>
                    <encoding>${project.build.sourceEncoding}</encoding>
                </configuration>
            </plugin>

            <!-- Surefire Test Runner Plugin -->
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
                <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>3.5.2</version>
            </plugin>

            <!-- Shading Plugin -->
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
                <artifactId>maven-shade-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>3.6.0</version>
                <executions>
                    <execution>
                        <phase>package</phase>
                        <goals>
                            <goal>shade</goal>
                        </goals>
                        <configuration>
                            <createDependencyReducedPom>false</createDependencyReducedPom>
                            <relocations>
                                <relocation>
                                    <pattern>com.zaxxer.hikari</pattern>
                                    <shadedPattern>com.nexora.anticheat.libs.hikari</shadedPattern>
                                </relocation>
                            </relocations>
                            <filters>
                                <filter>
                                    <artifact>*:*</artifact>
                                    <excludes>
                                        <exclude>META-INF/*.SF</exclude>
                                        <exclude>META-INF/*.DSA</exclude>
                                        <exclude>META-INF/*.RSA</exclude>
                                    </excludes>
                                </filter>
                            </filters>
                        </configuration>
                    </execution>
                </executions>
            </plugin>
        </plugins>
    </build>
</project>
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/NexoraPlugin.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat;

import com.nexora.anticheat.alert.AlertManager;
import com.nexora.anticheat.api.NexoraAPI;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckManager;
import com.nexora.anticheat.command.NexoraCommand;
import com.nexora.anticheat.config.ConfigManager;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerManager;
import com.nexora.anticheat.gui.StaffGuiManager;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.PunishmentManager;
import com.nexora.anticheat.storage.StorageManager;
import io.papermc.paper.plugin.lifecycle.event.types.LifecycleEvents;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.minimessage.MiniMessage;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;

/**
 * Main entry point for the Nexora AntiCheat plugin on Paper 26.2.
 */
public final class NexoraPlugin extends JavaPlugin {

    private ConfigManager configManager;
    private StorageManager storageManager;
    private PlayerManager playerManager;
    private AlertManager alertManager;
    private PunishmentManager punishmentManager;
    private com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.BanManager banManager;
    private com.nexora.anticheat.license.LicenseManager licenseManager;
    private CheckManager checkManager;
    private StaffGuiManager staffGuiManager;

    @Override
    public void onEnable() {
        long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // 1. Initialize configuration manager
        this.configManager = new ConfigManager(this);
        this.configManager.loadAll();

        // 1b. License Key Verification (Single-Host & Web Panel Authentication)
        this.licenseManager = new com.nexora.anticheat.license.LicenseManager(this);
        if (!this.licenseManager.verifyLicenseSync()) {
            // License validation failed; plugin self-disabled
            return;
        }

        // 1c. Initialize Bedrock / GeyserMC detection utility
        String bedrockPrefix = this.configManager.getConfig().getString("settings.bedrock.prefix", ".");
        com.nexora.anticheat.util.BedrockUtil.init(bedrockPrefix);

        // 2. Initialize storage manager (SQLite / MySQL)
        this.storageManager = new StorageManager(this);
        this.storageManager.init();

        // 3. Initialize player telemetry & state manager
        this.playerManager = new PlayerManager(this);
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(this.playerManager, this);

        // 4. Initialize alert dispatcher
        this.alertManager = new AlertManager(this);

        // 5. Initialize punishment & progressive ban managers
        this.punishmentManager = new PunishmentManager(this);
        this.banManager = new com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.BanManager(this);
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(this.banManager, this);

        // 6. Initialize staff interactive GUI manager
        this.staffGuiManager = new StaffGuiManager(this);
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(this.staffGuiManager, this);

        // 7. Initialize and register all anti-cheat checks
        this.checkManager = new CheckManager(this);
        this.checkManager.registerAll();

        // 8. Initialize Developer API
        NexoraAPI.init(this);

        // 9. Register modern Paper Brigadier commands via Lifecycle API
        this.getLifecycleManager().registerEventHandler(LifecycleEvents.COMMANDS, event -> {
            new NexoraCommand(this).register(event.registrar());
        });

        long duration = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;
        Component enableMsg = MiniMessage.miniMessage().deserialize(
                "<#FF0000><bold>Nexora AntiCheat</bold></#FF0000> <green>v" + getPluginMeta().getVersion()
                        + " enabled successfully in " + duration + "ms on Paper 26.2!</green>"
        );
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(enableMsg);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDisable() {
        if (this.storageManager != null) {
            this.storageManager.close();
        }

        if (this.playerManager != null) {
            this.playerManager.clearAll();
        }

        Component disableMsg = MiniMessage.miniMessage().deserialize(
                "<#FF0000><bold>Nexora AntiCheat</bold></#FF0000> <gray>has been disabled safely.</gray>"
        );
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(disableMsg);
    }

    /**
     * Hot-reloads all configurations and check parameters without restarting the server.
     */
    public void reloadPlugin() {
        this.configManager.reload();
        this.checkManager.reload();
    }

    public ConfigManager getConfigManager() {
        return configManager;
    }

    public StorageManager getStorageManager() {
        return storageManager;
    }

    public PlayerManager getPlayerDataManager() {
        return playerManager;
    }

    public AlertManager getAlertManager() {
        return alertManager;
    }

    public PunishmentManager getPunishmentManager() {
        return punishmentManager;
    }

    public com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.BanManager getBanManager() {
        return banManager;
    }

    public com.nexora.anticheat.license.LicenseManager getLicenseManager() {
        return licenseManager;
    }

    public CheckManager getCheckManager() {
        return checkManager;
    }

    public StaffGuiManager getStaffGuiManager() {
        return staffGuiManager;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/alert/AlertManager.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.alert;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.event.ClickEvent;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.event.HoverEvent;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Dispatches rich interactive staff alerts in-game and to external webhooks.
 */
public class AlertManager {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;
    private final DiscordWebhookSender discordSender = new DiscordWebhookSender();
    private final Map<String, Long> alertCooldowns = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public AlertManager(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void sendAlert(@NotNull Player player,
                          @NotNull String checkId,
                          @NotNull String category,
                          double vl,
                          double maxVl,
                          @NotNull String details) {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig();
        if (!config.getBoolean("alerts.enabled", true)) {
            return;
        }

        // Rate-limit alert spam per player & check
        String cooldownKey = player.getUniqueId() + ":" + checkId.toLowerCase();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long cooldownMs = config.getLong("alerts.cooldown-ms", 1000L);
        Long lastAlert = alertCooldowns.get(cooldownKey);
        if (lastAlert != null && now - lastAlert < cooldownMs) {
            return;
        }
        alertCooldowns.put(cooldownKey, now);

        int ping = player.getPing();
        double tps = Math.min(20.0, Math.round(Bukkit.getTPS()[0] * 10.0) / 10.0);

        // Build MiniMessage placeholders
        Map<String, String> placeholders = Map.of(
                "player", player.getName(),
                "check", checkId,
                "category", category,
                "vl", String.format("%.1f", vl),
                "max_vl", String.format("%.1f", maxVl),
                "details", details,
                "ping", String.valueOf(ping),
                "tps", String.valueOf(tps)
        );

        Component baseAlert = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                "alerts.format",
                "<prefix><red><player></red> <gray>failed</gray> <yellow><check></yellow> <dark_gray>(<gray>VL: <red><vl></red>/<gold><max_vl></gold></gray>)</dark_gray>",
                placeholders
        );

        Component hoverComponent = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                "alerts.hover",
                "<gray>Check: <yellow><check></yellow>\nCategory: <aqua><category></aqua>\nDetails: <white><details></white>\nPing: <green><ping>ms</green> | TPS: <green><tps></green>\n<yellow>Click to teleport to player!</yellow></gray>",
                placeholders
        );

        // Interactive hover and click-to-teleport component
        Component fullAlert = baseAlert
                .hoverEvent(HoverEvent.showText(hoverComponent))
                .clickEvent(ClickEvent.runCommand("/tp " + player.getName()));

        String permission = config.getString("alerts.permission", "nexora.alerts");

        // Broadcast to permitted online staff members
        for (Player onlineStaff : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            if (onlineStaff.hasPermission(permission)) {
                PlayerData staffData = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(onlineStaff);
                if (staffData == null || staffData.areAlertsEnabled()) {
                    onlineStaff.sendMessage(fullAlert);
                }
            }
        }

        // Console alert
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(baseAlert);

        // Discord Webhook integration (if not restricted to punishments only)
        if (config.getBoolean("alerts.discord.enabled", false)
                && !config.getBoolean("alerts.discord.only-on-punishment", true)) {
            String webhookUrl = config.getString("alerts.discord.webhook-url", "");
            if (webhookUrl != null && !webhookUrl.isBlank() && !webhookUrl.contains("YOUR_WEBHOOK_URL_HERE")) {
                int embedColor = config.getInt("alerts.discord.embed-color", 16711680);
                discordSender.sendAlertAsync(
                        plugin,
                        webhookUrl,
                        player.getName(),
                        checkId,
                        category,
                        vl,
                        maxVl,
                        details,
                        ping,
                        embedColor
                );
            }
        }
    }

    public DiscordWebhookSender getDiscordSender() {
        return discordSender;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/alert/DiscordWebhookSender.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.alert;

import com.google.gson.JsonArray;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.BanRecord;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.TaskUtil;
import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;

/**
 * Asynchronous Discord webhook dispatcher with rich embeds, player avatars, and full punishment details.
 */
public class DiscordWebhookSender {

    private final HttpClient httpClient;

    public DiscordWebhookSender() {
        this.httpClient = HttpClient.newBuilder()
                .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                .build();
    }

    /**
     * Dispatches a rich Discord punishment embed for Kicks and Bans.
     */
    public void sendPunishmentAlertAsync(@NotNull Plugin plugin,
                                         @NotNull String webhookUrl,
                                         @NotNull String playerName,
                                         @NotNull String playerUuid,
                                         @NotNull String action,
                                         @Nullable String durationText,
                                         @NotNull String checkId,
                                         @NotNull String category,
                                         double vl,
                                         @NotNull String details,
                                         int ping,
                                         double tps,
                                         @NotNull String clientBrand,
                                         boolean isCracked,
                                         @Nullable String ipAddress,
                                         boolean showIp,
                                         int embedColor) {
        TaskUtil.runAsync(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                JsonObject embed = new JsonObject();
                boolean isBan = action.equalsIgnoreCase("BAN") || action.toUpperCase().contains("BAN");
                embed.addProperty("title", isBan ? "🚨 Punishment Issued: BANNED" : "⚠️ Punishment Issued: KICKED");
                embed.addProperty("color", embedColor != 0 ? embedColor : (isBan ? 0xFF0000 : 0xFFAA00));
                embed.addProperty("timestamp", Instant.now().toString());

                // Player skin avatar thumbnail
                JsonObject thumbnail = new JsonObject();
                thumbnail.addProperty("url", "https://mc-heads.net/avatar/" + playerName + "/64");
                embed.add("thumbnail", thumbnail);

                JsonArray fields = new JsonArray();

                // Player field
                JsonObject playerField = new JsonObject();
                playerField.addProperty("name", "👤 Player");
                playerField.addProperty("value", "**" + playerName + "**\n`" + playerUuid + "`");
                playerField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(playerField);

                // Action field
                JsonObject actionField = new JsonObject();
                actionField.addProperty("name", "⚖️ Action");
                String actionVal = "**" + action.toUpperCase() + "**";
                if (durationText != null && !durationText.isBlank()) {
                    actionVal += "\n`Duration: " + durationText + "`";
                }
                actionField.addProperty("value", actionVal);
                actionField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(actionField);

                // Platform (Cracked vs Premium)
                JsonObject platformField = new JsonObject();
                platformField.addProperty("name", "🌐 Platform");
                platformField.addProperty("value", isCracked ? "🔴 **CRACKED** (Offline UUID)" : "🟢 **PREMIUM** (Mojang Auth)");
                platformField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(platformField);

                // Check & Category
                JsonObject checkField = new JsonObject();
                checkField.addProperty("name", "🛡️ Detection");
                checkField.addProperty("value", "`" + checkId + "` (" + category + ")\nVL: `" + String.format("%.1f", vl) + "`");
                checkField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(checkField);

                // Client Brand
                JsonObject brandField = new JsonObject();
                brandField.addProperty("name", "💻 Client Brand");
                brandField.addProperty("value", "`" + (clientBrand.isBlank() ? "Unknown" : clientBrand) + "`");
                brandField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(brandField);

                // Telemetry (Ping + TPS)
                JsonObject perfField = new JsonObject();
                perfField.addProperty("name", "📊 Performance");
                perfField.addProperty("value", "Ping: `" + ping + "ms`\nTPS: `" + String.format("%.1f", tps) + "`");
                perfField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(perfField);

                // IP Address (optional)
                if (showIp && ipAddress != null && !ipAddress.isBlank()) {
                    JsonObject ipField = new JsonObject();
                    ipField.addProperty("name", "🔒 IP Address");
                    ipField.addProperty("value", "||`" + ipAddress + "`||");
                    ipField.addProperty("inline", true);
                    fields.add(ipField);
                }

                // Detection Details
                JsonObject detailsField = new JsonObject();
                detailsField.addProperty("name", "📝 Details");
                detailsField.addProperty("value", "```\n" + (details.length() > 900 ? details.substring(0, 900) + "..." : details) + "\n```");
                detailsField.addProperty("inline", false);
                fields.add(detailsField);

                embed.add("fields", fields);

                JsonObject footer = new JsonObject();
                footer.addProperty("text", "Nexora AntiCheat Security Engine • Paper 26.2");
                embed.add("footer", footer);

                JsonArray embeds = new JsonArray();
                embeds.add(embed);

                JsonObject payload = new JsonObject();
                payload.addProperty("username", "Nexora Security");
                payload.addProperty("avatar_url", "https://mc-heads.net/avatar/Nexora/64");
                payload.add("embeds", embeds);

                HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                        .uri(URI.create(webhookUrl))
                        .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                        .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(payload.toString()))
                        .build();

                httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.discarding());
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Backward-compatible alert dispatcher for regular VL alerts.
     */
    public void sendAlertAsync(@NotNull Plugin plugin,
                               @NotNull String webhookUrl,
                               @NotNull String player,
                               @NotNull String check,
                               @NotNull String category,
                               double vl,
                               double maxVl,
                               @NotNull String details,
                               int ping,
                               int embedColor) {
        TaskUtil.runAsync(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                JsonObject embed = new JsonObject();
                embed.addProperty("title", "🚨 AntiCheat Flag: " + check);
                embed.addProperty("color", embedColor != 0 ? embedColor : 0xFF0000);
                embed.addProperty("timestamp", Instant.now().toString());

                JsonObject thumbnail = new JsonObject();
                thumbnail.addProperty("url", "https://mc-heads.net/avatar/" + player + "/64");
                embed.add("thumbnail", thumbnail);

                JsonArray fields = new JsonArray();

                JsonObject playerField = new JsonObject();
                playerField.addProperty("name", "Player");
                playerField.addProperty("value", player);
                playerField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(playerField);

                JsonObject checkField = new JsonObject();
                checkField.addProperty("name", "Check");
                checkField.addProperty("value", check + " (" + category + ")");
                checkField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(checkField);

                JsonObject vlField = new JsonObject();
                vlField.addProperty("name", "Violation Level");
                vlField.addProperty("value", String.format("%.1f / %.1f", vl, maxVl));
                vlField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(vlField);

                JsonObject detailsField = new JsonObject();
                detailsField.addProperty("name", "Details");
                detailsField.addProperty("value", "```\n" + details + "\n```");
                detailsField.addProperty("inline", false);
                fields.add(detailsField);

                JsonObject pingField = new JsonObject();
                pingField.addProperty("name", "Latency");
                pingField.addProperty("value", ping + "ms");
                pingField.addProperty("inline", true);
                fields.add(pingField);

                embed.add("fields", fields);

                JsonArray embeds = new JsonArray();
                embeds.add(embed);

                JsonObject payload = new JsonObject();
                payload.addProperty("username", "Nexora Security");
                payload.add("embeds", embeds);

                HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                        .uri(URI.create(webhookUrl))
                        .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                        .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(payload.toString()))
                        .build();

                httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.discarding());
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
            }
        });
    }
}
```
