`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/api/NexoraAPI.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.api;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Public developer API for Nexora AntiCheat.
 */
public final class NexoraAPI {

    private static NexoraPlugin plugin;

    private NexoraAPI() {
        // Singleton API access
    }

    /**
     * Internal initialization by plugin lifecycle.
     */
    public static void init(@NotNull NexoraPlugin instance) {
        plugin = instance;
    }

    /**
     * Gets the current violation level of a player for a specific check.
     *
     * @param player  The player to inspect.
     * @param checkId The ID of the check.
     * @return The current violation level, or 0.0 if not tracked.
     */
    public static double getViolationLevel(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull String checkId) {
        if (plugin == null) return 0.0;
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        return data != null ? data.getViolationLevel(checkId) : 0.0;
    }

    /**
     * Gets the sum of all active violation levels across all checks for a player.
     *
     * @param player Target player.
     * @return Total violation level.
     */
    public static double getTotalViolationLevel(@NotNull Player player) {
        if (plugin == null) return 0.0;
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        return data != null ? data.getTotalViolationLevel() : 0.0;
    }

    /**
     * Gets an unmodifiable map of all violation levels for a player.
     *
     * @param player Target player.
     * @return Map of check ID to VL.
     */
    @NotNull
    public static Map<String, Double> getAllViolations(@NotNull Player player) {
        if (plugin == null) return Collections.emptyMap();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        return data != null ? data.getViolations() : Collections.emptyMap();
    }

    /**
     * Checks if a player is currently exempted from detections.
     *
     * @param player Target player.
     * @return True if exempt.
     */
    public static boolean isExempt(@NotNull Player player) {
        if (plugin == null) return false;
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        return data != null && data.isExempt();
    }

    /**
     * Temporarily exempts a player from all anti-cheat detections.
     *
     * @param player     Target player.
     * @param durationMs Duration of exemption in milliseconds.
     */
    public static void setExempt(@NotNull Player player, long durationMs) {
        if (plugin == null) return;
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data != null) {
            data.setExemption(durationMs);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Clears any active temporary exemption on a player.
     *
     * @param player Target player.
     */
    public static void removeExemption(@NotNull Player player) {
        if (plugin == null) return;
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data != null) {
            data.clearExemption();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Clears all active violation levels for a player.
     *
     * @param player Target player.
     */
    public static void resetViolations(@NotNull Player player) {
        if (plugin == null) return;
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data != null) {
            data.resetViolations();
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/api/NexoraFlagEvent.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.api;

import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.Cancellable;
import org.bukkit.event.Event;
import org.bukkit.event.HandlerList;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Event called whenever an anti-cheat check flags a player for suspicious behavior.
 * This event is cancellable. Cancelling the event prevents the violation level from increasing
 * and suppresses alerts and setbacks.
 */
public class NexoraFlagEvent extends Event implements Cancellable {

    private static final HandlerList HANDLERS = new HandlerList();

    private final Player player;
    private final String checkId;
    private final String checkCategory;
    private final double addedVl;
    private final double totalVl;
    private final String details;
    private boolean cancelled;

    public NexoraFlagEvent(@NotNull Player player,
                           @NotNull String checkId,
                           @NotNull String checkCategory,
                           double addedVl,
                           double totalVl,
                           @NotNull String details) {
        super(!org.bukkit.Bukkit.isPrimaryThread());
        this.player = player;
        this.checkId = checkId;
        this.checkCategory = checkCategory;
        this.addedVl = addedVl;
        this.totalVl = totalVl;
        this.details = details;
        this.cancelled = false;
    }

    @NotNull
    public Player getPlayer() {
        return player;
    }

    @NotNull
    public String getCheckId() {
        return checkId;
    }

    @NotNull
    public String getCheckCategory() {
        return checkCategory;
    }

    public double getAddedVl() {
        return addedVl;
    }

    public double getTotalVl() {
        return totalVl;
    }

    @NotNull
    public String getDetails() {
        return details;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isCancelled() {
        return cancelled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setCancelled(boolean cancel) {
        this.cancelled = cancel;
    }

    @NotNull
    @Override
    public HandlerList getHandlers() {
        return HANDLERS;
    }

    @NotNull
    public static HandlerList getHandlerList() {
        return HANDLERS;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/api/NexoraPunishEvent.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.api;

import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.Cancellable;
import org.bukkit.event.Event;
import org.bukkit.event.HandlerList;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Event called when a player exceeds the maximum violation level for a check
 * and a punishment (e.g. kick, ban, custom command) is queued.
 * Cancelling this event prevents the punishment from executing.
 */
public class NexoraPunishEvent extends Event implements Cancellable {

    private static final HandlerList HANDLERS = new HandlerList();

    private final Player player;
    private final String checkId;
    private final String punishmentType;
    private final String reason;
    private boolean cancelled;

    public NexoraPunishEvent(@NotNull Player player,
                             @NotNull String checkId,
                             @NotNull String punishmentType,
                             @NotNull String reason) {
        super(!org.bukkit.Bukkit.isPrimaryThread());
        this.player = player;
        this.checkId = checkId;
        this.punishmentType = punishmentType;
        this.reason = reason;
        this.cancelled = false;
    }

    @NotNull
    public Player getPlayer() {
        return player;
    }

    @NotNull
    public String getCheckId() {
        return checkId;
    }

    @NotNull
    public String getPunishmentType() {
        return punishmentType;
    }

    @NotNull
    public String getReason() {
        return reason;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isCancelled() {
        return cancelled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setCancelled(boolean cancel) {
        this.cancelled = cancel;
    }

    @NotNull
    @Override
    public HandlerList getHandlers() {
        return HANDLERS;
    }

    @NotNull
    public static HandlerList getHandlerList() {
        return HANDLERS;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/Check.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.api.NexoraFlagEvent;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.PunishmentType;
import com.nexora.anticheat.storage.ViolationRecord;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.BedrockUtil;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Base abstract foundation for all anti-cheat detection modules.
 */
public abstract class Check implements Listener {

    protected final NexoraPlugin plugin;
    private final String id;
    private final String name;
    private final CheckCategory category;

    private boolean enabled = true;
    private double maxVl = 10.0;
    private double vlDecay = 1.0;
    private boolean setback = false;
    private PunishmentType punishment = PunishmentType.KICK;
    private boolean debug = false;

    public Check(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin,
                 @NotNull String id,
                 @NotNull String name,
                 @NotNull CheckCategory category) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.id = id;
        this.name = name;
        this.category = category;
    }

    public void reloadConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config) {
        String path = category.name().toLowerCase() + "." + id.toLowerCase();
        this.enabled = config.getBoolean(path + ".enabled", true);
        this.maxVl = config.getDouble(path + ".max-vl", 10.0);
        this.vlDecay = config.getDouble(path + ".vl-decay", 1.0);
        this.setback = config.getBoolean(path + ".setback", false);
        this.punishment = PunishmentType.fromString(config.getString(path + ".punishment", "KICK"), PunishmentType.KICK);
        loadCustomConfig(config, path);
    }

    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        // Overridden by specific check implementations
    }

    /**
     * Determines whether checks should be skipped due to lag, network spikes, or immunity.
     */
    public boolean shouldSkip(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull PlayerData data) {
        if (!enabled) {
            return true;
        }

        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) {
            return true;
        }

        FileConfiguration mainConfig = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig();
        if (mainConfig.getBoolean("settings.ops-bypass", true) && player.isOp()) {
            return true;
        }

        if (player.hasPermission("nexora.bypass") || player.hasPermission("nexora.bypass." + id.toLowerCase())) {
            return true;
        }

        if (data.isExempt()) {
            return true;
        }

        // Bedrock / GeyserMC player — skip checks that are incompatible with Bedrock physics
        if (BedrockUtil.isBedrock(player)) {
            java.util.List<String> bedrockSkip = mainConfig.getStringList("settings.bedrock.skip-checks");
            if (bedrockSkip.stream().anyMatch(s -> s.equalsIgnoreCase(id))) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        if (mainConfig.getBoolean("settings.lag-compensation.enabled", true)) {
            // Server TPS Lag Compensation
            double minTps = mainConfig.getDouble("settings.lag-compensation.min-tps", 18.5);
            if (Bukkit.getTPS()[0] < minTps) {
                return true;
            }

            // Client Ping Network Spike Compensation
            int maxPing = mainConfig.getInt("settings.lag-compensation.max-ping-ms", 350);
            if (player.getPing() > maxPing) {
                return true;
            }

            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

            // Teleport Immunity
            long teleportImmunityMs = mainConfig.getLong("settings.lag-compensation.teleport-immunity-ticks", 20L) * 50L;
            if (now - data.getLastTeleportTimestamp() < teleportImmunityMs) {
                return true;
            }

            // Respawn Immunity
            long respawnImmunityMs = mainConfig.getLong("settings.lag-compensation.respawn-immunity-ticks", 40L) * 50L;
            if (now - data.getLastRespawnTimestamp() < respawnImmunityMs) {
                return true;
            }

            // Join Immunity
            long joinImmunityMs = mainConfig.getLong("settings.lag-compensation.join-immunity-ticks", 60L) * 50L;
            if (now - data.getJoinTimestamp() < joinImmunityMs) {
                return true;
            }

            // Knockback / Velocity Immunity
            long velocityImmunityMs = mainConfig.getLong("settings.lag-compensation.velocity-immunity-ticks", 30L) * 50L;
            if (now - data.getLastVelocityTimestamp() < velocityImmunityMs) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Flags a player for an anti-cheat violation.
     */
    public void flag(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull PlayerData data, @NotNull String details, double addedVl) {
        double currentVl = data.getViolationLevel(id);
        double newTotalVl = currentVl + addedVl;

        NexoraFlagEvent flagEvent = new NexoraFlagEvent(player, id, category.getDisplayName(), addedVl, newTotalVl, details);
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().callEvent(flagEvent);

        if (flagEvent.isCancelled()) {
            return;
        }

        double resultingVl = data.addViolationLevel(id, addedVl);

        // Send staff alerts
        plugin.getAlertManager().sendAlert(player, id, category.getDisplayName(), resultingVl, maxVl, details);

        // Asynchronously log violation
        plugin.getStorageManager().logViolationAsync(new ViolationRecord(
                player.getUniqueId(),
                player.getName(),
                id,
                resultingVl,
                details,
                player.getPing(),
                System.currentTimeMillis()
        ));

        // Setback if enabled
        if (setback) {
            handleSetback(player, data);
        }

        // Execute punishment if threshold breached
        if (resultingVl >= maxVl) {
            plugin.getPunishmentManager().executePunishment(player, data, id, punishment, resultingVl);
        }
    }

    protected void handleSetback(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull PlayerData data) {
        plugin.getPunishmentManager().setback(player, data);
    }

    public String getId() {
        return id;
    }

    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    public CheckCategory getCategory() {
        return category;
    }

    public boolean isEnabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    public double getMaxVl() {
        return maxVl;
    }

    public double getVlDecay() {
        return vlDecay;
    }

    public boolean isSetback() {
        return setback;
    }

    public PunishmentType getPunishment() {
        return punishment;
    }

    public boolean isDebug() {
        return debug;
    }

    public void setDebug(boolean debug) {
        this.debug = debug;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/CheckManager.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.combat.*;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.movement.*;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.player.*;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.world.*;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.TaskUtil;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Registry and orchestrator for all anti-cheat detection modules and violation decays.
 */
public class CheckManager {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;
    private final Map<String, Check> checkMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public CheckManager(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void registerAll() {
        // Movement Checks
        register(new SpeedCheck(plugin));
        register(new FlyCheck(plugin));
        register(new NoFallCheck(plugin));
        register(new JesusCheck(plugin));
        register(new StepCheck(plugin));
        register(new PhaseCheck(plugin));
        register(new TimerCheck(plugin));
        register(new InvalidMovementCheck(plugin));

        // Combat Checks
        register(new KillAuraCheck(plugin));
        register(new ReachCheck(plugin));
        register(new AutoClickerCheck(plugin));
        register(new VelocityCheck(plugin));
        register(new HitBoxCheck(plugin));

        // World Checks
        register(new FastBreakCheck(plugin));
        register(new NukerCheck(plugin));
        register(new ScaffoldCheck(plugin));
        register(new FastPlaceCheck(plugin));
        register(new TowerCheck(plugin));
        register(new GhostHandCheck(plugin));
        register(new XRayCheck(plugin));
        register(new AntiDupeCheck(plugin));

        // Player / Packet Checks
        register(new BadPacketsCheck(plugin));
        register(new InventoryCheck(plugin));
        register(new FastEatCheck(plugin));
        register(new ExploitCheck(plugin));
        register(new ClientBrandCheck(plugin));

        // Register check listeners with Bukkit
        for (Check check : checkMap.values()) {
            Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(check, plugin);
        }

        // Load configuration values into checks
        reload();

        // Start async repeating VL decay task
        startDecayTask();
    }

    private void register(@NotNull Check check) {
        checkMap.put(check.getId().toLowerCase(), check);
    }

    public void reload() {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfigManager().getChecksConfig();
        for (Check check : checkMap.values()) {
            check.reloadConfig(config);
        }
    }

    private void startDecayTask() {
        long intervalSeconds = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig().getLong("settings.vl-decay.interval-seconds", 15L);
        double decayAmount = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig().getDouble("settings.vl-decay.decay-amount", 1.0);

        TaskUtil.runAsyncTimer(plugin, () -> {
            for (PlayerData data : plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getAllData()) {
                data.decayViolationLevels(decayAmount);
            }
        }, intervalSeconds * 1000L, intervalSeconds * 1000L);
    }

    @Nullable
    public Check getCheck(@NotNull String id) {
        return checkMap.get(id.toLowerCase());
    }

    @NotNull
    public Collection<Check> getAllChecks() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableCollection(checkMap.values());
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/combat/AutoClickerCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.combat;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects AutoClicker modifications via CPS thresholds and statistical interval variance.
 */
public class AutoClickerCheck extends Check {

    private double maxCps = 20.0;
    private double minVariance = 4.5;

    public AutoClickerCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "AutoClicker", "AutoClicker (CPS / Variance)", CheckCategory.COMBAT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxCps = config.getDouble(path + ".max-cps", 20.0);
        this.minVariance = config.getDouble(path + ".min-variance", 4.5);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        if (event.getAction() != Action.LEFT_CLICK_AIR && event.getAction() != Action.LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK) {
            return;
        }

        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        data.registerLeftClick();

        double cps = data.getCps();
        if (cps > maxCps) {
            flag(player, data, String.format("High CPS: %.1f > Max: %.1f", cps, maxCps), 1.0);
            return;
        }

        // Check for unnatural macro consistency (zero variance)
        if (cps >= 12.0) {
            double variance = data.getClickIntervalVariance();
            if (variance < minVariance && variance > 0.0) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Robotic click consistency: variance=%.2f < min=%.2f (CPS: %.1f)",
                        variance, minVariance, cps), 1.0);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/combat/HitBoxCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.combat;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.RayTraceResult;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects HitBox expansion cheats where players hit entities outside their legitimate bounding box.
 */
public class HitBoxCheck extends Check {

    private double expansionThreshold = 0.25;

    public HitBoxCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "HitBox", "HitBox (Bounding Box Expansion)", CheckCategory.COMBAT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.expansionThreshold = config.getDouble(path + ".expansion-threshold", 0.25);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player player) || !(event.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity target)) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        Location eyeLoc = player.getEyeLocation();
        Vector eyePos = eyeLoc.toVector();
        Vector direction = eyeLoc.getDirection().normalize();

        BoundingBox box = target.getBoundingBox();

        // Check if ray intersects the standard bounding box
        RayTraceResult hit = box.rayTrace(eyePos, direction, 6.0);
        if (hit == null) {
            // Ray missed the legitimate bounding box; test with expansion threshold
            BoundingBox expandedBox = box.clone().expand(expansionThreshold);
            RayTraceResult expandedHit = expandedBox.rayTrace(eyePos, direction, 6.0);

            if (expandedHit == null) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Attacked outside entity bounding box (expanded by +%.2f)", expansionThreshold), 1.0);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/combat/KillAuraCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.combat;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.MathUtil;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.RayTraceUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Detects KillAura through rotation snapping, impossible attack angles, and multi-target switching.
 */
public class KillAuraCheck extends Check {

    private double maxYawSpeed = 120.0;
    private long multiTargetWindowMs = 100L;

    public KillAuraCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "KillAura", "KillAura (Angle / MultiTarget)", CheckCategory.COMBAT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxYawSpeed = config.getDouble(path + ".max-yaw-speed", 120.0);
        this.multiTargetWindowMs = config.getLong(path + ".multi-target-window-ms", 100L);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player player) || !(event.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity target)) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        Location eyeLoc = player.getEyeLocation();
        Location targetLoc = target.getLocation().add(0, target.getHeight() / 2.0, 0);

        // 1. Check line-of-sight angle offset
        double angleOffset = RayTraceUtil.getAngleOffset(eyeLoc, targetLoc);
        if (angleOffset > 75.0) {
            flag(player, data, String.format("Attacked outside field of view: angle=%.1f° > 75°", angleOffset), 1.0);
            return;
        }

        // 2. Check multi-target aura
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        UUID lastTarget = data.getLastTargetId();
        if (lastTarget != null && !lastTarget.equals(target.getUniqueId())) {
            long diff = now - data.getLastAttackTimestamp();
            if (diff < multiTargetWindowMs) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Rapid target switch within %dms to different entity", diff), 1.5);
            }
        }

        // Register attack
        data.registerAttack(target.getUniqueId());
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/combat/ReachCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.combat;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.BedrockUtil;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.RayTraceUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerItemHeldEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;
import org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Detects reach hacks where players hit targets from beyond valid bounding-box distances.
 *
 * <p><b>Trident / Spear-swap fix (v1.1)</b>: The Trident's Riptide enchantment causes the
 * player to physically lunge, reaching up to ~5–6 blocks on the launch tick. Additionally,
 * players can "spear swap" by switching to a trident mid-air after a riptide lunge to
 * extend their effective melee range for a short window. We now:
 * <ul>
 *   <li>Track item-hold changes to detect when a player just equipped a Trident.</li>
 *   <li>Grant an extended reach window ({@code tridentExtendedReach}) for
 *       {@code tridentWindowMs} milliseconds after switching to a Trident or while riptiding.</li>
 *   <li>Apply Bedrock-specific reach tolerance (Bedrock client hit detection differs).</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public class ReachCheck extends Check {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ config
    private double maxReach            = 3.15;
    private double creativeReach       = 5.2;
    /** Extended reach allowed when the player is holding a Trident or riptiding. */
    private double tridentExtendedReach = 6.2;
    /** Time window (ms) after equipping a Trident where extended reach is allowed. */
    private long   tridentWindowMs     = 800L;
    /** Extra reach tolerance for Bedrock players (blocks). */
    private double bedrockTolerance    = 0.50;

    /** Tracks the last time each player switched to holding a Trident (ms timestamp). */
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastTridentEquip = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public ReachCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Reach", "Reach (Combat Distance)", CheckCategory.COMBAT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxReach             = config.getDouble(path + ".max-reach",              3.15);
        this.creativeReach        = config.getDouble(path + ".creative-reach",         5.2);
        this.tridentExtendedReach = config.getDouble(path + ".trident-extended-reach", 6.2);
        this.tridentWindowMs      = config.getLong(path + ".trident-window-ms",        800L);
        this.bedrockTolerance     = config.getDouble(path + ".bedrock-tolerance",      0.50);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Track Trident equip
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onItemHeld(PlayerItemHeldEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        ItemStack newItem = player.getInventory().getItem(event.getNewSlot());
        if (newItem != null && newItem.getType() == Material.TRIDENT) {
            lastTridentEquip.put(player.getUniqueId(), System.currentTimeMillis());
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Reach detection
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player player)) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) return;

        Entity target   = event.getEntity();
        double distance = RayTraceUtil.getDistanceToBox(player.getEyeLocation(), target);

        // Ping compensation allowance (≈ 0.001 blocks per ms of ping, capped at 0.30)
        double pingAllowance = Math.min(0.30, player.getPing() * 0.001);

        double threshold;
        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) {
            threshold = creativeReach + pingAllowance;
        } else if (isTridentWindow(player)) {
            // Player is riptiding OR just swapped to trident — apply extended reach
            threshold = tridentExtendedReach + pingAllowance;
        } else {
            threshold = maxReach + pingAllowance;
        }

        // Bedrock clients use slightly different hit-boxes / lag
        if (BedrockUtil.isBedrock(player)) {
            threshold += bedrockTolerance;
        }

        if (distance > threshold) {
            double excess = distance - threshold;
            flag(player, data,
                 String.format("Reach: %.2f > Max: %.2f (Ping: %dms, Excess: +%.2f, Trident: %b)",
                               distance, threshold, player.getPing(), excess, isTridentWindow(player)),
                 1.0);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Helpers
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private boolean isTridentWindow(@NotNull Player player) {
        // Active riptide lunge
        if (player.isRiptiding()) return true;

        // Recently switched to trident hand-slot
        Long equipTime = lastTridentEquip.get(player.getUniqueId());
        if (equipTime != null && System.currentTimeMillis() - equipTime < tridentWindowMs) {
            // Make sure they actually still hold a Trident (didn't switch away)
            ItemStack held = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();
            if (held.getType() == Material.TRIDENT) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/combat/VelocityCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.combat;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.LocationUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects Anti-Knockback (Velocity modification / 0% knockback).
 */
public class VelocityCheck extends Check {

    private double verticalThreshold = 0.85;

    public VelocityCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Velocity", "Velocity (Anti-Knockback)", CheckCategory.COMBAT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.verticalThreshold = config.getDouble(path + ".vertical-threshold", 0.85);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        if (!data.isVelocityPending()) {
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        double deltaY = to.getY() - from.getY();
        double expectedY = data.getExpectedVelocityY();

        // If player has blocks directly above head, skip
        if (to.clone().add(0, 2.0, 0).getBlock().getType().isSolid()) {
            data.setVelocityPending(false);
            return;
        }

        if (LocationUtil.isClimbable(to) || LocationUtil.isInOrNearLiquid(to) || LocationUtil.isNearSlimeOrHoney(to)) {
            data.setVelocityPending(false);
            return;
        }

        // If server sent substantial vertical velocity, but client took zero vertical movement
        if (expectedY > 0.25 && deltaY <= 0.005) {
            flag(player, data, String.format("Anti-KB detected: expectedDeltaY=%.2f, actualDeltaY=%.4f", expectedY, deltaY), 1.0);
        }

        data.setVelocityPending(false);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/movement/FlyCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.movement;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.LocationUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Detects vertical fly hacks, air hovering, and illegal gravity suspension.
 */
public class FlyCheck extends Check {

    private int maxHoverTicks = 4;
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> airTicksMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> hoverTicksMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public FlyCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Fly", "Fly / Hovering", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxHoverTicks = config.getInt(path + ".max-hover-ticks", 4);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        if (player.isGliding() || player.isFlying() || player.isInsideVehicle() || player.isRiptiding()) {
            airTicksMap.remove(player.getUniqueId());
            hoverTicksMap.remove(player.getUniqueId());
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();

        // If player is in liquid, climbable, slime, or near ground -> reset counters
        if (player.isOnGround() || LocationUtil.isNearGround(to) || LocationUtil.isInOrNearLiquid(to)
                || LocationUtil.isClimbable(to) || LocationUtil.isNearSlimeOrHoney(to)) {
            airTicksMap.remove(player.getUniqueId());
            hoverTicksMap.remove(player.getUniqueId());
            return;
        }

        double deltaY = to.getY() - from.getY();
        int airTicks = airTicksMap.compute(player.getUniqueId(), (k, v) -> v == null ? 1 : v + 1);

        // Detect air hovering (constant Y coordinate in air)
        if (Math.abs(deltaY) < 0.005) {
            int hoverTicks = hoverTicksMap.compute(player.getUniqueId(), (k, v) -> v == null ? 1 : v + 1);
            if (hoverTicks > maxHoverTicks) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Hovering in air for %d ticks (deltaY: %.4f)", hoverTicks, deltaY), 1.0);
            }
        } else {
            hoverTicksMap.remove(player.getUniqueId());
        }

        // Detect ascending in air without jump velocity
        if (deltaY > 0.0 && airTicks > 10) {
            double maxJumpHeight = 0.42;
            PotionEffect jump = player.getPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.JUMP_BOOST);
            if (jump != null) {
                maxJumpHeight += (jump.getAmplifier() + 1) * 0.1;
            }

            if (deltaY > maxJumpHeight && !data.isVelocityPending()) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Illegal air ascension: deltaY=%.3f, airTicks=%d", deltaY, airTicks), 1.0);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/movement/InvalidMovementCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.movement;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects malformed packets, invalid pitch, NaN coordinates, or impossible coordinate jumps.
 */
public class InvalidMovementCheck extends Check {

    public InvalidMovementCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "InvalidMovement", "Invalid Movement / Derp", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }

        Location to = event.getTo();

        // Check for NaN or Infinite coordinates
        if (Double.isNaN(to.getX()) || Double.isNaN(to.getY()) || Double.isNaN(to.getZ())
                || Double.isInfinite(to.getX()) || Double.isInfinite(to.getY()) || Double.isInfinite(to.getZ())) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, data, "NaN or Infinite coordinates detected in packet", 5.0);
            return;
        }

        // Vanilla pitch boundaries are [-90.0, 90.0]
        float pitch = to.getPitch();
        if (Math.abs(pitch) > 90.0F) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, data, String.format("Illegal pitch angle: %.2f (Allowed: [-90, 90])", pitch), 2.0);
            return;
        }

        // Extreme instant displacement (greater than 100 blocks in a single tick without teleport)
        Location from = event.getFrom();
        double distSq = from.distanceSquared(to);
        if (distSq > 10000.0) {
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            if (now - data.getLastTeleportTimestamp() > 1000L) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                flag(player, data, String.format("Impossible coordinate jump: %.1f blocks without teleport", Math.sqrt(distSq)), 3.0);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/movement/JesusCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.movement;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.LocationUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Detects Jesus / WaterWalk hacks where players walk directly on water or lava surfaces.
 */
public class JesusCheck extends Check {

    private int maxLiquidTicks = 3;
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> liquidTicks = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public JesusCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Jesus", "Jesus / WaterWalk", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxLiquidTicks = config.getInt(path + ".max-liquid-ticks", 3);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        if (player.isGliding() || player.isFlying() || player.isInsideVehicle() || player.isRiptiding()) {
            liquidTicks.remove(player.getUniqueId());
            return;
        }

        // Frost Walker boots check
        ItemStack boots = player.getInventory().getBoots();
        if (boots != null && boots.containsEnchantment(Enchantment.FROST_WALKER)) {
            liquidTicks.remove(player.getUniqueId());
            return;
        }

        Location to = event.getTo();
        Block footBlock = to.getBlock();
        Block belowBlock = to.clone().subtract(0, 0.4, 0).getBlock();

        boolean onLiquid = (belowBlock.isLiquid() || footBlock.isLiquid()) && !LocationUtil.isNearGround(to);

        if (onLiquid && player.isOnGround()) {
            int ticks = liquidTicks.compute(player.getUniqueId(), (k, v) -> v == null ? 1 : v + 1);
            if (ticks > maxLiquidTicks) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Walking on liquid surface for %d ticks", ticks), 1.0);
            }
        } else {
            liquidTicks.remove(player.getUniqueId());
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/movement/NoFallCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.movement;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.LocationUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects clients spoofing onGround packet state to avoid fall damage.
 */
public class NoFallCheck extends Check {

    private double minAirDistance = 0.65;

    public NoFallCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "NoFall", "NoFall (Spoofed Ground)", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.minAirDistance = config.getDouble(path + ".min-air-distance", 0.65);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        if (player.isGliding() || player.isFlying() || player.isInsideVehicle()) {
            return;
        }

        Location to = event.getTo();

        // Check if player packet claims onGround == true
        if (player.isOnGround()) {
            // Verify if block below is actually ground or liquid
            if (!LocationUtil.isNearGround(to) && !LocationUtil.isInOrNearLiquid(to) && !LocationUtil.isClimbable(to)) {
                double groundDist = to.getY() - Math.floor(to.getY());
                if (groundDist > minAirDistance) {
                    flag(player, data, String.format("Client claimed onGround but distance to ground is %.3f", groundDist), 1.0);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/movement/PhaseCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.movement;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects phase, noclip, and door/wall glitching through occluding solid blocks.
 */
public class PhaseCheck extends Check {

    public PhaseCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Phase", "Phase / NoClip", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        if (player.isGliding() || player.isInsideVehicle()) {
            return;
        }

        Location to = event.getTo();
        Block headBlock = to.clone().add(0, 1.2, 0).getBlock();
        Block torsoBlock = to.clone().add(0, 0.6, 0).getBlock();

        // Check if player center is inside a solid full block
        if (isPhaseableSolid(headBlock) && isPhaseableSolid(torsoBlock)) {
            flag(player, data, String.format("Phasing inside solid block: %s at %d,%d,%d",
                    torsoBlock.getType(), torsoBlock.getX(), torsoBlock.getY(), torsoBlock.getZ()), 1.0);
        }
    }

    private boolean isPhaseableSolid(Block block) {
        Material type = block.getType();
        return type.isSolid() && type.isOccluding()
                && !type.name().contains("DOOR")
                && !type.name().contains("FENCE")
                && !type.name().contains("GATE")
                && !type.name().contains("STAIRS")
                && !type.name().contains("SLAB")
                && !type.name().contains("TRAPDOOR")
                && !type.name().contains("BED")
                && !type.name().contains("PISTON");
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/movement/SpeedCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.movement;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.BedrockUtil;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.LocationUtil;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects abnormal horizontal movement speed (Speed Hack, BunnyHop).
 *
 * <p><b>Jump false-positive fix (v1.1)</b>: Minecraft sprint-jumping produces a
 * predictable horizontal impulse (~0.12 extra blocks per tick on the jump tick itself
 * that rapidly decays with air friction). Instead of using a flat air multiplier, we
 * now track the jump state and grant a generous burst allowance only on the single
 * take-off tick, then taper it on subsequent air ticks. This eliminates all
 * false-positives from normal sprint-jumping while still catching Speed exploits.
 *
 * <p><b>Bedrock support</b>: Bedrock clients handle physics slightly differently;
 * they receive an additional {@code bedrockMultiplier} tolerance factor.
 */
public class SpeedCheck extends Check {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ config
    private double baseSpeed       = 0.36;
    private double iceMultiplier   = 1.6;
    /** Allowance multiplier applied for the first air tick after a jump. */
    private double jumpBoostFactor = 1.50;
    /** Allowance multiplier for subsequent air ticks (sprint-jump arc). */
    private double airMultiplier   = 1.18;
    /** Extra lenience for Bedrock clients. */
    private double bedrockMultiplier = 1.20;

    public SpeedCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Speed", "Speed (Horizontal)", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.baseSpeed        = config.getDouble(path + ".base-speed",        0.36);
        this.iceMultiplier    = config.getDouble(path + ".ice-multiplier",    1.6);
        this.jumpBoostFactor  = config.getDouble(path + ".jump-boost-factor", 1.50);
        this.airMultiplier    = config.getDouble(path + ".air-multiplier",    1.18);
        this.bedrockMultiplier= config.getDouble(path + ".bedrock-multiplier",1.20);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) return;

        // Exempt: gliding, riptide, vehicle, creative fly
        if (player.isGliding() || player.isRiptiding()
                || player.isInsideVehicle() || player.isFlying()) {
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to   = event.getTo();
        double distance = MathUtil.distanceHorizontal(from.getX(), from.getZ(), to.getX(), to.getZ());

        if (distance < 0.001) return; // pure rotation

        // ----------------------------------------------------------------
        // Determine the maximum allowed speed this tick
        // ----------------------------------------------------------------
        double maxAllowed = baseSpeed;

        // Ice surface bonus
        if (LocationUtil.isNearIce(from) || LocationUtil.isNearIce(to)) {
            maxAllowed *= iceMultiplier;
        }

        boolean onGround = player.isOnGround();
        boolean wasOnGround = data.wasLastTickOnGround();

        if (!onGround) {
            // Detect the *first* air tick after leaving the ground (jump launch tick)
            if (wasOnGround) {
                // Take-off tick — allow the jump burst
                maxAllowed *= jumpBoostFactor;
            } else {
                // Subsequent air ticks — friction causes speed to decay; use moderate multiplier
                maxAllowed *= airMultiplier;
            }
        }
        // Update ground state for next tick
        data.setLastTickOnGround(onGround);

        // Speed potion compensation
        PotionEffect speedEffect = player.getPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.SPEED);
        if (speedEffect != null) {
            maxAllowed += (speedEffect.getAmplifier() + 1) * 0.08;
        }

        // Jump boost potion — increases jump height AND sprint speed
        PotionEffect jumpEffect = player.getPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.JUMP_BOOST);
        if (jumpEffect != null && !onGround) {
            maxAllowed += (jumpEffect.getAmplifier() + 1) * 0.04;
        }

        // Server-sent velocity (knockback, etc.)
        if (data.isVelocityPending()) {
            maxAllowed += data.getExpectedHorizontalVelocity();
            data.setVelocityPending(false);
        }

        // Bedrock clients get extra tolerance
        if (BedrockUtil.isBedrock(player)) {
            maxAllowed *= bedrockMultiplier;
        }

        if (distance > maxAllowed) {
            double excess = distance - maxAllowed;
            flag(player, data,
                 String.format("Speed: %.3f > Max: %.3f (Excess: +%.3f, Ground: %b)",
                               distance, maxAllowed, excess, onGround),
                 1.0);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/movement/StepCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.movement;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects illegal step height modifications (e.g. stepping up full blocks instantaneously).
 */
public class StepCheck extends Check {

    private double maxStepHeight = 0.62;

    public StepCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Step", "Step (Illegal Step Height)", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxStepHeight = config.getDouble(path + ".max-step-height", 0.62);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        if (player.isGliding() || player.isFlying() || player.isInsideVehicle() || player.isRiptiding()) {
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();

        double deltaY = to.getY() - from.getY();

        // Normal vanilla step height is 0.6 (slabs/stairs/path blocks)
        // If client gains > maxStepHeight in a single ground tick without jump velocity or pending server velocity
        if (deltaY > maxStepHeight && player.isOnGround() && !data.isVelocityPending()) {
            flag(player, data, String.format("Illegal step height: deltaY=%.3f > maxStep=%.3f", deltaY, maxStepHeight), 1.0);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/movement/TimerCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.movement;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Detects Timer hacks by analyzing client movement packet frequency and clock drift.
 */
public class TimerCheck extends Check {

    private int maxPacketsPerSecond = 24;
    private final Map<UUID, Long> windowStartMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> packetCountMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public TimerCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Timer", "Timer (Game Clock Modification)", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxPacketsPerSecond = config.getInt(path + ".max-packets-per-second", 24);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        long windowStart = windowStartMap.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> now);
        int count = packetCountMap.compute(uuid, (k, v) -> v == null ? 1 : v + 1);

        if (now - windowStart >= 1000L) {
            if (count > maxPacketsPerSecond) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Packets: %d/s > Max: %d/s", count, maxPacketsPerSecond), 1.0);
            }
            windowStartMap.put(uuid, now);
            packetCountMap.put(uuid, 0);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/player/BadPacketsCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.player;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerItemHeldEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects protocol level abnormalities, out-of-bound hotbar slots, and invalid packet sequences.
 */
public class BadPacketsCheck extends Check {

    public BadPacketsCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "BadPackets", "BadPackets (Protocol / Slot Bounds)", CheckCategory.PLAYER);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onItemHeld(PlayerItemHeldEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }

        int newSlot = event.getNewSlot();
        int previousSlot = event.getPreviousSlot();

        // Valid vanilla hotbar slots are strictly 0 through 8
        if (newSlot < 0 || newSlot > 8 || previousSlot < 0 || previousSlot > 8) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, data, String.format("Illegal hotbar slot index: new=%d, prev=%d", newSlot, previousSlot), 2.0);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        // Check if player is sprinting while sneaking (mutually exclusive in vanilla packet rules)
        if (player.isSprinting() && player.isSneaking()) {
            flag(player, data, "Simultaneous sprint and sneak packet state detected", 1.0);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/player/ClientBrandCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.player;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.TaskUtil;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerJoinEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Detects blacklisted client brands (e.g. Meteor, Wurst, LiquidBounce, Aristois).
 * Flags the client and routes kicks through the progressive ban escalation system.
 */
public class ClientBrandCheck extends Check {

    private List<String> bannedBrands = List.of("meteor", "wurst", "liquidbounce", "impact", "aristois", "ares");

    public ClientBrandCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "ClientBrand", "ClientBrand (Blacklisted Client)", CheckCategory.PLAYER);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        List<String> list = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig().getStringList("settings.banned-clients.brands");
        if (list != null && !list.isEmpty()) {
            this.bannedBrands = list;
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        // Schedule check 10 ticks after join to allow brand handshake packet to register
        TaskUtil.runEntityLater(plugin, player, () -> {
            if (!player.isOnline()) {
                return;
            }

            String brand = player.getClientBrandName();
            if (brand != null && !brand.isBlank()) {
                data.setClientBrand(brand);
                inspectBrand(player, data, brand);
            }
        }, 10L);
    }

    public void inspectBrand(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull PlayerData data, @NotNull String brand) {
        if (!isEnabled() || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        String lowerBrand = brand.toLowerCase();
        for (String banned : bannedBrands) {
            if (lowerBrand.contains(banned.toLowerCase())) {
                flag(player, data, "Unauthorized client brand detected: '" + brand + "' matches blacklist rule '" + banned + "'", getMaxVl());
                break;
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/player/ExploitCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.player;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.serializer.plain.PlainTextComponentSerializer;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.SignChangeEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerEditBookEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.meta.BookMeta;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Detects server crash exploits, oversized book NBT payloads, and sign overflow packets.
 */
public class ExploitCheck extends Check {

    private int maxBookPages = 50;
    private int maxSignLength = 120;

    public ExploitCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Exploit", "Exploit (Book / Sign / Crash Packets)", CheckCategory.PLAYER);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxBookPages = config.getInt(path + ".max-book-pages", 50);
        this.maxSignLength = config.getInt(path + ".max-sign-length", 120);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onBookEdit(PlayerEditBookEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }

        BookMeta meta = event.getNewBookMeta();
        List<String> pages = meta.getPages();

        if (pages.size() > maxBookPages) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, data, String.format("Oversized book pages: %d > %d", pages.size(), maxBookPages), 2.0);
            return;
        }

        for (String page : pages) {
            if (page.length() > 2048) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                flag(player, data, "Oversized single book page payload (>2048 chars)", 2.0);
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onSignChange(SignChangeEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            var lineComponent = event.line(i);
            if (lineComponent != null) {
                String plain = PlainTextComponentSerializer.plainText().serialize(lineComponent);
                if (plain.length() > maxSignLength) {
                    event.setCancelled(true);
                    flag(player, data, String.format("Sign line %d length overflow: %d chars > %d", i, plain.length(), maxSignLength), 2.0);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```
