`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/player/FastEatCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.player;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerItemConsumeEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects FastEat and FastUse cheats where items or consumables are used in abnormally short intervals.
 */
public class FastEatCheck extends Check {

    private long minConsumeDurationMs = 1350L; // 27 ticks (vanilla is 32 ticks = 1600ms)

    public FastEatCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "FastEat", "FastEat / FastUse (Consumable Timers)", CheckCategory.PLAYER);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        int minTicks = config.getInt(path + ".min-eat-ticks", 28);
        this.minConsumeDurationMs = minTicks * 50L;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        if (event.getAction() != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR && event.getAction() != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK) {
            return;
        }

        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        ItemStack item = event.getItem();
        if (item != null && (item.getType().isEdible() || item.getType() == Material.POTION || item.getType() == Material.MILK_BUCKET)) {
            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.registerItemUseStart();
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onItemConsume(PlayerItemConsumeEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        long startTime = data.getItemUseStartTimestamp();
        if (startTime == 0L) {
            return;
        }

        long duration = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;
        if (duration < minConsumeDurationMs) {
            flag(player, data, String.format("FastEat: consumed %s in %dms < min %dms",
                    event.getItem().getType(), duration, minConsumeDurationMs), 1.0);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/player/InventoryCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.player;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Detects InventoryMove hacks where players manipulate inventory items while sprinting or in combat.
 */
public class InventoryCheck extends Check {

    private int maxSprintClicks = 2;
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> sprintClickCount = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public InventoryCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Inventory", "Inventory (Illegal Clicks / InvMove)", CheckCategory.PLAYER);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxSprintClicks = config.getInt(path + ".max-sprint-clicks", 2);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player player)) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        // Vanilla clients cannot sprint while having an inventory container open and clicking items
        if (player.isSprinting()) {
            int count = sprintClickCount.compute(player.getUniqueId(), (k, v) -> v == null ? 1 : v + 1);
            if (count > maxSprintClicks) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("InvMove: %d item clicks while in active sprint state", count), 1.0);
            }
        } else {
            sprintClickCount.remove(player.getUniqueId());
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/world/AntiDupeCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.world;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryType;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerDropItemEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerPickupItemEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Anti-Dupe Check — detects item duplication exploits.
 *
 * <p>Strategy (no false positives):
 * <ul>
 *   <li><b>Rapid pickup burst</b>: more than {@code maxPickupsPerWindow} item pickups within
 *       {@code windowMs} milliseconds is abnormal (vanilla piston/dropper dupes, gravity block dupes).</li>
 *   <li><b>Stack-count jump</b>: if an item's stack count in the player's inventory suddenly
 *       increases beyond what a single pickup/craft could explain (delta &gt; maxSingleStackGain),
 *       flag and cancel the action.</li>
 *   <li><b>Drop → immediate pickup of inflated stack</b>: track drop counts per item type and
 *       compare against pickup counts. A pickup of significantly more items than were dropped
 *       triggers a flag.</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p>Exemptions: Creative/Spectator, OPs (if ops-bypass enabled), velocity/lag windows.
 */
public class AntiDupeCheck extends Check {

    /** Time window for rapid pickup detection (ms). */
    private long windowMs = 1000L;

    /** Max pickups allowed within windowMs before flagging. */
    private int maxPickupsPerWindow = 20;

    /** Max legitimate single-stack gain per pickup/craft event. */
    private int maxSingleStackGain = 64;

    /** Per-player ring buffers of pickup timestamps. */
    private final Map<UUID, Deque<Long>> pickupTimestamps = new HashMap<>();

    /** Per-player snapshot of inventory item amounts (material -> count). */
    private final Map<UUID, Map<Material, Integer>> inventorySnapshot = new HashMap<>();

    /** Per-player running count of drops per material (cleared after windowMs). */
    private final Map<UUID, Map<Material, Integer>> recentDrops = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> dropWindowStart = new HashMap<>();

    public AntiDupeCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "AntiDupe", "Anti-Dupe (Item Duplication)", CheckCategory.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.windowMs = config.getLong(path + ".window-ms", 1000L);
        this.maxPickupsPerWindow = config.getInt(path + ".max-pickups-per-window", 20);
        this.maxSingleStackGain = config.getInt(path + ".max-single-stack-gain", 64);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Drop tracking — record what the player drops so we can compare
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDrop(PlayerDropItemEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) return;

        UUID uid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // Reset drop window if stale
        long windowStart = dropWindowStart.getOrDefault(uid, 0L);
        if (now - windowStart > windowMs) {
            recentDrops.remove(uid);
            dropWindowStart.put(uid, now);
        }

        Material mat = event.getItemDrop().getItemStack().getType();
        int qty = event.getItemDrop().getItemStack().getAmount();
        recentDrops.computeIfAbsent(uid, k -> new HashMap<>())
                   .merge(mat, qty, Integer::sum);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Pickup tracking — check for suspicious gain
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPickup(PlayerPickupItemEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) return;

        UUID uid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // --- Check 1: rapid pickup burst ---
        Deque<Long> times = pickupTimestamps.computeIfAbsent(uid, k -> new ArrayDeque<>());
        times.addLast(now);
        // Prune entries outside the window
        while (!times.isEmpty() && now - times.peekFirst() > windowMs) {
            times.pollFirst();
        }
        if (times.size() > maxPickupsPerWindow) {
            flag(player, data,
                 String.format("Rapid pickups: %d in %dms (max %d)", times.size(), windowMs, maxPickupsPerWindow),
                 1.5);
            return;
        }

        // --- Check 2: stack-count jump ---
        ItemStack picked = event.getItem().getItemStack();
        Material mat = picked.getType();
        int pickedAmt = picked.getAmount();

        Map<Material, Integer> snapshot = inventorySnapshot.computeIfAbsent(uid, k -> buildSnapshot(player));
        int before = snapshot.getOrDefault(mat, 0);
        int currentInv = countInInventory(player, mat);

        // After pickup the inventory will have currentInv + pickedAmt
        int projectedTotal = currentInv + pickedAmt;
        int gain = projectedTotal - before;

        if (gain > maxSingleStackGain) {
            // Check if this can be explained by prior drops (dupe exploit usually drops then re-picks)
            int droppedAmt = recentDrops.getOrDefault(uid, new HashMap<>()).getOrDefault(mat, 0);
            int legitGain = droppedAmt + maxSingleStackGain;
            if (gain > legitGain) {
                flag(player, data,
                     String.format("Stack jump: %s gained %d (dropped %d, max legit %d)",
                                   mat.name(), gain, droppedAmt, legitGain),
                     2.0);
            }
        }

        // Update snapshot after pickup is processed
        snapshot.put(mat, projectedTotal);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Inventory interaction — detect impossible click patterns
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player player)) return;
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) return;

        // Only care about crafting result slots (common dupe vector)
        if (event.getSlotType() != InventoryType.SlotType.RESULT) return;

        ItemStack result = event.getCurrentItem();
        if (result == null || result.getType().isAir()) return;

        // If the player is in Creative, vanilla rules allow item creation — skip
        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) return;

        UUID uid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // Rapid crafting result extraction (e.g. bundle dupe, crafting dupe)
        Deque<Long> times = pickupTimestamps.computeIfAbsent(uid, k -> new ArrayDeque<>());
        times.addLast(now);
        while (!times.isEmpty() && now - times.peekFirst() > windowMs) {
            times.pollFirst();
        }
        // Threshold for crafting clicks is halved (each craft click is deliberate)
        if (times.size() > maxPickupsPerWindow / 2) {
            flag(player, data,
                 String.format("Rapid craft extractions: %d in %dms", times.size(), windowMs),
                 1.0);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Helpers
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private Map<Material, Integer> buildSnapshot(@NotNull Player player) {
        Map<Material, Integer> map = new HashMap<>();
        for (ItemStack item : player.getInventory().getContents()) {
            if (item != null && !item.getType().isAir()) {
                map.merge(item.getType(), item.getAmount(), Integer::sum);
            }
        }
        return map;
    }

    private int countInInventory(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Material mat) {
        int total = 0;
        for (ItemStack item : player.getInventory().getContents()) {
            if (item != null && item.getType() == mat) {
                total += item.getAmount();
            }
        }
        return total;
    }

    /** Must be called on player quit to prevent memory leak. */
    public void cleanup(@NotNull UUID uuid) {
        pickupTimestamps.remove(uuid);
        inventorySnapshot.remove(uuid);
        recentDrops.remove(uuid);
        dropWindowStart.remove(uuid);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/world/FastBreakCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.world;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.BlockUtil;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockDamageEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Detects FastBreak / InstantBreak cheats where players mine blocks faster than vanilla survival limits.
 */
public class FastBreakCheck extends Check {

    private double breakTolerance = 0.85;
    private final Map<String, Long> startDamageTimings = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public FastBreakCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "FastBreak", "FastBreak (Mining Speed)", CheckCategory.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.breakTolerance = config.getDouble(path + ".break-tolerance", 0.85);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockDamage(BlockDamageEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        String key = player.getUniqueId() + ":" + event.getBlock().getLocation().toVector().toString();
        startDamageTimings.put(key, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        Block block = event.getBlock();
        if (BlockUtil.isInstaBreak(block.getType())) {
            return;
        }

        String key = player.getUniqueId() + ":" + block.getLocation().toVector().toString();
        Long startTime = startDamageTimings.remove(key);

        long estimatedMinMs = (long) (BlockUtil.getEstimatedBreakDurationMs(player, block) * breakTolerance);
        if (startTime == null) {
            // Broken without any prior damage packet received
            if (estimatedMinMs > 150L) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Instant broken block %s with no damage duration (expected: %dms)",
                        block.getType(), estimatedMinMs), 1.0);
            }
            return;
        }

        long actualDuration = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;
        if (actualDuration < estimatedMinMs) {
            flag(player, data, String.format("FastBreak: %dms < EstimatedMin: %dms on %s",
                    actualDuration, estimatedMinMs, block.getType()), 1.0);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/world/FastPlaceCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.world;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects FastPlace hacks where players place blocks faster than the client 4-tick placement cooldown.
 */
public class FastPlaceCheck extends Check {

    private long minDelayMs = 80L;

    public FastPlaceCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "FastPlace", "FastPlace (Placement Cooldown)", CheckCategory.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.minDelayMs = config.getLong(path + ".min-delay-ms", 80L);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long lastPlace = data.getLastBlockPlaceTimestamp();
        data.registerBlockPlace();

        if (lastPlace == 0L) {
            return;
        }

        long delay = now - lastPlace;
        if (delay < minDelayMs) {
            flag(player, data, String.format("FastPlace: %dms delay < %dms minimum", delay, minDelayMs), 1.0);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/world/GhostHandCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.world;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.FluidCollisionMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.RayTraceResult;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects GhostHand hacks where players interact with or break blocks through solid occluding walls.
 */
public class GhostHandCheck extends Check {

    public GhostHandCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "GhostHand", "GhostHand (Wall Ray-Trace)", CheckCategory.WORLD);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        Block targetBlock = event.getBlock();
        Location eyeLoc = player.getEyeLocation();
        Vector direction = targetBlock.getLocation().add(0.5, 0.5, 0.5).toVector().subtract(eyeLoc.toVector());
        double maxDist = direction.length();

        if (maxDist < 0.2) {
            return;
        }

        direction.normalize();

        // Perform ray trace between eye and block
        RayTraceResult hit = player.getWorld().rayTraceBlocks(
                eyeLoc,
                direction,
                maxDist - 0.2,
                FluidCollisionMode.NEVER,
                true
        );

        if (hit != null && hit.getHitBlock() != null) {
            Block obstruction = hit.getHitBlock();
            if (!obstruction.equals(targetBlock) && obstruction.getType().isOccluding()) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Mined %s through solid wall %s at %d,%d,%d",
                        targetBlock.getType(), obstruction.getType(), obstruction.getX(), obstruction.getY(), obstruction.getZ()), 1.0);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/world/NukerCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.world;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.BlockUtil;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Detects Nuker cheats breaking multiple blocks in a single tick or at extreme frequencies.
 */
public class NukerCheck extends Check {

    private int maxBlocksPerTick = 2;
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastBreakTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> breakCountInWindow = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public NukerCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Nuker", "Nuker (Mass Block Breaking)", CheckCategory.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxBlocksPerTick = config.getInt(path + ".max-blocks-per-tick", 2);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        if (BlockUtil.isInstaBreak(event.getBlock().getType())) {
            return;
        }

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long lastTime = lastBreakTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L);

        if (now - lastTime < 50L) {
            int count = breakCountInWindow.compute(uuid, (k, v) -> v == null ? 1 : v + 1);
            if (count > maxBlocksPerTick) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Nuker: Broke %d non-insta blocks in <50ms", count), 1.0);
            }
        } else {
            breakCountInWindow.put(uuid, 1);
            lastBreakTime.put(uuid, now);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/world/ScaffoldCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.world;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.RayTraceUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects Scaffold / AutoBridge hacks where players place blocks without looking or while sprinting backwards.
 */
public class ScaffoldCheck extends Check {

    private double maxAngle = 75.0;

    public ScaffoldCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Scaffold", "Scaffold (Bridging / Placement Angle)", CheckCategory.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxAngle = config.getDouble(path + ".max-angle", 75.0);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        Block placed = event.getBlockPlaced();
        Location eyeLoc = player.getEyeLocation();
        Location blockCenter = placed.getLocation().add(0.5, 0.5, 0.5);

        double angle = RayTraceUtil.getAngleOffset(eyeLoc, blockCenter);

        // Placing blocks behind back or outside line of sight
        if (angle > maxAngle) {
            flag(player, data, String.format("Placing block outside field of view: angle=%.1f° > %.1f°", angle, maxAngle), 1.0);
            return;
        }

        // Downward bridging while sprinting without sneaking
        if (placed.getY() < player.getLocation().getY() && player.isSprinting() && !player.isSneaking()) {
            if (angle > 65.0) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Fast downward sprint bridging: angle=%.1f°", angle), 1.0);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/world/TowerCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.world;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Detects Tower hacks where players rapidly ascend vertically by spamming blocks beneath themselves.
 */
public class TowerCheck extends Check {

    private double maxTowerSpeed = 0.42;

    public TowerCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Tower", "Tower (Vertical Auto-Bridging)", CheckCategory.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.maxTowerSpeed = config.getDouble(path + ".max-tower-speed", 0.42);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        Block placed = event.getBlockPlaced();
        Location playerLoc = player.getLocation();

        // Check if block was placed directly below feet while moving straight up
        if (Math.abs(placed.getX() + 0.5 - playerLoc.getX()) < 0.6
                && Math.abs(placed.getZ() + 0.5 - playerLoc.getZ()) < 0.6
                && placed.getY() < playerLoc.getY()) {

            long lastPlace = data.getLastBlockPlaceTimestamp();
            long diff = System.currentTimeMillis() - lastPlace;

            // If ascending vertically and placing blocks at unnatural speed (<120ms without velocity)
            if (diff < 120L && player.getVelocity().getY() > maxTowerSpeed && !data.isVelocityPending()) {
                flag(player, data, String.format("Fast Tower placement: deltaY=%.3f, delay=%dms", player.getVelocity().getY(), diff), 1.0);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/check/world/XRayCheck.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.check.world;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.CheckCategory;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.PunishmentType;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.Particle;
import org.bukkit.Sound;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Detects and prevents X-Ray / FreeCam / Ore ESP cheats.
 * Rather than abruptly kicking players, this check blocks the suspicious ore from being harvested,
 * either by canceling the break or replacing the ore with surrounding stone.
 */
public class XRayCheck extends Check {

    private boolean blockOre = true;
    private boolean replaceWithStone = true;
    private int minStoneBetweenOres = 12;
    private int maxHiddenOreStreak = 3;

    private static final Set<Material> VALUABLE_ORES = Set.of(
            Material.DIAMOND_ORE,
            Material.DEEPSLATE_DIAMOND_ORE,
            Material.ANCIENT_DEBRIS,
            Material.EMERALD_ORE,
            Material.DEEPSLATE_EMERALD_ORE,
            Material.GOLD_ORE,
            Material.DEEPSLATE_GOLD_ORE,
            Material.NETHER_GOLD_ORE
    );

    private static final Set<Material> FILLER_BLOCKS = Set.of(
            Material.STONE,
            Material.DEEPSLATE,
            Material.NETHERRACK,
            Material.TUFF,
            Material.ANDESITE,
            Material.DIORITE,
            Material.GRANITE,
            Material.BLACKSTONE,
            Material.BASALT
    );

    private static final BlockFace[] ADJACENT_FACES = {
            BlockFace.NORTH, BlockFace.SOUTH, BlockFace.EAST, BlockFace.WEST, BlockFace.UP, BlockFace.DOWN
    };

    // Tracking state per player
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> stoneMinedSinceLastOre = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> hiddenOreStreak = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public XRayCheck(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "XRay", "XRay (Ore ESP / Tunneling)", CheckCategory.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadCustomConfig(@NotNull FileConfiguration config, @NotNull String path) {
        this.blockOre = config.getBoolean(path + ".block-ore", true);
        this.replaceWithStone = config.getBoolean(path + ".replace-with-stone", true);
        this.minStoneBetweenOres = config.getInt(path + ".min-stone-between-ores", 12);
        this.maxHiddenOreStreak = config.getInt(path + ".max-hidden-ore-streak", 3);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
        if (data == null || shouldSkip(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        Block block = event.getBlock();
        Material mat = block.getType();
        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();

        // Count stone and filler mining
        if (FILLER_BLOCKS.contains(mat)) {
            stoneMinedSinceLastOre.compute(uuid, (k, v) -> v == null ? 1 : v + 1);
            return;
        }

        // Only analyze high-value target ores
        if (!VALUABLE_ORES.contains(mat)) {
            return;
        }

        int stoneCount = stoneMinedSinceLastOre.getOrDefault(uuid, 0);
        boolean isHiddenOre = isOreBuried(block);

        int streak = hiddenOreStreak.getOrDefault(uuid, 0);
        if (isHiddenOre) {
            streak++;
            hiddenOreStreak.put(uuid, streak);
        } else {
            // Ore was naturally exposed to air/cave, reduce suspicion
            hiddenOreStreak.put(uuid, Math.max(0, streak - 1));
        }

        // Suspicious: mining hidden ores in succession without mining standard stone/deepslate tunnels
        if (isHiddenOre && (streak >= maxHiddenOreStreak || stoneCount < minStoneBetweenOres)) {
            String details = String.format("Mined buried %s (Stone mined: %d, Hidden streak: %d)",
                    mat.name().toLowerCase(), stoneCount, streak);

            // Flag violation
            flag(player, data, details, 1.5);

            // Reset counter
            stoneMinedSinceLastOre.put(uuid, 0);

            // Block the ore exploitation without kicking
            if (blockOre) {
                event.setCancelled(true);

                if (replaceWithStone) {
                    Material replacement = mat.name().contains("DEEPSLATE") ? Material.DEEPSLATE
                            : (mat == Material.ANCIENT_DEBRIS || mat == Material.NETHER_GOLD_ORE) ? Material.NETHERRACK
                            : Material.STONE;
                    block.setType(replacement, false);
                }

                // Visual and audio feedback
                player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.SMOKE, block.getLocation().add(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), 8, 0.2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.01);
                player.playSound(block.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_FIRE_EXTINGUISH, 0.7F, 1.2F);

                player.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                        "punish.xray-blocked",
                        "<prefix><red>This ore crumbled away as you touched it...</red>",
                        Map.of("player", player.getName(), "ore", mat.name().toLowerCase())
                ));
            }
        } else {
            // Legitimate mining find
            stoneMinedSinceLastOre.put(uuid, 0);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Checks if an ore block is buried inside rock with 0 exposed air/liquid surfaces.
     */
    private boolean isOreBuried(Block block) {
        for (BlockFace face : ADJACENT_FACES) {
            Block relative = block.getRelative(face);
            Material type = relative.getType();
            if (type.isAir() || type == Material.CAVE_AIR || type == Material.VOID_AIR || type == Material.WATER) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/command/NexoraCommand.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.command;

import com.mojang.brigadier.arguments.StringArgumentType;
import com.mojang.brigadier.builder.LiteralArgumentBuilder;
import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.check.Check;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.PunishmentType;
import com.nexora.anticheat.storage.ViolationRecord;
import io.papermc.paper.command.brigadier.CommandSourceStack;
import io.papermc.paper.command.brigadier.Commands;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.minimessage.MiniMessage;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Modern Paper Brigadier command handler for /nxanti and its administration subcommands.
 */
public class NexoraCommand {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;

    public NexoraCommand(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void register(@NotNull Commands commands) {
        LiteralArgumentBuilder<CommandSourceStack> root = Commands.literal("nxanti")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.help")
                        || source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.admin"))
                .executes(ctx -> {
                    sendHelp(ctx.getSource().getSender());
                    return 1;
                });

        // /nxanti help
        root.then(Commands.literal("help")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.help"))
                .executes(ctx -> {
                    sendHelp(ctx.getSource().getSender());
                    return 1;
                }));

        // /nxanti reload
        root.then(Commands.literal("reload")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.reload"))
                .executes(ctx -> {
                    CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                    long start = System.currentTimeMillis();
                    try {
                        plugin.reloadPlugin();
                        long duration = System.currentTimeMillis() - start;
                        Component msg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                "general.reload-success",
                                "<prefix><green>Configuration, checks, and messages reloaded successfully in <yellow><ms>ms</yellow>!</green>",
                                Map.of("ms", String.valueOf(duration))
                        );
                        sender.sendMessage(msg);
                    } catch (Exception ex) {
                        Component msg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                "general.reload-failed",
                                "<prefix><red>Failed to reload configuration: <error></red>",
                                Map.of("error", ex.getMessage())
                        );
                        sender.sendMessage(msg);
                    }
                    return 1;
                }));

        // /nxanti alerts [on|off]
        root.then(Commands.literal("alerts")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.alerts"))
                .executes(ctx -> {
                    CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                    if (sender instanceof Player player) {
                        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
                        if (data != null) {
                            boolean newState = !data.areAlertsEnabled();
                            data.setAlertsEnabled(newState);
                            sendAlertStatus(player, newState);
                        }
                    } else {
                        sender.sendMessage(Component.text("Console always receives violation alerts."));
                    }
                    return 1;
                })
                .then(Commands.argument("state", StringArgumentType.word())
                        .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                            builder.suggest("on");
                            builder.suggest("off");
                            return builder.buildFuture();
                        })
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                            String state = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "state");
                            if (sender instanceof Player player) {
                                PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(player);
                                if (data != null) {
                                    boolean enable = state.equalsIgnoreCase("on");
                                    data.setAlertsEnabled(enable);
                                    sendAlertStatus(player, enable);
                                }
                            }
                            return 1;
                        })));

        // /nxanti info <player>
        root.then(Commands.literal("info")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.info"))
                .then(Commands.argument("player", StringArgumentType.word())
                        .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                            for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                                builder.suggest(p.getName());
                            }
                            return builder.buildFuture();
                        })
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                            String targetName = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "player");
                            Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(targetName);
                            if (target == null) {
                                sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                        "general.player-not-found",
                                        "<prefix><red>Player <yellow><player></yellow> was not found online.</red>",
                                        Map.of("player", targetName)
                                ));
                                return 0;
                            }
                            sendPlayerInfo(sender, target);
                            return 1;
                        })));

        // /nxanti history <player>
        root.then(Commands.literal("history")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.history"))
                .then(Commands.argument("player", StringArgumentType.word())
                        .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                            // Suggest all online players
                            for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                                builder.suggest(p.getName());
                            }
                            // Suggest all recorded offline players from database
                            for (String recordedName : plugin.getStorageManager().getCachedPlayerNames()) {
                                builder.suggest(recordedName);
                            }
                            return builder.buildFuture();
                        })
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                            String targetName = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "player");
                            sendPlayerHistoryByName(sender, targetName);
                            return 1;
                        })));

        // /nxanti reset <player>
        root.then(Commands.literal("reset")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.reset"))
                .then(Commands.argument("player", StringArgumentType.word())
                        .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                            for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                                builder.suggest(p.getName());
                            }
                            return builder.buildFuture();
                        })
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                            String targetName = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "player");
                            Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(targetName);
                            if (target == null) {
                                sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                        "general.player-not-found",
                                        "<prefix><red>Player <yellow><player></yellow> was not found online.</red>",
                                        Map.of("player", targetName)
                                ));
                                return 0;
                            }
                            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(target);
                            if (data != null) {
                                data.resetViolations();
                                sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                        "reset.success",
                                        "<prefix><green>Successfully reset all violations for <yellow><player></yellow>.</green>",
                                        Map.of("player", target.getName())
                                ));
                            }
                            return 1;
                        })));

        // /nxanti punish <player> <reason>
        root.then(Commands.literal("punish")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.punish"))
                .then(Commands.argument("player", StringArgumentType.word())
                        .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                            for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                                builder.suggest(p.getName());
                            }
                            return builder.buildFuture();
                        })
                        .then(Commands.argument("reason", StringArgumentType.greedyString())
                                .executes(ctx -> {
                                    CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                                    String targetName = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "player");
                                    String reason = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "reason");
                                    Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(targetName);
                                    if (target == null) {
                                        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                                "general.player-not-found",
                                                "<prefix><red>Player <yellow><player></yellow> was not found online.</red>",
                                                Map.of("player", targetName)
                                        ));
                                        return 0;
                                    }
                                    PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(target);
                                    if (data != null) {
                                        plugin.getPunishmentManager().executePunishment(target, data, reason, PunishmentType.KICK, data.getTotalViolationLevel());
                                        sender.sendMessage(Component.text("Executed kick punishment on " + target.getName()));
                                    }
                                    return 1;
                                }))));

        // /nxanti exempt <player> [duration]
        root.then(Commands.literal("exempt")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.exempt"))
                .then(Commands.argument("player", StringArgumentType.word())
                        .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                            for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                                builder.suggest(p.getName());
                            }
                            return builder.buildFuture();
                        })
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                            String targetName = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "player");
                            Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(targetName);
                            if (target == null) {
                                sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                        "general.player-not-found",
                                        "<prefix><red>Player <yellow><player></yellow> was not found online.</red>",
                                        Map.of("player", targetName)
                                ));
                                return 0;
                            }
                            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(target);
                            if (data != null) {
                                if (data.isExempt()) {
                                    data.clearExemption();
                                    sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                            "exempt.removed",
                                            "<prefix><green>Removed exemption for <yellow><player></yellow>.</green>",
                                            Map.of("player", target.getName())
                                    ));
                                } else {
                                    data.setExemption(300_000L); // Default 5 minutes
                                    sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                            "exempt.success",
                                            "<prefix><green>Player <yellow><player></yellow> is now exempt from checks for <yellow><duration></yellow>.</green>",
                                            Map.of("player", target.getName(), "duration", "5m")
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                            return 1;
                        })
                        .then(Commands.argument("duration", StringArgumentType.word())
                                .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                                    builder.suggest("1m");
                                    builder.suggest("5m");
                                    builder.suggest("15m");
                                    builder.suggest("1h");
                                    return builder.buildFuture();
                                })
                                .executes(ctx -> {
                                    CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                                    String targetName = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "player");
                                    String durStr = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "duration");
                                    Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(targetName);
                                    if (target == null) {
                                        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                                "general.player-not-found",
                                                "<prefix><red>Player <yellow><player></yellow> was not found online.</red>",
                                                Map.of("player", targetName)
                                        ));
                                        return 0;
                                    }
                                    long ms = parseDuration(durStr);
                                    PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(target);
                                    if (data != null) {
                                        data.setExemption(ms);
                                        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                                "exempt.success",
                                                "<prefix><green>Player <yellow><player></yellow> is now exempt from checks for <yellow><duration></yellow>.</green>",
                                                Map.of("player", target.getName(), "duration", durStr)
                                        ));
                                    }
                                    return 1;
                                }))));

        // /nxanti gui
        root.then(Commands.literal("gui")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.gui"))
                .executes(ctx -> {
                    CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                    if (sender instanceof Player player) {
                        plugin.getStaffGuiManager().openGui(player);
                    } else {
                        sender.sendMessage(Component.text("The GUI can only be opened by players."));
                    }
                    return 1;
                }));

        // /nxanti debug <check>
        root.then(Commands.literal("debug")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.debug"))
                .then(Commands.argument("check", StringArgumentType.word())
                        .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                            for (Check c : plugin.getCheckManager().getAllChecks()) {
                                builder.suggest(c.getId());
                            }
                            return builder.buildFuture();
                        })
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                            String checkName = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "check");
                            Check check = plugin.getCheckManager().getCheck(checkName);
                            if (check == null) {
                                sender.sendMessage(Component.text("Check not found: " + checkName));
                                return 0;
                            }
                            boolean newState = !check.isDebug();
                            check.setDebug(newState);
                            Component msg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                    newState ? "debug.enabled" : "debug.disabled",
                                    newState ? "<prefix><green>Debug mode enabled for check <yellow><check></yellow>.</green>"
                                             : "<prefix><red>Debug mode disabled for check <yellow><check></yellow>.</red>",
                                    Map.of("check", check.getId())
                            );
                            sender.sendMessage(msg);
                            return 1;
                        })));

        // /nxanti unban <player|ip>
        root.then(Commands.literal("unban")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.unban")
                        || source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.admin"))
                .then(Commands.argument("target", StringArgumentType.word())
                        .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                            for (String name : plugin.getStorageManager().getCachedPlayerNames()) {
                                builder.suggest(name);
                            }
                            return builder.buildFuture();
                        })
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                            String target = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "target");

                            if (target.contains(".")) {
                                // Assume IP unban
                                plugin.getBanManager().unbanIp(target);
                                sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                        "punish.unban-ip-success",
                                        "<prefix><green>IP address <yellow><target></yellow> has been unbanned.</green>",
                                        Map.of("target", target)
                                ));
                            } else {
                                // Player name or UUID unban
                                plugin.getBanManager().unbanPlayer(target);
                                sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                        "punish.unban-success",
                                        "<prefix><green>Player <yellow><target></yellow> has been unbanned and their kick counter reset.</green>",
                                        Map.of("target", target)
                                ));
                            }
                            return 1;
                        })));

        // /nxanti bans <player>
        root.then(Commands.literal("bans")
                .requires(source -> source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.command.bans")
                        || source.getSender().hasPermission("nexora.admin"))
                .then(Commands.argument("player", StringArgumentType.word())
                        .suggests((ctx, builder) -> {
                            for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                                builder.suggest(p.getName());
                            }
                            for (String name : plugin.getStorageManager().getCachedPlayerNames()) {
                                builder.suggest(name);
                            }
                            return builder.buildFuture();
                        })
                        .executes(ctx -> {
                            CommandSender sender = ctx.getSource().getSender();
                            String targetName = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "player");
                            sendPlayerBans(sender, targetName);
                            return 1;
                        })));

        // Register main command with aliases /nexora and /nxac
        commands.register(root.build(), "Nexora AntiCheat main administration command", List.of("nexora", "nxac"));
    }

    private void sendHelp(CommandSender sender) {
        MiniMessage mm = MiniMessage.miniMessage();
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize("<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━ <bold>Nexora AntiCheat Help</bold> ━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti help</red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>Displays this command overview</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti reload</red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>Reloads configuration, messages & checks</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti alerts [on|off]</red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>Toggles staff violation notifications</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti info <player></red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>View player telemetry and violation stats</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti history <player></red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>View logged violations history</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti reset <player></red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>Clears active violation levels for player</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti punish <player> <reason></red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>Manually punish player</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti exempt <player> [duration]</red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>Temporarily exempt player</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti unban <player|ip></red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>Pardon a banned player or IP address</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti bans <player></red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>View ban records and escalation history</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti gui</red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>Open interactive staff overview GUI</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize(" <red>/nxanti debug <check></red> <dark_gray>-</dark_gray> <gray>Toggle check debugging</gray>"));
        sender.sendMessage(mm.deserialize("<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>"));
    }

    private void sendAlertStatus(Player player, boolean enabled) {
        Component msg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                enabled ? "alerts.enabled" : "alerts.disabled",
                enabled ? "<prefix><green>Staff violation alerts have been <bold>ENABLED</bold>.</green>"
                        : "<prefix><red>Staff violation alerts have been <bold>DISABLED</bold>.</red>",
                Map.of()
        );
        player.sendMessage(msg);
    }

    private void sendPlayerInfo(CommandSender sender, Player target) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(target);
        if (data == null) return;

        Map<String, String> placeholders = Map.of(
                "player", target.getName(),
                "uuid", target.getUniqueId().toString(),
                "ping", String.valueOf(target.getPing()),
                "tps", String.format("%.1f", Math.min(20.0, Bukkit.getTPS()[0])),
                "brand", data.getClientBrand(),
                "exempt", data.isExempt() ? "Yes" : "No",
                "total_vl", String.format("%.1f", data.getTotalViolationLevel())
        );

        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent("info.header", "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━ <bold>Nexora Player Info</bold> ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>", placeholders));
        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent("info.player", "<gray>Player: <yellow><player></yellow> <dark_gray>(<white><uuid></white>)</dark_gray>", placeholders));
        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent("info.ping-tps", "<gray>Ping: <green><ping>ms</green> | Server TPS: <green><tps></green>", placeholders));
        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent("info.client-brand", "<gray>Client Brand: <aqua><brand></aqua>", placeholders));
        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent("info.exempted", "<gray>Exempted: <yellow><exempt></yellow>", placeholders));
        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent("info.total-vl", "<gray>Total Violations: <red><total_vl></red>", placeholders));

        Map<String, Double> violations = data.getViolations();
        if (!violations.isEmpty()) {
            sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent("info.active-checks-header", "<gray>Active Check Flags:</gray>", placeholders));
            violations.forEach((checkId, vl) -> {
                if (vl > 0.0) {
                    Check check = plugin.getCheckManager().getCheck(checkId);
                    double maxVl = check != null ? check.getMaxVl() : 10.0;
                    sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "info.check-entry",
                            "  <dark_gray>•</dark_gray> <yellow><check></yellow>: <red><vl></red> <dark_gray>/ <gray><max_vl></gray></dark_gray>",
                            Map.of("check", checkId, "vl", String.format("%.1f", vl), "max_vl", String.format("%.1f", maxVl))
                    ));
                }
            });
        }
        sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent("info.footer", "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>", placeholders));
    }

    private void sendPlayerHistoryByName(CommandSender sender, String targetName) {
        plugin.getStorageManager().getHistoryByNameAsync(targetName, 15).thenAccept(records -> {
            boolean isOnline = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(targetName) != null;
            String displayName = isOnline ? targetName : targetName + " <gray>(Offline)</gray>";

            sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                    "history.header",
                    "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━ <bold>Violation History: <player></bold> ━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>",
                    Map.of("player", displayName)
            ));

            if (records.isEmpty()) {
                sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                        "history.empty",
                        "<prefix><gray>No violation logs found for <yellow><player></yellow>.</gray>",
                        Map.of("player", targetName)
                ));
            } else {
                for (ViolationRecord record : records) {
                    sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "history.entry",
                            "<dark_gray>[<gray><time></gray>]</dark_gray> <yellow><check></yellow> <dark_gray>(<red>VL: <vl></red>)</dark_gray> <gray>- <white><details></white> <dark_gray>(<green><ping>ms</green>)</dark_gray>",
                            Map.of(
                                    "time", record.formattedTime(),
                                    "check", record.checkId(),
                                    "vl", String.format("%.1f", record.vl()),
                                    "details", record.details(),
                                    "ping", String.valueOf(record.ping())
                            )
                    ));
                }
            }
            sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                    "history.footer",
                    "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>",
                    Map.of()
            ));
        });
    }

    private void sendPlayerBans(CommandSender sender, String targetName) {
        plugin.getStorageManager().getBanHistoryByNameAsync(targetName, 10).thenAccept(records -> {
            sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                    "bans.header",
                    "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━ <bold>Ban History: <player></bold> ━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>",
                    Map.of("player", targetName)
            ));

            if (records.isEmpty()) {
                sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                        "bans.empty",
                        "<prefix><gray>No ban records found for <yellow><player></yellow>.</gray>",
                        Map.of("player", targetName)
                ));
            } else {
                for (com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.BanRecord ban : records) {
                    String status = ban.active() ? (ban.isExpired() ? "<yellow>[Expired]</yellow>" : "<red>[Active]</red>") : "<gray>[Pardoned]</gray>";
                    sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "bans.entry",
                            "<status> <aqua><duration></aqua> <gray>- Reason: <yellow><reason></yellow> <dark_gray>(Expires: <gold><expires></gold>)</dark_gray></gray>",
                            Map.of(
                                    "status", status,
                                    "duration", ban.tierDescription(),
                                    "reason", ban.reason(),
                                    "expires", ban.formattedExpiresAt()
                            )
                    ));
                }
            }

            sender.sendMessage(plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                    "bans.footer",
                    "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>",
                    Map.of()
            ));
        });
    }

    private long parseDuration(String str) {
        try {
            str = str.toLowerCase();
            if (str.endsWith("s")) {
                return Long.parseLong(str.substring(0, str.length() - 1)) * 1000L;
            } else if (str.endsWith("m")) {
                return Long.parseLong(str.substring(0, str.length() - 1)) * 60_000L;
            } else if (str.endsWith("h")) {
                return Long.parseLong(str.substring(0, str.length() - 1)) * 3600_000L;
            } else if (str.endsWith("d")) {
                return Long.parseLong(str.substring(0, str.length() - 1)) * 86400_000L;
            } else {
                return Long.parseLong(str) * 1000L;
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return 300_000L; // Default 5m
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/config/ConfigManager.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.config;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.logging.Level;

/**
 * Loads, verifies, and reloads plugin configuration files without requiring a server reboot.
 */
public class ConfigManager {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;
    private final Messages messages = new Messages();

    private FileConfiguration config;
    private FileConfiguration messagesConfig;
    private FileConfiguration checksConfig;

    private File configFile;
    private File messagesFile;
    private File checksFile;

    public ConfigManager(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void loadAll() {
        if (!plugin.getDataFolder().exists()) {
            plugin.getDataFolder().mkdirs();
        }

        configFile = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "config.yml");
        if (!configFile.exists()) {
            plugin.saveResource("config.yml", false);
        }
        config = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(configFile);

        messagesFile = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "messages.yml");
        if (!messagesFile.exists()) {
            plugin.saveResource("messages.yml", false);
        }
        messagesConfig = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(messagesFile);
        messages.load(messagesConfig);

        checksFile = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "checks.yml");
        if (!checksFile.exists()) {
            plugin.saveResource("checks.yml", false);
        }
        checksConfig = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(checksFile);

        validate();
    }

    private void validate() {
        if (config.getDouble("settings.lag-compensation.min-tps", 18.5) <= 0) {
            plugin.getLogger().warning("Invalid min-tps configured! Resetting to 18.5");
            config.set("settings.lag-compensation.min-tps", 18.5);
        }
    }

    public void reload() {
        loadAll();
    }

    @NotNull
    public FileConfiguration getConfig() {
        return config;
    }

    @NotNull
    public FileConfiguration getMessagesConfig() {
        return messagesConfig;
    }

    @NotNull
    public FileConfiguration getChecksConfig() {
        return checksConfig;
    }

    @NotNull
    public Messages getMessages() {
        return messages;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/config/Messages.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.config;

import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.minimessage.MiniMessage;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.minimessage.tag.resolver.Placeholder;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.minimessage.tag.resolver.TagResolver;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Message translation and MiniMessage rendering utility with #FF0000 styling.
 */
public class Messages {

    private final MiniMessage miniMessage = MiniMessage.miniMessage();
    private FileConfiguration messagesConfig;
    private String prefix = "<#FF0000><bold>Nexora</bold></#FF0000> <dark_gray>»</dark_gray> ";

    public void load(@NotNull FileConfiguration config) {
        this.messagesConfig = config;
        this.prefix = config.getString("prefix", "<#FF0000><bold>Nexora</bold></#FF0000> <dark_gray>»</dark_gray> ");
    }

    @NotNull
    public String getRaw(@NotNull String path, @NotNull String def) {
        if (messagesConfig == null) return def;
        return messagesConfig.getString(path, def);
    }

    @NotNull
    public Component getComponent(@NotNull String path, @NotNull String def, Map<String, String> placeholders) {
        String raw = getRaw(path, def);
        raw = raw.replace("<prefix>", prefix);

        List<TagResolver> resolvers = new ArrayList<>();
        if (placeholders != null) {
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : placeholders.entrySet()) {
                resolvers.add(Placeholder.parsed(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue()));
            }
        }

        return miniMessage.deserialize(raw, TagResolver.resolver(resolvers));
    }

    @NotNull
    public Component parse(@NotNull String raw, Map<String, String> placeholders) {
        String formatted = raw.replace("<prefix>", prefix);
        List<TagResolver> resolvers = new ArrayList<>();
        if (placeholders != null) {
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : placeholders.entrySet()) {
                resolvers.add(Placeholder.parsed(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue()));
            }
        }
        return miniMessage.deserialize(formatted, TagResolver.resolver(resolvers));
    }

    @NotNull
    public String getPrefix() {
        return prefix;
    }
}
```
