`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/data/LocationRecord.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.data;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

/**
 * Immutable snapshot of a player's spatial transform and environmental state at a given instant.
 */
public record LocationRecord(
        double x,
        double y,
        double z,
        float yaw,
        float pitch,
        boolean onGround,
        long timestamp
) {
    public static LocationRecord fromLocation(@NotNull Location location, boolean onGround) {
        return new LocationRecord(
                location.getX(),
                location.getY(),
                location.getZ(),
                location.getYaw(),
                location.getPitch(),
                onGround,
                System.currentTimeMillis()
        );
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/data/PlayerData.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.data;

import com.nexora.anticheat.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedDeque;

/**
 * Thread-safe container encapsulating all anti-cheat telemetry and temporal state for a player.
 */
public class PlayerData {

    private final UUID uniqueId;
    private String username;
    private String clientBrand = "vanilla";

    // Movement History
    private final Deque<LocationRecord> movementHistory = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();
    private static final int MAX_HISTORY_SIZE = 40;
    private Location lastSafeGroundLocation;

    // Click Analysis
    private final Deque<Long> leftClickTimings = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();
    private static final int MAX_CLICK_SAMPLES = 50;

    // Server-sent Velocity Tracking
    private double expectedVelocityX;
    private double expectedVelocityY;
    private double expectedVelocityZ;
    private long lastVelocityTimestamp;
    private boolean velocityPending;

    // Immunity & Lag State Timestamps
    private long lastTeleportTimestamp;
    private long lastRespawnTimestamp;
    private final long joinTimestamp;
    private long exemptionExpiry = 0L;

    // Combat & Action Timestamps
    private long lastAttackTimestamp;
    private UUID lastTargetId;
    private long lastBlockPlaceTimestamp;
    private long lastBlockBreakTimestamp;
    private long itemUseStartTimestamp;

    // Violations Map: Check ID -> Violation Level
    private final Map<String, Double> violationLevels = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    // Movement per-tick state (used by SpeedCheck to distinguish jump-launch tick)
    private volatile boolean lastTickOnGround = true;

    // Alert toggles (Staff)
    private boolean alertsEnabled = true;

    public PlayerData(@NotNull UUID uniqueId, @NotNull String username) {
        this.uniqueId = uniqueId;
        this.username = username;
        this.joinTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public UUID getUniqueId() {
        return uniqueId;
    }

    public String getUsername() {
        return username;
    }

    public void setUsername(String username) {
        this.username = username;
    }

    public String getClientBrand() {
        return clientBrand;
    }

    public void setClientBrand(String clientBrand) {
        this.clientBrand = clientBrand;
    }

    // --- Movement History ---

    public void addLocationRecord(@NotNull Location location, boolean onGround) {
        movementHistory.addFirst(LocationRecord.fromLocation(location, onGround));
        while (movementHistory.size() > MAX_HISTORY_SIZE) {
            movementHistory.removeLast();
        }

        if (onGround && location.getBlock().getType().isSolid()) {
            this.lastSafeGroundLocation = location.clone();
        }
    }

    @Nullable
    public LocationRecord getPreviousLocation(int index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= movementHistory.size()) {
            return null;
        }
        Iterator<LocationRecord> it = movementHistory.iterator();
        LocationRecord current = null;
        for (int i = 0; i <= index && it.hasNext(); i++) {
            current = it.next();
        }
        return current;
    }

    @Nullable
    public Location getLastSafeGroundLocation() {
        return lastSafeGroundLocation;
    }

    public void setLastSafeGroundLocation(@Nullable Location loc) {
        this.lastSafeGroundLocation = loc != null ? loc.clone() : null;
    }

    // --- Clicks & AutoClicker Analysis ---

    public void registerLeftClick() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        leftClickTimings.addFirst(now);
        while (leftClickTimings.size() > MAX_CLICK_SAMPLES) {
            leftClickTimings.removeLast();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Calculates the Clicks Per Second (CPS) within the last 1000 milliseconds.
     */
    public double getCps() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long threshold = now - 1000L;
        int count = 0;
        for (Long timestamp : leftClickTimings) {
            if (timestamp >= threshold) {
                count++;
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        return count;
    }

    /**
     * Computes the statistical variance of the click interval delays in milliseconds.
     */
    public double getClickIntervalVariance() {
        List<Long> intervals = new ArrayList<>();
        Long prev = null;
        for (Long timestamp : leftClickTimings) {
            if (prev != null) {
                intervals.add(Math.abs(prev - timestamp));
            }
            prev = timestamp;
            if (intervals.size() >= 20) break;
        }
        return MathUtil.getVariance(intervals);
    }

    // --- Server-Sent Velocity ---

    public void registerServerVelocity(double vx, double vy, double vz) {
        this.expectedVelocityX = vx;
        this.expectedVelocityY = vy;
        this.expectedVelocityZ = vz;
        this.lastVelocityTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
        this.velocityPending = true;
    }

    public boolean isVelocityPending() {
        return velocityPending;
    }

    public void setVelocityPending(boolean pending) {
        this.velocityPending = pending;
    }

    public long getLastVelocityTimestamp() {
        return lastVelocityTimestamp;
    }

    public double getExpectedVelocityY() {
        return expectedVelocityY;
    }

    public double getExpectedHorizontalVelocity() {
        return Math.sqrt(expectedVelocityX * expectedVelocityX + expectedVelocityZ * expectedVelocityZ);
    }

    // --- Action Timestamps ---

    public void registerTeleport() {
        this.lastTeleportTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public long getLastTeleportTimestamp() {
        return lastTeleportTimestamp;
    }

    public void registerRespawn() {
        this.lastRespawnTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public long getLastRespawnTimestamp() {
        return lastRespawnTimestamp;
    }

    public long getJoinTimestamp() {
        return joinTimestamp;
    }

    public void registerAttack(UUID targetId) {
        this.lastAttackTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
        this.lastTargetId = targetId;
    }

    public long getLastAttackTimestamp() {
        return lastAttackTimestamp;
    }

    public UUID getLastTargetId() {
        return lastTargetId;
    }

    public void registerBlockPlace() {
        this.lastBlockPlaceTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public long getLastBlockPlaceTimestamp() {
        return lastBlockPlaceTimestamp;
    }

    public void registerBlockBreak() {
        this.lastBlockBreakTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public long getLastBlockBreakTimestamp() {
        return lastBlockBreakTimestamp;
    }

    public void registerItemUseStart() {
        this.itemUseStartTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public long getItemUseStartTimestamp() {
        return itemUseStartTimestamp;
    }

    // --- Exemption ---

    public boolean isExempt() {
        return System.currentTimeMillis() < exemptionExpiry;
    }

    public void setExemption(long durationMs) {
        this.exemptionExpiry = System.currentTimeMillis() + durationMs;
    }

    public void clearExemption() {
        this.exemptionExpiry = 0L;
    }

    public long getExemptionExpiry() {
        return exemptionExpiry;
    }

    // --- Violation Tracking ---

    public double getViolationLevel(String checkId) {
        return violationLevels.getOrDefault(checkId.toLowerCase(), 0.0);
    }

    public double addViolationLevel(String checkId, double amount) {
        return violationLevels.compute(checkId.toLowerCase(), (id, current) -> (current == null ? 0.0 : current) + amount);
    }

    public void decayViolationLevels(double decayAmount) {
        violationLevels.replaceAll((k, v) -> Math.max(0.0, v - decayAmount));
    }

    public void resetViolations() {
        violationLevels.clear();
    }

    public double getTotalViolationLevel() {
        double sum = 0.0;
        for (Double vl : violationLevels.values()) {
            sum += vl;
        }
        return sum;
    }

    public Map<String, Double> getViolations() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableMap(violationLevels);
    }

    // --- Ground State (per-tick, for SpeedCheck jump detection) ---

    public boolean wasLastTickOnGround() {
        return lastTickOnGround;
    }

    public void setLastTickOnGround(boolean onGround) {
        this.lastTickOnGround = onGround;
    }

    public boolean areAlertsEnabled() {
        return alertsEnabled;
    }

    public void setAlertsEnabled(boolean alertsEnabled) {
        this.alertsEnabled = alertsEnabled;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/data/PlayerManager.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.data;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerJoinEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerQuitEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerRespawnEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerTeleportEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerVelocityEvent;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.BedrockUtil;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Manages the lifecycle of PlayerData instances and hooks core Bukkit state transition events.
 */
public class PlayerManager implements Listener {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;
    private final Map<UUID, PlayerData> playerDataMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public PlayerManager(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    @NotNull
    public PlayerData getOrCreate(@NotNull Player player) {
        return playerDataMap.computeIfAbsent(player.getUniqueId(),
                uuid -> new PlayerData(uuid, player.getName()));
    }

    @Nullable
    public PlayerData getData(@NotNull Player player) {
        return playerDataMap.get(player.getUniqueId());
    }

    @Nullable
    public PlayerData getData(@NotNull UUID uuid) {
        return playerDataMap.get(uuid);
    }

    @NotNull
    public Collection<PlayerData> getAllData() {
        return playerDataMap.values();
    }

    public void remove(@NotNull UUID uuid) {
        playerDataMap.remove(uuid);
    }

    public void clearAll() {
        playerDataMap.clear();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onPlayerJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = getOrCreate(player);
        data.setUsername(player.getName());
        data.setLastSafeGroundLocation(player.getLocation());
        data.addLocationRecord(player.getLocation(), player.isOnGround());
        // Resolve and cache whether this player is a Bedrock client
        BedrockUtil.onPlayerJoin(player);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        // Complete memory cleanup on player quit
        BedrockUtil.onPlayerQuit(event.getPlayer());
        playerDataMap.remove(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
        // Clean up AntiDupeCheck per-player state
        com.nexora.anticheat.check.world.AntiDupeCheck antiDupe =
                (com.nexora.anticheat.check.world.AntiDupeCheck)
                        plugin.getCheckManager().getCheck("AntiDupe");
        if (antiDupe != null) {
            antiDupe.cleanup(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = getData(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.registerTeleport();
            data.setLastSafeGroundLocation(event.getTo());
            data.addLocationRecord(event.getTo(), event.getPlayer().isOnGround());
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerRespawn(PlayerRespawnEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = getData(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.registerRespawn();
            data.setLastSafeGroundLocation(event.getRespawnLocation());
            data.addLocationRecord(event.getRespawnLocation(), true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerVelocity(PlayerVelocityEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = getData(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            Vector v = event.getVelocity();
            data.registerServerVelocity(v.getX(), v.getY(), v.getZ());
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onEntityDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = getData(player);
            if (data != null) {
                // Damage can cause knockback or damage-tick delays
                data.registerServerVelocity(0.1, 0.4, 0.1);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/gui/StaffGuiManager.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.gui;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.PunishmentType;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.format.NamedTextColor;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.format.TextDecoration;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.Inventory;
import org.bukkit.inventory.InventoryHolder;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.inventory.meta.SkullMeta;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Interactive staff overview GUI displaying live suspicious player metrics and quick-action tools.
 */
public class StaffGuiManager implements Listener {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;
    private static final Component GUI_TITLE = Component.text("Nexora AntiCheat » Staff GUI", NamedTextColor.RED, TextDecoration.BOLD);

    public StaffGuiManager(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public static class NexoraGuiHolder implements InventoryHolder {
        private final Inventory inventory;

        public NexoraGuiHolder(int size) {
            this.inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(this, size, GUI_TITLE);
        }

        @Override
        public @NotNull Inventory getInventory() {
            return inventory;
        }
    }

    public void openGui(@NotNull Player staff) {
        NexoraGuiHolder holder = new NexoraGuiHolder(54);
        Inventory inv = holder.getInventory();

        int slot = 0;
        for (Player target : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            if (slot >= 54) break;

            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(target);
            if (data == null) continue;

            double totalVl = data.getTotalViolationLevel();

            ItemStack head = new ItemStack(Material.PLAYER_HEAD);
            SkullMeta meta = (SkullMeta) head.getItemMeta();
            if (meta != null) {
                meta.setOwningPlayer(target);
                meta.displayName(Component.text(target.getName(), totalVl > 0 ? NamedTextColor.RED : NamedTextColor.GREEN));

                List<Component> lore = new ArrayList<>();
                lore.add(Component.text("Ping: " + target.getPing() + "ms", NamedTextColor.GRAY));
                lore.add(Component.text("Total VL: " + String.format("%.1f", totalVl), totalVl > 0 ? NamedTextColor.RED : NamedTextColor.YELLOW));
                lore.add(Component.text("Client: " + data.getClientBrand(), NamedTextColor.DARK_AQUA));
                lore.add(Component.empty());

                Map<String, Double> violations = data.getViolations();
                if (!violations.isEmpty()) {
                    lore.add(Component.text("Active Flags:", NamedTextColor.GOLD));
                    violations.forEach((check, vl) -> {
                        if (vl > 0.0) {
                            lore.add(Component.text(" • " + check + ": " + String.format("%.1f", vl), NamedTextColor.RED));
                        }
                    });
                    lore.add(Component.empty());
                }

                lore.add(Component.text("Left-Click to Teleport", NamedTextColor.YELLOW));
                lore.add(Component.text("Right-Click to Reset VL", NamedTextColor.AQUA));
                lore.add(Component.text("Shift-Click to Punish (Kick)", NamedTextColor.DARK_RED));

                meta.lore(lore);
                head.setItemMeta(meta);
            }

            inv.setItem(slot++, head);
        }

        staff.openInventory(inv);
    }

    @EventHandler
    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getInventory().getHolder() instanceof NexoraGuiHolder)) {
            return;
        }

        event.setCancelled(true);

        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player staff)) {
            return;
        }

        ItemStack clicked = event.getCurrentItem();
        if (clicked == null || clicked.getType() != Material.PLAYER_HEAD) {
            return;
        }

        SkullMeta meta = (SkullMeta) clicked.getItemMeta();
        if (meta == null || meta.getOwningPlayer() == null) {
            return;
        }

        Player target = meta.getOwningPlayer().getPlayer();
        if (target == null || !target.isOnline()) {
            staff.sendMessage(Component.text("Target player is no longer online.", NamedTextColor.RED));
            staff.closeInventory();
            return;
        }

        PlayerData targetData = plugin.getPlayerDataManager().getData(target);
        if (targetData == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (event.isShiftClick()) {
            staff.closeInventory();
            plugin.getPunishmentManager().executePunishment(target, targetData, "Manual Staff Kick", PunishmentType.KICK, targetData.getTotalViolationLevel());
            staff.sendMessage(Component.text("Executed kick punishment on " + target.getName(), NamedTextColor.GREEN));
        } else if (event.isRightClick()) {
            targetData.resetViolations();
            staff.sendMessage(Component.text("Reset all violations for " + target.getName(), NamedTextColor.GREEN));
            openGui(staff); // Refresh GUI
        } else if (event.isLeftClick()) {
            staff.closeInventory();
            staff.teleport(target.getLocation());
            staff.sendMessage(Component.text("Teleported to " + target.getName(), NamedTextColor.GREEN));
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/license/LicenseManager.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.license;

import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.TaskUtil;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.minimessage.MiniMessage;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.time.Duration;

/**
 * Validates the server's license key against the remote Nexora Web Panel Licensing Authority.
 * Disables the plugin if the key is invalid, revoked, banned, or bound to another host.
 */
public class LicenseManager {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;
    private final HttpClient httpClient;

    private boolean verified = false;
    private String licensedOwner = "Unknown";
    private String licenseTier = "Unknown";

    public LicenseManager(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.httpClient = HttpClient.newBuilder()
                .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(4))
                .build();
    }

    /**
     * Synchronously validates the license on server startup.
     *
     * @return True if license is valid; false if invalid or banned.
     */
    public boolean verifyLicenseSync() {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig();
        boolean enabled = config.getBoolean("license.enabled", true);
        if (!enabled) {
            this.verified = true;
            return true;
        }

        String key = config.getString("license.key", "NX-DEMO-2026-KEY1");
        String apiUrl = config.getString("license.api-url", "http://localhost:3000/api");
        String serverName = config.getString("license.server-name", "Minecraft Server");

        MiniMessage mm = MiniMessage.miniMessage();

        try {
            JsonObject payload = new JsonObject();
            payload.addProperty("key", key);
            payload.addProperty("serverName", serverName);
            payload.addProperty("port", Bukkit.getPort());
            payload.addProperty("mcVersion", Bukkit.getVersion());
            payload.addProperty("pluginVersion", plugin.getPluginMeta().getVersion());

            HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                    .uri(URI.create(apiUrl + "/license/verify"))
                    .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                    .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(payload.toString()))
                    .build();

            HttpResponse<String> response = httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
            JsonObject json = JsonParser.parseString(response.body()).getAsJsonObject();

            boolean valid = json.has("valid") && json.get("valid").getAsBoolean();

            if (valid) {
                this.verified = true;
                this.licensedOwner = json.has("owner") ? json.get("owner").getAsString() : "Verified Customer";
                this.licenseTier = json.has("tier") ? json.get("tier").getAsString() : "LIFETIME";

                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━ <bold>Nexora License Verified</bold> ━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>"
                ));
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        " <green>✓ License Status: <bold>VALID & ACTIVE</bold></green>"
                ));
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        " <gray>Licensed To: <yellow>" + licensedOwner + "</yellow> | Tier: <aqua>" + licenseTier + "</aqua></gray>"
                ));
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        " <gray>Key: <white>" + key + "</white></gray>"
                ));
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>"
                ));

                // Start periodic telemetry heartbeat
                startHeartbeatTask();
                return true;
            } else {
                String reason = json.has("reason") ? json.get("reason").getAsString() : "UNKNOWN_ERROR";
                String message = json.has("message") ? json.get("message").getAsString() : "Verification rejected.";

                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━ <bold>NEXORA LICENSE ERROR</bold> ━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>"
                ));
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        " <red>✘ License Verification FAILED: <bold>" + reason + "</bold></red>"
                ));
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        " <yellow>Details: " + message + "</yellow>"
                ));
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        " <red>Nexora AntiCheat will now disable itself to protect integrity.</red>"
                ));
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        "<#FF0000>━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━</#FF0000>"
                ));

                Bukkit.getPluginManager().disablePlugin(plugin);
                return false;
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            boolean allowOfflineGrace = config.getBoolean("license.allow-offline-grace", true);
            if (allowOfflineGrace) {
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        "<yellow>[Nexora Licensing] Panel API unreachable (" + ex.getMessage() + "). Running in offline grace mode.</yellow>"
                ));
                this.verified = true;
                return true;
            } else {
                Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                        "<#FF0000>[Nexora Licensing] Failed to reach licensing server. Disabling plugin.</#FF0000>"
                ));
                Bukkit.getPluginManager().disablePlugin(plugin);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Schedules periodic heartbeat pings to the Web Panel every 60-120 seconds.
     * If the license key is banned or suspended remotely, shuts down the plugin.
     */
    private void startHeartbeatTask() {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig();
        long intervalSec = config.getLong("license.heartbeat-interval-seconds", 60L);
        String key = config.getString("license.key", "NX-DEMO-2026-KEY1");
        String apiUrl = config.getString("license.api-url", "http://localhost:3000/api");
        String serverName = config.getString("license.server-name", "Minecraft Server");

        TaskUtil.runAsyncTimer(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                JsonObject payload = new JsonObject();
                payload.addProperty("key", key);
                payload.addProperty("serverName", serverName);
                payload.addProperty("onlinePlayers", Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers().size());
                payload.addProperty("tps", Bukkit.getTPS()[0]);

                HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                        .uri(URI.create(apiUrl + "/license/heartbeat"))
                        .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(4))
                        .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(payload.toString()))
                        .build();

                HttpResponse<String> response = httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
                JsonObject json = JsonParser.parseString(response.body()).getAsJsonObject();

                boolean valid = json.has("valid") && json.get("valid").getAsBoolean();
                if (!valid) {
                    String reason = json.has("reason") ? json.get("reason").getAsString() : "BANNED";
                    MiniMessage mm = MiniMessage.miniMessage();
                    Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(mm.deserialize(
                            "<#FF0000><bold>[Nexora Security] Remote revocation received: " + reason + "! Disabling plugin...</bold></#FF0000>"
                    ));
                    Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> Bukkit.getPluginManager().disablePlugin(plugin));
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
                // Network hiccup - ignore single failed heartbeat
            }
        }, intervalSec * 1000L, intervalSec * 1000L);
    }

    public boolean isVerified() {
        return verified;
    }

    public String getLicensedOwner() {
        return licensedOwner;
    }

    public String getLicenseTier() {
        return licenseTier;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/punishment/BanManager.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.punishment;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.TaskUtil;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.minimessage.MiniMessage;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.player.AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerLoginEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.net.InetAddress;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Manages progressive bans, IP bans, kick-escalation tiers, and cracked-player enforcement.
 */
public class BanManager implements Listener {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;

    // Cache to prevent excessive DB queries during quick reconnects
    private final Map<UUID, BanRecord> activeBansCache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, IpBanRecord> activeIpBansCache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    // Tier durations in milliseconds
    private static final long TIER_1_DURATION = 24L * 60 * 60 * 1000L;         // 24 Hours
    private static final long TIER_2_DURATION = 7L * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000L;      // 7 Days
    private static final long TIER_3_DURATION = 30L * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000L;     // 30 Days

    public BanManager(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    /**
     * Inspects incoming logins to enforce active UUID bans, IP bans, and cracked-client policies.
     */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onPreLogin(AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent event) {
        UUID uuid = event.getUniqueId();
        String name = event.getName();
        InetAddress address = event.getAddress();
        String ip = address != null ? address.getHostAddress() : null;

        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig();

        // 1. Cracked Player Detection (if enabled and server is online-mode)
        boolean crackedDetectionEnabled = config.getBoolean("settings.cracked-detection.enabled", true);
        boolean serverIsOfflineMode = config.getBoolean("settings.cracked-detection.server-is-offline-mode", false);

        if (crackedDetectionEnabled && !serverIsOfflineMode && Bukkit.getOnlineMode()) {
            boolean isCracked = uuid.version() == 3;
            if (isCracked) {
                boolean permanentBan = config.getBoolean("settings.cracked-detection.permanent-ban-cracked", true);
                if (permanentBan) {
                    // Issue permanent ban + IP ban immediately
                    executeBanInternal(uuid, name, ip, "Cracked/Offline client detected on online-mode server", "CrackedDetection", "NexoraAC", 4, -1L);
                    if (ip != null) {
                        executeIpBanInternal(ip, uuid, name, "Cracked/Offline client detected", "NexoraAC");
                    }

                    // Dispatch Discord alert
                    dispatchDiscordPunishment(name, uuid.toString(), "BAN", "Permanent (Lifetime)", "CrackedDetection", "SECURITY", 999.0,
                            "Detected offline-mode UUID version 3 on an online-mode server. Permanent IP ban applied.", 0, 20.0, "Cracked/Offline", true, ip);

                    Component kickMsg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "punish.cracked-ban",
                            "<#FF0000><bold>NEXORA SECURITY</bold></#FF0000>\n\n<red>Premium Mojang account required.\nOffline / cracked clients are forbidden on this server.</red>",
                            Map.of()
                    );
                    event.disallow(AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent.Result.KICK_BANNED, kickMsg);
                    return;
                } else {
                    Component kickMsg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "punish.cracked-kick",
                            "<#FF0000><bold>NEXORA SECURITY</bold></#FF0000>\n\n<red>Premium Mojang account required.\nPlease buy Minecraft at minecraft.net.</red>",
                            Map.of()
                    );
                    event.disallow(AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent.Result.KICK_OTHER, kickMsg);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Enforce IP Ban
        if (ip != null) {
            try {
                IpBanRecord ipBan = plugin.getStorageManager().getDatabase() != null
                        ? plugin.getStorageManager().getDatabase().getActiveIpBan(ip)
                        : null;
                if (ipBan != null) {
                    Component ipBanMsg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "punish.ip-ban-screen",
                            "<#FF0000><bold>NEXORA ANTICHEAT</bold></#FF0000>\n\n<red>Your IP address is permanently banned.</red>\n<gray>Reason: <yellow><reason></yellow></gray>\n<gray>Banned by: <white><banner></white></gray>",
                            Map.of("reason", ipBan.reason(), "banner", ipBan.bannedBy())
                    );
                    event.disallow(AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent.Result.KICK_BANNED, ipBanMsg);
                    return;
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
            }
        }

        // 3. Enforce UUID Ban
        try {
            BanRecord ban = plugin.getStorageManager().getDatabase() != null
                    ? plugin.getStorageManager().getDatabase().getActiveBan(uuid)
                    : null;
            if (ban != null) {
                if (ban.isExpired()) {
                    // Ban expired — deactivate
                    plugin.getStorageManager().getDatabase().deactivateBansForPlayer(uuid);
                } else {
                    Map<String, String> placeholders = Map.of(
                            "reason", ban.reason(),
                            "duration", ban.tierDescription(),
                            "expires", ban.formattedExpiresAt(),
                            "banned_at", ban.formattedBannedAt(),
                            "banner", ban.bannedBy()
                    );
                    Component banMsg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "punish.ban-screen",
                            "<#FF0000><bold>NEXORA ANTICHEAT</bold></#FF0000>\n\n<red>You are banned from this server.</red>\n<gray>Reason: <yellow><reason></yellow>\nDuration: <aqua><duration></aqua>\nExpires: <gold><expires></gold>\nAppeal at our community Discord.</gray>",
                            placeholders
                    );
                    event.disallow(AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent.Result.KICK_BANNED, banMsg);
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
        }
    }

    /**
     * Handles punishment execution for kick violations, evaluating progressive ban thresholds.
     */
    public void processViolationKick(@NotNull Player player,
                                     @NotNull PlayerData data,
                                     @NotNull String checkId,
                                     double vl,
                                     @NotNull String details) {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig();
        boolean progressiveBans = config.getBoolean("settings.progressive-bans.enabled", true);
        int kicksBeforeBan = config.getInt("settings.progressive-bans.kicks-before-ban", 3);

        String ip = player.getAddress() != null ? player.getAddress().getAddress().getHostAddress() : null;
        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        String name = player.getName();
        boolean isCracked = uuid.version() == 3;
        String clientBrand = data.getClientBrand();
        int ping = player.getPing();
        double tps = Math.min(20.0, Math.round(Bukkit.getTPS()[0] * 10.0) / 10.0);

        if (!progressiveBans) {
            // Standard single kick
            kickPlayer(player, checkId, vl, 1, 1);
            dispatchDiscordPunishment(name, uuid.toString(), "KICK", null, checkId, "COMBAT", vl, details, ping, tps, clientBrand, isCracked, ip);
            return;
        }

        // Query current kick count asynchronously, then act
        plugin.getStorageManager().getKickCountAsync(uuid).thenAccept(currentKicks -> {
            int newKickCount = currentKicks + 1;
            plugin.getStorageManager().incrementKickCountAsync(uuid, name);

            if (newKickCount >= kicksBeforeBan) {
                // Progressive Ban Escalation!
                plugin.getStorageManager().getBanHistoryAsync(uuid, 50).thenAccept(banHistory -> {
                    int previousBanCount = banHistory.size();
                    int tier = Math.min(4, previousBanCount + 1);

                    Long durationMs = switch (tier) {
                        case 1 -> TIER_1_DURATION;
                        case 2 -> TIER_2_DURATION;
                        case 3 -> TIER_3_DURATION;
                        default -> null; // Permanent
                    };

                    String durationText = switch (tier) {
                        case 1 -> "24 Hours";
                        case 2 -> "7 Days";
                        case 3 -> "30 Days";
                        default -> "Permanent / Lifetime";
                    };

                    executeBanInternal(uuid, name, ip, "Exceeded maximum violation kicks (" + checkId + ")", checkId, "NexoraAC", tier, durationMs != null ? durationMs : -1L);

                    // IP Ban on permanent ban tier
                    boolean ipBanOnPerm = config.getBoolean("settings.bans.ip-ban-on-permanent", true);
                    if (tier >= 4 && ipBanOnPerm && ip != null) {
                        executeIpBanInternal(ip, uuid, name, "Permanent ban reached (Tier 4)", "NexoraAC");
                    }

                    // Reset kick count after ban
                    plugin.getStorageManager().resetKickCountAsync(uuid);

                    // Execute on entity thread
                    TaskUtil.runEntity(plugin, player, () -> {
                        Map<String, String> placeholders = Map.of(
                                "player", name,
                                "check", checkId,
                                "duration", durationText,
                                "tier", String.valueOf(tier)
                        );

                        Component banReason = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                "punish.progressive-ban-screen",
                                "<#FF0000><bold>NEXORA ANTICHEAT</bold></#FF0000>\n\n<red>You have been banned for suspicious modifications.</red>\n<gray>Check: <yellow><check></yellow>\nDuration: <aqua><duration></aqua> (Tier <tier>/4)\nExpires: Automatically after duration expires.</gray>",
                                placeholders
                        );
                        player.kick(banReason);

                        Component broadcast = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                                "punish.ban-broadcast",
                                "<prefix><red><player></red> <gray>was banned by <#FF0000>Nexora</#FF0000> for <aqua><duration></aqua> (<yellow><check></yellow>).</gray>",
                                placeholders
                        );
                        Bukkit.broadcast(broadcast);
                    });

                    // Dispatch Discord alert
                    dispatchDiscordPunishment(name, uuid.toString(), "BAN", durationText + " (Tier " + tier + ")", checkId, "ANTICHEAT", vl, details, ping, tps, clientBrand, isCracked, ip);
                });
            } else {
                // Warning kick (not yet banned)
                TaskUtil.runEntity(plugin, player, () -> {
                    kickPlayer(player, checkId, vl, newKickCount, kicksBeforeBan);
                });
                dispatchDiscordPunishment(name, uuid.toString(), "KICK", "Warning " + newKickCount + "/" + kicksBeforeBan, checkId, "ANTICHEAT", vl, details, ping, tps, clientBrand, isCracked, ip);
            }
        });
    }

    private void kickPlayer(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull String checkId, double vl, int currentKick, int maxKicks) {
        Map<String, String> placeholders = Map.of(
                "player", player.getName(),
                "check", checkId,
                "vl", String.format("%.1f", vl),
                "kicks", String.valueOf(currentKick),
                "max_kicks", String.valueOf(maxKicks)
        );

        Component kickReason = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                "punish.progressive-kick-reason",
                "<#FF0000><bold>NEXORA ANTICHEAT</bold></#FF0000>\n\n<red>Disconnected for suspicious gameplay (<yellow><check></yellow>).</red>\n<yellow>Warning: <gold><kicks></gold>/<gold><max_kicks></gold> kicks before an automatic ban!</yellow>",
                placeholders
        );
        player.kick(kickReason);

        Component broadcast = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                "punish.kick-broadcast",
                "<prefix><red><player></red> <gray>was kicked by <#FF0000>Nexora</#FF0000> (<yellow><check></yellow> | Kick <kicks>/<max_kicks>).</gray>",
                placeholders
        );
        Bukkit.broadcast(broadcast);
    }

    public void executeBanInternal(@NotNull UUID uuid,
                                   @NotNull String playerName,
                                   @Nullable String ipAddress,
                                   @NotNull String reason,
                                   @Nullable String checkId,
                                   @NotNull String banner,
                                   int tier,
                                   long durationMs) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        Long expiresAt = durationMs > 0 ? now + durationMs : null;
        Long durationObj = durationMs > 0 ? durationMs : null;

        BanRecord record = new BanRecord(
                0,
                uuid,
                playerName,
                reason,
                checkId,
                banner,
                now,
                expiresAt,
                durationObj,
                tier,
                true,
                ipAddress
        );

        plugin.getStorageManager().saveBanAsync(record);
    }

    public void executeIpBanInternal(@NotNull String ipAddress,
                                     @Nullable UUID uuid,
                                     @Nullable String playerName,
                                     @NotNull String reason,
                                     @NotNull String banner) {
        IpBanRecord record = new IpBanRecord(
                0,
                ipAddress,
                uuid,
                playerName,
                reason,
                System.currentTimeMillis(),
                banner,
                true
        );
        plugin.getStorageManager().saveIpBanAsync(record);
    }

    public void unbanPlayer(@NotNull String target) {
        plugin.getStorageManager().unbanPlayerAsync(target);
    }

    public void unbanIp(@NotNull String ipAddress) {
        plugin.getStorageManager().unbanIpAsync(ipAddress);
    }

    private void dispatchDiscordPunishment(@NotNull String playerName,
                                           @NotNull String playerUuid,
                                           @NotNull String action,
                                           @Nullable String durationText,
                                           @NotNull String checkId,
                                           @NotNull String category,
                                           double vl,
                                           @NotNull String details,
                                           int ping,
                                           double tps,
                                           @NotNull String clientBrand,
                                           boolean isCracked,
                                           @Nullable String ipAddress) {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig();
        if (!config.getBoolean("alerts.discord.enabled", false)) {
            return;
        }

        String webhookUrl = config.getString("alerts.discord.webhook-url", "");
        if (webhookUrl == null || webhookUrl.isBlank() || webhookUrl.contains("YOUR_WEBHOOK_URL_HERE")) {
            return;
        }

        boolean showIp = config.getBoolean("alerts.discord.show-ip-in-embed", false);
        int color = config.getInt("alerts.discord.embed-color", 16711680);

        plugin.getAlertManager().getDiscordSender().sendPunishmentAlertAsync(
                plugin,
                webhookUrl,
                playerName,
                playerUuid,
                action,
                durationText,
                checkId,
                category,
                vl,
                details,
                ping,
                tps,
                clientBrand,
                isCracked,
                ipAddress,
                showIp,
                color
        );
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/punishment/BanRecord.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.punishment;

import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Data model representing a ban entry for a player.
 */
public record BanRecord(
        int id,
        @NotNull UUID uuid,
        @NotNull String playerName,
        @NotNull String reason,
        @Nullable String checkId,
        @NotNull String bannedBy,
        long bannedAt,
        @Nullable Long expiresAt,
        @Nullable Long durationMs,
        int tier,
        boolean active,
        @Nullable String ipAddress
) {

    private static final DateTimeFormatter FORMATTER = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
            .withZone(ZoneId.systemDefault());

    public boolean isPermanent() {
        return expiresAt == null || expiresAt <= 0;
    }

    public boolean isExpired() {
        if (isPermanent()) {
            return false;
        }
        return System.currentTimeMillis() >= expiresAt;
    }

    public String formattedBannedAt() {
        return FORMATTER.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(bannedAt));
    }

    public String formattedExpiresAt() {
        if (isPermanent()) {
            return "Permanent (Lifetime)";
        }
        return FORMATTER.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(expiresAt));
    }

    public String tierDescription() {
        return switch (tier) {
            case 1 -> "24 Hours (Tier 1)";
            case 2 -> "7 Days (Tier 2)";
            case 3 -> "30 Days (Tier 3)";
            default -> "Permanent / Lifetime (Tier 4)";
        };
    }
}
```

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.punishment;

/**

public record IpBanRecord(
        int id,
        @NotNull String ipAddress,
        @Nullable UUID uuid,
        @Nullable String playerName,
        @NotNull String reason,
        long bannedAt,
        @NotNull String bannedBy,
        boolean active
) {

    private static final DateTimeFormatter FORMATTER = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
            .withZone(ZoneId.systemDefault());

    public String formattedBannedAt() {
        return FORMATTER.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(bannedAt));
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/punishment/PunishmentManager.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.punishment;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.api.NexoraPunishEvent;
import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.TaskUtil;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Executes setbacks, kicks, bans, and console commands when thresholds are exceeded.
 */
public class PunishmentManager {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;

    public PunishmentManager(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void executePunishment(@NotNull Player player,
                                  @NotNull PlayerData data,
                                  @NotNull String checkId,
                                  @NotNull PunishmentType type,
                                  double vl) {
        TaskUtil.runEntity(plugin, player, () -> {
            if (!player.isOnline()) {
                return;
            }

            NexoraPunishEvent event = new NexoraPunishEvent(player, checkId, type.name(), "Exceeded threshold for " + checkId);
            Bukkit.getPluginManager().callEvent(event);
            if (event.isCancelled()) {
                return;
            }

            Map<String, String> placeholders = Map.of(
                    "player", player.getName(),
                    "check", checkId,
                    "vl", String.format("%.1f", vl)
            );

            switch (type) {
                case SETBACK -> setback(player, data);
                case WARN -> {
                    Component warnMsg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "punish.warn",
                            "<prefix><red><bold>WARNING:</bold> Unfair advantage detected. Please disable any illegal modifications.</red>",
                            placeholders
                    );
                    player.sendMessage(warnMsg);
                }
                case KICK -> {
                    plugin.getBanManager().processViolationKick(player, data, checkId, vl, "Exceeded threshold for " + checkId);
                }
                case BAN -> {
                    String ip = player.getAddress() != null ? player.getAddress().getAddress().getHostAddress() : null;
                    plugin.getBanManager().executeBanInternal(
                            player.getUniqueId(),
                            player.getName(),
                            ip,
                            "Unfair advantages detected (" + checkId + ")",
                            checkId,
                            "NexoraAC",
                            4,
                            -1L
                    );
                    if (ip != null && plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig().getBoolean("settings.bans.ip-ban-on-permanent", true)) {
                        plugin.getBanManager().executeIpBanInternal(ip, player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), "Permanent ban (" + checkId + ")", "NexoraAC");
                    }

                    Component banReason = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "punish.ban-reason",
                            "<#FF0000><bold>NEXORA ANTICHEAT</bold></#FF0000>\n\n<red>You are permanently banned for cheating (<yellow><check></yellow>).</red>",
                            placeholders
                    );
                    player.kick(banReason);

                    Component broadcast = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "punish.ban-broadcast",
                            "<prefix><red><player></red> <gray>was permanently banned by <#FF0000>Nexora</#FF0000> for cheating.</gray>",
                            placeholders
                    );
                    Bukkit.broadcast(broadcast);
                }
                case COMMAND -> {
                    List<String> commands = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig().getStringList("punishments.commands");
                    for (String cmd : commands) {
                        String parsedCmd = cmd.replace("%player%", player.getName())
                                .replace("%check%", checkId)
                                .replace("%vl%", String.format("%.1f", vl));
                        Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), parsedCmd);
                    }
                }
                case BLOCK -> {
                    Component blockMsg = plugin.getConfigManager().getMessages().getComponent(
                            "punish.block",
                            "<prefix><red>Suspicious interaction blocked by <#FF0000>Nexora</#FF0000>.</red>",
                            placeholders
                    );
                    player.sendMessage(blockMsg);
                }
            }
        });
    }

    public void setback(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull PlayerData data) {
        Location safeLoc = data.getLastSafeGroundLocation();
        if (safeLoc != null && safeLoc.getWorld() != null) {
            TaskUtil.runEntity(plugin, player, () -> {
                data.registerTeleport();
                player.teleport(safeLoc);
            });
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/punishment/PunishmentType.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.punishment;

/**
 * Standard punishment actions supported by Nexora AntiCheat.
 */
public enum PunishmentType {
    SETBACK,
    WARN,
    KICK,
    BAN,
    COMMAND,
    BLOCK;

    public static PunishmentType fromString(String str, PunishmentType def) {
        if (str == null) return def;
        try {
            return valueOf(str.toUpperCase());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return def;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/storage/DatabaseStorage.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.storage;

import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.BanRecord;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.IpBanRecord;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Common contract for persistence providers (SQLite, MySQL).
 */
public interface DatabaseStorage {

    void init() throws Exception;

    void logViolation(ViolationRecord record) throws Exception;

    List<ViolationRecord> getHistory(UUID playerUuid, int limit) throws Exception;

    List<ViolationRecord> getHistoryByName(String playerName, int limit) throws Exception;

    List<String> getRecordedPlayerNames() throws Exception;

    // --- Ban & Progressive Punishment Methods ---

    void saveBan(BanRecord record) throws Exception;

    BanRecord getActiveBan(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception;

    BanRecord getActiveBanByName(String playerName) throws Exception;

    List<BanRecord> getBanHistory(UUID playerUuid, int limit) throws Exception;

    List<BanRecord> getBanHistoryByName(String playerName, int limit) throws Exception;

    void deactivateBansForPlayer(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception;

    void deactivateBansForPlayerName(String playerName) throws Exception;

    // --- IP Ban Methods ---

    void saveIpBan(IpBanRecord record) throws Exception;

    IpBanRecord getActiveIpBan(String ipAddress) throws Exception;

    void deactivateIpBan(String ipAddress) throws Exception;

    // --- Kick Counter Methods ---

    int getKickCount(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception;

    void incrementKickCount(UUID playerUuid, String playerName) throws Exception;

    void resetKickCount(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception;

    void resetKickCountByName(String playerName) throws Exception;

    void close();
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/storage/MySQLStorage.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.storage;

import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.BanRecord;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.IpBanRecord;
import com.zaxxer.hikari.HikariConfig;
import com.zaxxer.hikari.HikariDataSource;

import java.sql.*;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * High-performance remote MySQL database storage backed by HikariCP.
 */
public class MySQLStorage implements DatabaseStorage {

    private final String host;
    private final int port;
    private final String database;
    private final String username;
    private final String password;
    private final int maxPoolSize;

    private HikariDataSource dataSource;

    public MySQLStorage(String host, int port, String database, String username, String password, int maxPoolSize) {
        this.host = host;
        this.port = port;
        this.database = database;
        this.username = username;
        this.password = password;
        this.maxPoolSize = maxPoolSize;
    }

    @Override
    public void init() throws Exception {
        HikariConfig config = new HikariConfig();
        config.setJdbcUrl("jdbc:mysql://" + host + ":" + port + "/" + database + "?useSSL=false&allowPublicKeyRetrieval=true&serverTimezone=UTC");
        config.setUsername(username);
        config.setPassword(password);
        config.setMaximumPoolSize(Math.max(2, maxPoolSize));
        config.setPoolName("Nexora-HikariPool");

        this.dataSource = new HikariDataSource(config);

        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             Statement stmt = conn.createStatement()) {
            stmt.executeUpdate("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_violations (
                    id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
                    uuid VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL,
                    player VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
                    check_id VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
                    vl DOUBLE NOT NULL,
                    details TEXT,
                    ping INT NOT NULL,
                    timestamp BIGINT NOT NULL,
                    INDEX idx_uuid (uuid),
                    INDEX idx_player (player),
                    INDEX idx_timestamp (timestamp)
                ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4;
            """);

            stmt.executeUpdate("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_bans (
                    id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
                    uuid VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL,
                    player VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
                    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
                    check_id VARCHAR(32),
                    banned_by VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
                    banned_at BIGINT NOT NULL,
                    expires_at BIGINT,
                    duration_ms BIGINT,
                    tier INT NOT NULL,
                    active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                    ip VARCHAR(45),
                    INDEX idx_bans_uuid (uuid),
                    INDEX idx_bans_player (player),
                    INDEX idx_bans_active (active)
                ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4;
            """);

            stmt.executeUpdate("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_ip_bans (
                    id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
                    ip VARCHAR(45) NOT NULL,
                    uuid VARCHAR(36),
                    player VARCHAR(16),
                    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
                    banned_at BIGINT NOT NULL,
                    banned_by VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
                    active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                    INDEX idx_ip (ip),
                    INDEX idx_active (active)
                ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4;
            """);

            stmt.executeUpdate("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_kick_counts (
                    uuid VARCHAR(36) PRIMARY KEY,
                    player VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
                    kick_count INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                    last_kick_at BIGINT NOT NULL
                ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4;
            """);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void logViolation(ViolationRecord record) throws Exception {
        String sql = "INSERT INTO nexora_violations (uuid, player, check_id, vl, details, ping, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, record.playerUuid().toString());
            ps.setString(2, record.playerName());
            ps.setString(3, record.checkId());
            ps.setDouble(4, record.vl());
            ps.setString(5, record.details());
            ps.setInt(6, record.ping());
            ps.setLong(7, record.timestamp());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public List<ViolationRecord> getHistory(UUID playerUuid, int limit) throws Exception {
        List<ViolationRecord> results = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT uuid, player, check_id, vl, details, ping, timestamp FROM nexora_violations WHERE uuid = ? ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.setInt(2, limit);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                while (rs.next()) {
                    results.add(new ViolationRecord(
                            UUID.fromString(rs.getString("uuid")),
                            rs.getString("player"),
                            rs.getString("check_id"),
                            rs.getDouble("vl"),
                            rs.getString("details"),
                            rs.getInt("ping"),
                            rs.getLong("timestamp")
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    @Override
    public List<ViolationRecord> getHistoryByName(String playerName, int limit) throws Exception {
        List<ViolationRecord> results = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT uuid, player, check_id, vl, details, ping, timestamp FROM nexora_violations WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?) ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            ps.setInt(2, limit);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                while (rs.next()) {
                    results.add(new ViolationRecord(
                            UUID.fromString(rs.getString("uuid")),
                            rs.getString("player"),
                            rs.getString("check_id"),
                            rs.getDouble("vl"),
                            rs.getString("details"),
                            rs.getInt("ping"),
                            rs.getLong("timestamp")
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> getRecordedPlayerNames() throws Exception {
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT DISTINCT player FROM nexora_violations";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             Statement stmt = conn.createStatement();
             ResultSet rs = stmt.executeQuery(sql)) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                names.add(rs.getString("player"));
            }
        }
        return names;
    }

    @Override
    public void saveBan(BanRecord record) throws Exception {
        String sql = "INSERT INTO nexora_bans (uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, record.uuid().toString());
            ps.setString(2, record.playerName());
            ps.setString(3, record.reason());
            ps.setString(4, record.checkId());
            ps.setString(5, record.bannedBy());
            ps.setLong(6, record.bannedAt());
            if (record.expiresAt() != null) {
                ps.setLong(7, record.expiresAt());
            } else {
                ps.setNull(7, Types.BIGINT);
            }
            if (record.durationMs() != null) {
                ps.setLong(8, record.durationMs());
            } else {
                ps.setNull(8, Types.BIGINT);
            }
            ps.setInt(9, record.tier());
            ps.setInt(10, record.active() ? 1 : 0);
            ps.setString(11, record.ipAddress());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public BanRecord getActiveBan(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception {
        String sql = "SELECT id, uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip FROM nexora_bans WHERE uuid = ? AND active = 1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                if (rs.next()) {
                    return mapBanRecord(rs);
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    @Override
    public BanRecord getActiveBanByName(String playerName) throws Exception {
        String sql = "SELECT id, uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip FROM nexora_bans WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?) AND active = 1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                if (rs.next()) {
                    return mapBanRecord(rs);
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    @Override
    public List<BanRecord> getBanHistory(UUID playerUuid, int limit) throws Exception {
        List<BanRecord> results = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT id, uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip FROM nexora_bans WHERE uuid = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.setInt(2, limit);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                while (rs.next()) {
                    results.add(mapBanRecord(rs));
                }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    @Override
    public List<BanRecord> getBanHistoryByName(String playerName, int limit) throws Exception {
        List<BanRecord> results = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT id, uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip FROM nexora_bans WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?) ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            ps.setInt(2, limit);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                while (rs.next()) {
                    results.add(mapBanRecord(rs));
                }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    @Override
    public void deactivateBansForPlayer(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_bans SET active = 0 WHERE uuid = ?";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void deactivateBansForPlayerName(String playerName) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_bans SET active = 0 WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?)";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void saveIpBan(IpBanRecord record) throws Exception {
        String sql = "INSERT INTO nexora_ip_bans (ip, uuid, player, reason, banned_at, banned_by, active) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, record.ipAddress());
            ps.setString(2, record.uuid() != null ? record.uuid().toString() : null);
            ps.setString(3, record.playerName());
            ps.setString(4, record.reason());
            ps.setLong(5, record.bannedAt());
            ps.setString(6, record.bannedBy());
            ps.setInt(7, record.active() ? 1 : 0);
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public IpBanRecord getActiveIpBan(String ipAddress) throws Exception {
        String sql = "SELECT id, ip, uuid, player, reason, banned_at, banned_by, active FROM nexora_ip_bans WHERE ip = ? AND active = 1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, ipAddress);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                if (rs.next()) {
                    return new IpBanRecord(
                            rs.getInt("id"),
                            rs.getString("ip"),
                            rs.getString("uuid") != null ? UUID.fromString(rs.getString("uuid")) : null,
                            rs.getString("player"),
                            rs.getString("reason"),
                            rs.getLong("banned_at"),
                            rs.getString("banned_by"),
                            rs.getInt("active") == 1
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    @Override
    public void deactivateIpBan(String ipAddress) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_ip_bans SET active = 0 WHERE ip = ?";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, ipAddress);
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public int getKickCount(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception {
        String sql = "SELECT kick_count FROM nexora_kick_counts WHERE uuid = ?";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                if (rs.next()) {
                    return rs.getInt("kick_count");
                }
            }
        }
        return 0;
    }

    @Override
    public void incrementKickCount(UUID playerUuid, String playerName) throws Exception {
        String sql = """
            INSERT INTO nexora_kick_counts (uuid, player, kick_count, last_kick_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, 1, ?)
            ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                player = VALUES(player),
                kick_count = kick_count + 1,
                last_kick_at = VALUES(last_kick_at);
        """;
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.setString(2, playerName);
            ps.setLong(3, System.currentTimeMillis());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void resetKickCount(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_kick_counts SET kick_count = 0 WHERE uuid = ?";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void resetKickCountByName(String playerName) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_kick_counts SET kick_count = 0 WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?)";
        try (Connection conn = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement ps = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    private BanRecord mapBanRecord(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
        long exp = rs.getLong("expires_at");
        Long expiresAt = rs.wasNull() ? null : exp;
        long dur = rs.getLong("duration_ms");
        Long durationMs = rs.wasNull() ? null : dur;

        return new BanRecord(
                rs.getInt("id"),
                UUID.fromString(rs.getString("uuid")),
                rs.getString("player"),
                rs.getString("reason"),
                rs.getString("check_id"),
                rs.getString("banned_by"),
                rs.getLong("banned_at"),
                expiresAt,
                durationMs,
                rs.getInt("tier"),
                rs.getInt("active") == 1,
                rs.getString("ip")
        );
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (dataSource != null && !dataSource.isClosed()) {
            dataSource.close();
        }
    }
}
```
