`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/storage/SQLiteStorage.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.storage;

import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.BanRecord;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.IpBanRecord;

import java.io.File;
import java.sql.*;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Embedded SQLite database storage implementation for standalone deployments.
 */
public class SQLiteStorage implements DatabaseStorage {

    private final File dbFile;
    private Connection connection;

    public SQLiteStorage(File dbFile) {
        this.dbFile = dbFile;
    }

    private synchronized Connection getConnection() throws SQLException {
        if (connection == null || connection.isClosed()) {
            connection = DriverManager.getConnection("jdbc:sqlite:" + dbFile.getAbsolutePath());
        }
        return connection;
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized void init() throws Exception {
        Class.forName("org.sqlite.JDBC");
        try (Statement stmt = getConnection().createStatement()) {
            // Violations table
            stmt.executeUpdate("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_violations (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    uuid TEXT NOT NULL,
                    player TEXT NOT NULL,
                    check_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    vl REAL NOT NULL,
                    details TEXT,
                    ping INTEGER,
                    timestamp INTEGER NOT NULL
                );
            """);
            stmt.executeUpdate("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_nexora_uuid ON nexora_violations(uuid);");
            stmt.executeUpdate("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_nexora_player ON nexora_violations(player);");
            stmt.executeUpdate("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_nexora_timestamp ON nexora_violations(timestamp);");

            // Bans table
            stmt.executeUpdate("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_bans (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    uuid TEXT NOT NULL,
                    player TEXT NOT NULL,
                    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
                    check_id TEXT,
                    banned_by TEXT NOT NULL,
                    banned_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    expires_at INTEGER,
                    duration_ms INTEGER,
                    tier INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                    ip TEXT
                );
            """);
            stmt.executeUpdate("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bans_uuid ON nexora_bans(uuid);");
            stmt.executeUpdate("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bans_player ON nexora_bans(player);");
            stmt.executeUpdate("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bans_active ON nexora_bans(active);");

            // IP Bans table
            stmt.executeUpdate("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_ip_bans (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    ip TEXT NOT NULL,
                    uuid TEXT,
                    player TEXT,
                    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
                    banned_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    banned_by TEXT NOT NULL,
                    active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
                );
            """);
            stmt.executeUpdate("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ip_bans_ip ON nexora_ip_bans(ip);");
            stmt.executeUpdate("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ip_bans_active ON nexora_ip_bans(active);");

            // Kick counts table
            stmt.executeUpdate("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_kick_counts (
                    uuid TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    player TEXT NOT NULL,
                    kick_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                    last_kick_at INTEGER NOT NULL
                );
            """);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized void logViolation(ViolationRecord record) throws Exception {
        String sql = "INSERT INTO nexora_violations (uuid, player, check_id, vl, details, ping, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, record.playerUuid().toString());
            ps.setString(2, record.playerName());
            ps.setString(3, record.checkId());
            ps.setDouble(4, record.vl());
            ps.setString(5, record.details());
            ps.setInt(6, record.ping());
            ps.setLong(7, record.timestamp());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized List<ViolationRecord> getHistory(UUID playerUuid, int limit) throws Exception {
        List<ViolationRecord> results = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT uuid, player, check_id, vl, details, ping, timestamp FROM nexora_violations WHERE uuid = ? ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.setInt(2, limit);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                while (rs.next()) {
                    results.add(new ViolationRecord(
                            UUID.fromString(rs.getString("uuid")),
                            rs.getString("player"),
                            rs.getString("check_id"),
                            rs.getDouble("vl"),
                            rs.getString("details"),
                            rs.getInt("ping"),
                            rs.getLong("timestamp")
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized List<ViolationRecord> getHistoryByName(String playerName, int limit) throws Exception {
        List<ViolationRecord> results = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT uuid, player, check_id, vl, details, ping, timestamp FROM nexora_violations WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?) ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            ps.setInt(2, limit);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                while (rs.next()) {
                    results.add(new ViolationRecord(
                            UUID.fromString(rs.getString("uuid")),
                            rs.getString("player"),
                            rs.getString("check_id"),
                            rs.getDouble("vl"),
                            rs.getString("details"),
                            rs.getInt("ping"),
                            rs.getLong("timestamp")
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized List<String> getRecordedPlayerNames() throws Exception {
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT DISTINCT player FROM nexora_violations";
        try (Statement stmt = getConnection().createStatement();
             ResultSet rs = stmt.executeQuery(sql)) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                names.add(rs.getString("player"));
            }
        }
        return names;
    }

    // --- Ban Operations ---

    @Override
    public synchronized void saveBan(BanRecord record) throws Exception {
        String sql = "INSERT INTO nexora_bans (uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, record.uuid().toString());
            ps.setString(2, record.playerName());
            ps.setString(3, record.reason());
            ps.setString(4, record.checkId());
            ps.setString(5, record.bannedBy());
            ps.setLong(6, record.bannedAt());
            if (record.expiresAt() != null) {
                ps.setLong(7, record.expiresAt());
            } else {
                ps.setNull(7, Types.INTEGER);
            }
            if (record.durationMs() != null) {
                ps.setLong(8, record.durationMs());
            } else {
                ps.setNull(8, Types.INTEGER);
            }
            ps.setInt(9, record.tier());
            ps.setInt(10, record.active() ? 1 : 0);
            ps.setString(11, record.ipAddress());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized BanRecord getActiveBan(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception {
        String sql = "SELECT id, uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip FROM nexora_bans WHERE uuid = ? AND active = 1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                if (rs.next()) {
                    return mapBanRecord(rs);
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized BanRecord getActiveBanByName(String playerName) throws Exception {
        String sql = "SELECT id, uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip FROM nexora_bans WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?) AND active = 1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                if (rs.next()) {
                    return mapBanRecord(rs);
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized List<BanRecord> getBanHistory(UUID playerUuid, int limit) throws Exception {
        List<BanRecord> results = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT id, uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip FROM nexora_bans WHERE uuid = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.setInt(2, limit);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                while (rs.next()) {
                    results.add(mapBanRecord(rs));
                }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized List<BanRecord> getBanHistoryByName(String playerName, int limit) throws Exception {
        List<BanRecord> results = new ArrayList<>();
        String sql = "SELECT id, uuid, player, reason, check_id, banned_by, banned_at, expires_at, duration_ms, tier, active, ip FROM nexora_bans WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?) ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            ps.setInt(2, limit);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                while (rs.next()) {
                    results.add(mapBanRecord(rs));
                }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized void deactivateBansForPlayer(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_bans SET active = 0 WHERE uuid = ?";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized void deactivateBansForPlayerName(String playerName) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_bans SET active = 0 WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?)";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    // --- IP Ban Operations ---

    @Override
    public synchronized void saveIpBan(IpBanRecord record) throws Exception {
        String sql = "INSERT INTO nexora_ip_bans (ip, uuid, player, reason, banned_at, banned_by, active) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, record.ipAddress());
            ps.setString(2, record.uuid() != null ? record.uuid().toString() : null);
            ps.setString(3, record.playerName());
            ps.setString(4, record.reason());
            ps.setLong(5, record.bannedAt());
            ps.setString(6, record.bannedBy());
            ps.setInt(7, record.active() ? 1 : 0);
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized IpBanRecord getActiveIpBan(String ipAddress) throws Exception {
        String sql = "SELECT id, ip, uuid, player, reason, banned_at, banned_by, active FROM nexora_ip_bans WHERE ip = ? AND active = 1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, ipAddress);
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                if (rs.next()) {
                    return new IpBanRecord(
                            rs.getInt("id"),
                            rs.getString("ip"),
                            rs.getString("uuid") != null ? UUID.fromString(rs.getString("uuid")) : null,
                            rs.getString("player"),
                            rs.getString("reason"),
                            rs.getLong("banned_at"),
                            rs.getString("banned_by"),
                            rs.getInt("active") == 1
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized void deactivateIpBan(String ipAddress) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_ip_bans SET active = 0 WHERE ip = ?";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, ipAddress);
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    // --- Kick Counter Operations ---

    @Override
    public synchronized int getKickCount(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception {
        String sql = "SELECT kick_count FROM nexora_kick_counts WHERE uuid = ?";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
                if (rs.next()) {
                    return rs.getInt("kick_count");
                }
            }
        }
        return 0;
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized void incrementKickCount(UUID playerUuid, String playerName) throws Exception {
        String sql = """
            INSERT INTO nexora_kick_counts (uuid, player, kick_count, last_kick_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, 1, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(uuid) DO UPDATE SET
                player = excluded.player,
                kick_count = kick_count + 1,
                last_kick_at = excluded.last_kick_at;
        """;
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.setString(2, playerName);
            ps.setLong(3, System.currentTimeMillis());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized void resetKickCount(UUID playerUuid) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_kick_counts SET kick_count = 0 WHERE uuid = ?";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerUuid.toString());
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized void resetKickCountByName(String playerName) throws Exception {
        String sql = "UPDATE nexora_kick_counts SET kick_count = 0 WHERE LOWER(player) = LOWER(?)";
        try (PreparedStatement ps = getConnection().prepareStatement(sql)) {
            ps.setString(1, playerName);
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    private BanRecord mapBanRecord(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
        long exp = rs.getLong("expires_at");
        Long expiresAt = rs.wasNull() ? null : exp;
        long dur = rs.getLong("duration_ms");
        Long durationMs = rs.wasNull() ? null : dur;

        return new BanRecord(
                rs.getInt("id"),
                UUID.fromString(rs.getString("uuid")),
                rs.getString("player"),
                rs.getString("reason"),
                rs.getString("check_id"),
                rs.getString("banned_by"),
                rs.getLong("banned_at"),
                expiresAt,
                durationMs,
                rs.getInt("tier"),
                rs.getInt("active") == 1,
                rs.getString("ip")
        );
    }

    @Override
    public synchronized void close() {
        if (connection != null) {
            try {
                if (!connection.isClosed()) {
                    connection.close();
                }
            } catch (SQLException ignored) {
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/storage/StorageManager.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.storage;

import com.nexora.anticheat.NexoraPlugin;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.BanRecord;
import com.nexora.anticheat.punishment.IpBanRecord;
import com.nexora.anticheat.util.TaskUtil;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.logging.Level;

/**
 * Manages asynchronous logging to files and database persistence.
 */
public class StorageManager {

    private final NexoraPlugin plugin;
    private DatabaseStorage database;
    private File logFile;

    public StorageManager(@NotNull NexoraPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void init() {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfigManager().getConfig();
        String type = config.getString("storage.type", "SQLITE").toUpperCase();

        try {
            if ("MYSQL".equals(type)) {
                String host = config.getString("storage.mysql.host", "localhost");
                int port = config.getInt("storage.mysql.port", 3306);
                String databaseName = config.getString("storage.mysql.database", "anticheat");
                String user = config.getString("storage.mysql.username", "root");
                String pass = config.getString("storage.mysql.password", "");
                int poolSize = config.getInt("storage.mysql.pool.maximum-pool-size", 10);

                this.database = new MySQLStorage(host, port, databaseName, user, pass, poolSize);
            } else {
                File dbFile = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), config.getString("storage.sqlite.file-name", "nexora_violations.db"));
                this.database = new SQLiteStorage(dbFile);
            }
            this.database.init();
            plugin.getLogger().info("Successfully initialized " + type + " storage backend.");
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            plugin.getLogger().log(Level.SEVERE, "Failed to initialize database storage, falling back to SQLite: " + ex.getMessage(), ex);
            try {
                this.database = new SQLiteStorage(new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "nexora_violations.db"));
                this.database.init();
            } catch (Exception fallbackEx) {
                plugin.getLogger().log(Level.SEVERE, "Failed to initialize fallback SQLite storage!", fallbackEx);
            }
        }

        // Initialize log file
        File logsDir = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "logs");
        if (!logsDir.exists()) {
            logsDir.mkdirs();
        }
        this.logFile = new File(logsDir, "violations.log");

        // Warm up recorded player names cache
        refreshPlayerNamesCache();
    }

    private final java.util.Set<String> cachedPlayerNames = java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    public void refreshPlayerNamesCache() {
        TaskUtil.runAsync(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                if (database != null) {
                    cachedPlayerNames.addAll(database.getRecordedPlayerNames());
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
            }
        });
    }

    public java.util.Set<String> getCachedPlayerNames() {
        return java.util.Collections.unmodifiableSet(cachedPlayerNames);
    }

    public void logViolationAsync(@NotNull ViolationRecord record) {
        cachedPlayerNames.add(record.playerName());
        TaskUtil.runAsync(plugin, () -> {
            if (database != null) {
                try {
                    database.logViolation(record);
                } catch (Exception ex) {
                    plugin.getLogger().warning("Failed to persist violation record to DB: " + ex.getMessage());
                }
            }

            if (logFile != null) {
                try (FileWriter fw = new FileWriter(logFile, true);
                     PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(fw)) {
                    pw.printf("[%s] [VL: %.1f] %s (%s) failed %s: %s (Ping: %dms)%n",
                            record.formattedTime(),
                            record.vl(),
                            record.playerName(),
                            record.playerUuid(),
                            record.checkId(),
                            record.details(),
                            record.ping());
                } catch (Exception ignored) {
                }
            }
        });
    }

    public CompletableFuture<List<ViolationRecord>> getHistoryAsync(@NotNull UUID playerUuid, int limit) {
        CompletableFuture<List<ViolationRecord>> future = new CompletableFuture<>();
        TaskUtil.runAsync(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                future.complete(database != null ? database.getHistory(playerUuid, limit) : List.of());
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                plugin.getLogger().warning("Failed to fetch violation history: " + ex.getMessage());
                future.complete(List.of());
            }
        });
        return future;
    }

    public CompletableFuture<List<ViolationRecord>> getHistoryByNameAsync(@NotNull String playerName, int limit) {
        CompletableFuture<List<ViolationRecord>> future = new CompletableFuture<>();

                future.complete(database != null ? database.getHistoryByName(playerName, limit) : List.of());
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                plugin.getLogger().warning("Failed to fetch violation history by name: " + ex.getMessage());
                future.complete(List.of());
            }
        });
        return future;
    }

    // --- Ban Async Operations ---

    public CompletableFuture<Void> saveBanAsync(@NotNull BanRecord record) {
        CompletableFuture<Void> future = new CompletableFuture<>();

            try {
                if (database != null) {
                    database.saveBan(record);
                }
                future.complete(null);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                plugin.getLogger().warning("Failed to save ban record: " + ex.getMessage());
                future.completeExceptionally(ex);
            }
        });
        return future;
    }

    public CompletableFuture<Void> unbanPlayerAsync(@NotNull String playerNameOrUuid) {
        CompletableFuture<Void> future = new CompletableFuture<>();
        TaskUtil.runAsync(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                if (database != null) {
                    try {
                        UUID uuid = UUID.fromString(playerNameOrUuid);
                        database.deactivateBansForPlayer(uuid);
                        database.resetKickCount(uuid);
                    } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                        database.deactivateBansForPlayerName(playerNameOrUuid);
                        database.resetKickCountByName(playerNameOrUuid);
                    }
                }
                future.complete(null);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                plugin.getLogger().warning("Failed to unban player: " + ex.getMessage());
                future.completeExceptionally(ex);
            }
        });
        return future;
    }

        CompletableFuture<Integer> future = new CompletableFuture<>();

    }

        CompletableFuture<Void> future = new CompletableFuture<>();

    }

    public CompletableFuture<Void> resetKickCountAsync(@NotNull UUID playerUuid) {
        CompletableFuture<Void> future = new CompletableFuture<>();
        TaskUtil.runAsync(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                if (database != null) {
                    database.resetKickCount(playerUuid);
                }
                future.complete(null);
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
                future.complete(null);
            }
        });
        return future;
    }

    @Nullable
    public DatabaseStorage getDatabase() {
        return database;
    }

    public void close() {
        if (database != null) {
            database.close();
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/storage/ViolationRecord.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.storage;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Historical record of a logged anti-cheat violation.
 */
public record ViolationRecord(
        UUID playerUuid,
        String playerName,
        String checkId,
        double vl,
        String details,
        int ping,
        long timestamp
) {
    private static final DateTimeFormatter FORMATTER = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
            .withZone(ZoneId.systemDefault());

    public String formattedTime() {
        return FORMATTER.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(timestamp));
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/util/BedrockUtil.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.util;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

/**
 * Utility for detecting Bedrock (GeyserMC / Floodgate) players.
 *
 * <p>Detection priority:
 * <ol>
 *   <li>Floodgate API — most reliable; zero false-positives.</li>
 *   <li>Name prefix  — configurable string prepended by Geyser (default {@code "."}).</li>
 *   <li>Permission   — {@code nexora.bedrock} granted via Floodgate's group mapping.</li>
 * </ol>
 *
 * <p>Bedrock players receive relaxed thresholds on several checks because their client
 * physics differ from Java Edition (different tick rate handling, input latency, etc.).
 */
public final class BedrockUtil {

    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger("NexoraAC");

    /** Floodgate class name — loaded reflectively so the plugin starts without Floodgate. */
    private static final String FLOODGATE_API_CLASS = "org.geysermc.floodgate.api.FloodgateApi";

    private static boolean floodgatePresent = false;
    private static String bedrockPrefix = ".";

    /**
     * Cache of already-resolved Bedrock UUIDs to avoid repeated reflection calls per packet.
     * Entries are added on join, removed on quit.
     */
    private static final Set<UUID> bedrockCache = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    private BedrockUtil() {}

    /**
     * Initialises the detector. Must be called once on plugin enable, before any players join.
     *
     * @param prefix configured Bedrock name prefix (e.g. {@code "."})
     */
    public static void init(@NotNull String prefix) {
        bedrockPrefix = prefix == null ? "." : prefix;
        try {
            Class.forName(FLOODGATE_API_CLASS);
            floodgatePresent = true;
            LOG.info("[NexoraAC] Floodgate detected — Bedrock player detection via Floodgate API enabled.");
        } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
            if (!bedrockPrefix.isEmpty()) {
                LOG.info("[NexoraAC] Floodgate not found — using name prefix \"" + bedrockPrefix
                        + "\" + permission fallback for Bedrock detection.");
            } else {
                LOG.info("[NexoraAC] Floodgate not found and bedrock-prefix is empty — Bedrock bypass disabled.");
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Returns {@code true} if the player is a Bedrock (GeyserMC) client.
     * Result is cached per session for performance.
     */
    public static boolean isBedrock(@NotNull Player player) {
        return bedrockCache.contains(player.getUniqueId());
    }

    /**
     * Called on PlayerJoinEvent. Resolves and caches the Bedrock status for a player.
     */
    public static void onPlayerJoin(@NotNull Player player) {
        if (resolve(player)) {
            bedrockCache.add(player.getUniqueId());
        }
    }

    /**
     * Called on PlayerQuitEvent. Clears the cache entry.
     */
    public static void onPlayerQuit(@NotNull Player player) {
        bedrockCache.remove(player.getUniqueId());
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    private static boolean resolve(@NotNull Player player) {
        // 1. Floodgate API (via reflection — Floodgate JAR is optional)
        if (floodgatePresent) {
            try {
                Class<?> apiClass   = Class.forName(FLOODGATE_API_CLASS);
                Object   apiInst    = apiClass.getMethod("getInstance").invoke(null);
                if (apiInst != null) {
                    Object result = apiClass.getMethod("isFloodgatePlayer", java.util.UUID.class)
                                            .invoke(apiInst, player.getUniqueId());
                    if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(result)) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                // Reflection failed — fall through to prefix/permission
            }
        }

        // 2. Name prefix
        if (!bedrockPrefix.isEmpty() && player.getName().startsWith(bedrockPrefix)) {
            return true;
        }

        // 3. Permission node (e.g. granted by LuckPerms Floodgate extension)
        return player.hasPermission("nexora.bedrock");
    }

    /** @return whether the Floodgate plugin is installed */
    public static boolean isFloodgatePresent() {
        return floodgatePresent;
    }

    /** @return the configured Bedrock name prefix */
    public static String getBedrockPrefix() {
        return bedrockPrefix;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/util/BlockUtil.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.util;

import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;

/**
 * Utility for estimating block breaking speeds and validity based on tool and potion effects.
 */
public final class BlockUtil {

    private BlockUtil() {
        // Utility class
    }

    /**
     * Determines whether a block can be broken instantly (e.g. grass, flowers, torches, redstone wire).
     *
     * @param material Block material to check.
     * @return True if block breaks with 0 mining duration.
     */
    public static boolean isInstaBreak(Material material) {
        if (material == null) return false;
        return material.getHardness() <= 0.05F || material == Material.TORCH
                || material == Material.REDSTONE_WIRE
                || material == Material.TRIPWIRE
                || material.name().contains("FLOWER")
                || material.name().contains("SAPLING")
                || material.name().contains("MUSHROOM")
                || material.name().contains("CARPET");
    }

    /**
     * Computes the approximate minimum breaking duration (in milliseconds) for a player mining a block.
     *
     * @param player The mining player.
     * @param block  The target block.
     * @return The estimated minimum duration in milliseconds.
     */
    public static long getEstimatedBreakDurationMs(Player player, Block block) {
        if (block == null || isInstaBreak(block.getType())) {
            return 0L;
        }

        float hardness = block.getType().getHardness();
        if (hardness < 0) {
            // Unbreakable blocks like bedrock
            return Long.MAX_VALUE;
        }

        double speedMultiplier = 1.0;
        ItemStack tool = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();

        // Check tool efficiency
        if (tool != null && tool.getType() != Material.AIR) {
            int effLevel = tool.getEnchantmentLevel(Enchantment.EFFICIENCY);
            if (effLevel > 0) {
                speedMultiplier += (effLevel * effLevel + 1);
            }
        }

        // Haste effect
        PotionEffect haste = player.getPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.HASTE);
        if (haste != null) {
            speedMultiplier *= (1.0 + 0.2 * (haste.getAmplifier() + 1));
        }

        // Mining fatigue effect
        PotionEffect fatigue = player.getPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.MINING_FATIGUE);
        if (fatigue != null) {
            speedMultiplier *= Math.pow(0.3, Math.min(fatigue.getAmplifier() + 1, 4));
        }

        // Base ticks estimation: (hardness * 30) / speedMultiplier
        double estimatedTicks = (hardness * 30.0) / Math.max(0.1, speedMultiplier);
        return Math.max(50L, (long) (estimatedTicks * 50.0));
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/util/LocationUtil.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.util;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;

/**
 * Utility methods for inspecting blocks and bounding colliders around player locations.
 */
public final class LocationUtil {

    private LocationUtil() {
        // Utility class
    }

    /**
     * Checks if the block or neighboring blocks immediately beneath/around a location are solid ground.
     *
     * @param location The player location to check.
     * @return True if solid blocks are present near feet level.
     */
    public static boolean isNearGround(Location location) {
        if (location == null || location.getWorld() == null) {
            return false;
        }

        World world = location.getWorld();
        double x = location.getX();
        double y = location.getY();
        double z = location.getZ();

        // Sample bounding box beneath the player (0.3 radius, -0.5 to +0.1 height)
        for (double dx = -0.3; dx <= 0.3; dx += 0.3) {
            for (double dz = -0.3; dz <= 0.3; dz += 0.3) {
                for (double dy = -0.5; dy <= 0.1; dy += 0.3) {
                    Block block = world.getBlockAt(
                            (int) Math.floor(x + dx),
                            (int) Math.floor(y + dy),
                            (int) Math.floor(z + dz)
                    );
                    if (block.getType().isSolid()) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Checks if a location is in or directly adjacent to liquid (water, lava, bubble column).
     *
     * @param location Location to evaluate.
     * @return True if in or near liquid.
     */
    public static boolean isInOrNearLiquid(Location location) {
        if (location == null || location.getWorld() == null) {
            return false;
        }

        World world = location.getWorld();
        int bx = location.getBlockX();
        int by = location.getBlockY();
        int bz = location.getBlockZ();

        for (int x = -1; x <= 1; x++) {
            for (int y = -1; y <= 1; y++) {
                for (int z = -1; z <= 1; z++) {
                    Material mat = world.getBlockAt(bx + x, by + y, bz + z).getType();
                    if (mat == Material.WATER || mat == Material.LAVA || mat == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Checks if the player is standing on or near ice (regular ice, packed ice, blue ice, frosted ice).
     *
     * @param location Location to test.
     * @return True if near ice.
     */
    public static boolean isNearIce(Location location) {
        if (location == null || location.getWorld() == null) {
            return false;
        }

        World world = location.getWorld();
        int bx = location.getBlockX();
        int by = location.getBlockY();
        int bz = location.getBlockZ();

        for (int x = -1; x <= 1; x++) {
            for (int y = -2; y <= 0; y++) {
                for (int z = -1; z <= 1; z++) {
                    Material mat = world.getBlockAt(bx + x, by + y, bz + z).getType();
                    if (mat == Material.ICE || mat == Material.PACKED_ICE || mat == Material.BLUE_ICE || mat == Material.FROSTED_ICE) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Checks if the player is climbing (ladders, vines, weeping vines, twisting vines, scaffolding).
     *
     * @param location Location to test.
     * @return True if near climbable blocks.
     */
    public static boolean isClimbable(Location location) {
        if (location == null || location.getWorld() == null) {
            return false;
        }

        Block block = location.getBlock();
        Material mat = block.getType();
        return mat == Material.LADDER || mat == Material.VINE || mat == Material.SCAFFOLDING
                || mat == Material.WEEPING_VINES || mat == Material.TWISTING_VINES;
    }

    /**
     * Checks if a location is near slime blocks or honey blocks (bouncing mechanics).
     *
     * @param location Location to test.
     * @return True if near slime or honey.
     */
    public static boolean isNearSlimeOrHoney(Location location) {
        if (location == null || location.getWorld() == null) {
            return false;
        }

        World world = location.getWorld();
        int bx = location.getBlockX();
        int by = location.getBlockY();
        int bz = location.getBlockZ();

        for (int x = -1; x <= 1; x++) {
            for (int y = -2; y <= 0; y++) {
                for (int z = -1; z <= 1; z++) {
                    Material mat = world.getBlockAt(bx + x, by + y, bz + z).getType();
                    if (mat == Material.SLIME_BLOCK || mat == Material.HONEY_BLOCK) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/util/MathUtil.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.util;

import java.util.Collection;

/**
 * High-performance mathematical and statistical utilities used across anti-cheat detections.
 */
public final class MathUtil {

    private MathUtil() {
        // Utility class
    }

    /**
     * Normalizes an angle into the [-180, 180] degree range.
     *
     * @param angle The raw angle in degrees.
     * @return The normalized angle in degrees.
     */
    public static float normalizeAngle(float angle) {
        float normalized = angle % 360.0F;
        while (normalized <= -180.0F) {
            normalized += 360.0F;
        }
        while (normalized > 180.0F) {
            normalized -= 360.0F;
        }
        return normalized;
    }

    /**
     * Calculates the absolute minimal angular difference between two yaw angles.
     *
     * @param yaw1 The first yaw angle.
     * @param yaw2 The second yaw angle.
     * @return The absolute degree difference in [0, 180].
     */
    public static float getAngleDistance(float yaw1, float yaw2) {
        float diff = Math.abs(normalizeAngle(yaw1) - normalizeAngle(yaw2));
        if (diff > 180.0F) {
            diff = 360.0F - diff;
        }
        return diff;
    }

    /**
     * Calculates the horizontal distance between two coordinate pairs.
     *
     * @param x1 First X coordinate.
     * @param z1 First Z coordinate.
     * @param x2 Second X coordinate.
     * @param z2 Second Z coordinate.
     * @return The 2D Euclidean distance.
     */
    public static double distanceHorizontal(double x1, double z1, double x2, double z2) {
        double dx = x2 - x1;
        double dz = z2 - z1;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
    }

    /**
     * Calculates the statistical variance of a collection of numerical samples.
     *
     * @param data The collection of numbers.
     * @return The variance, or 0.0 if insufficient data.
     */
    public static double getVariance(Collection<? extends Number> data) {
        if (data == null || data.size() < 2) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        double sum = 0.0;
        for (Number num : data) {
            sum += num.doubleValue();
        }
        double mean = sum / data.size();

        double temp = 0.0;
        for (Number num : data) {
            double diff = num.doubleValue() - mean;
            temp += diff * diff;
        }

        return temp / (data.size() - 1);
    }

    /**
     * Calculates the statistical standard deviation of a collection of numerical samples.
     *
     * @param data The collection of numbers.
     * @return The standard deviation, or 0.0 if insufficient data.
     */
    public static double getStandardDeviation(Collection<? extends Number> data) {
        return Math.sqrt(getVariance(data));
    }

    /**
     * Clamps a value within specified boundaries.
     *
     * @param value The value to clamp.
     * @param min   The minimum allowed value.
     * @param max   The maximum allowed value.
     * @return The clamped value.
     */
    public static double clamp(double value, double min, double max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/util/RayTraceUtil.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.util;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

/**
 * Utility for ray tracing and calculating distance from player eye to entity bounding boxes.
 */
public final class RayTraceUtil {

    private RayTraceUtil() {
        // Utility class
    }

    /**
     * Calculates the minimum distance from a ray source (such as player's eyes) to an entity's bounding box.
     *
     * @param eyeLocation The origin location of the look ray.
     * @param target      The target entity.
     * @return The distance in blocks to the closest point of intersection on the box,
     *         or distance to the entity center if ray misses bounding box.
     */
    public static double getDistanceToBox(Location eyeLocation, Entity target) {
        if (eyeLocation == null || target == null) {
            return Double.MAX_VALUE;
        }

        BoundingBox box = target.getBoundingBox();
        Vector eyePos = eyeLocation.toVector();
        Vector direction = eyeLocation.getDirection().normalize();

        // Calculate ray-box intersection
        Vector hit = box.rayTrace(eyePos, direction, 10.0) != null
                ? box.rayTrace(eyePos, direction, 10.0).getHitPosition()
                : null;

        if (hit != null) {
            return eyePos.distance(hit);
        }

        // Fallback: closest point on the bounding box to eye position
        double clampedX = MathUtil.clamp(eyePos.getX(), box.getMinX(), box.getMaxX());
        double clampedY = MathUtil.clamp(eyePos.getY(), box.getMinY(), box.getMaxY());
        double clampedZ = MathUtil.clamp(eyePos.getZ(), box.getMinZ(), box.getMaxZ());
        Vector closest = new Vector(clampedX, clampedY, clampedZ);

        return eyePos.distance(closest);
    }

    /**
     * Calculates the angle in degrees between the player's view vector and the vector pointing to the target.
     *
     * @param eyeLocation Eye location of the attacker.
     * @param targetLocation Center/Eye location of the target.
     * @return Offset angle in degrees [0, 180].
     */
    public static double getAngleOffset(Location eyeLocation, Location targetLocation) {
        if (eyeLocation == null || targetLocation == null) {
            return 180.0;
        }

        Vector viewVector = eyeLocation.getDirection().normalize();
        Vector targetVector = targetLocation.toVector().subtract(eyeLocation.toVector()).normalize();

        double dot = viewVector.dot(targetVector);
        dot = MathUtil.clamp(dot, -1.0, 1.0);

        return Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(dot));
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/nexora/anticheat/util/TaskUtil.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat.util;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;

import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

/**
 * Universal scheduling utility supporting modern Paper and Folia regionized threading models.
 */
public final class TaskUtil {

    private static boolean isFolia = false;

    static {
        try {
            Class.forName("io.papermc.paper.threadedregions.RegionizedServer");
            isFolia = true;
        } catch (ClassNotFoundException ignored) {
            isFolia = false;
        }
    }

    private TaskUtil() {
        // Utility class
    }

    /**
     * Checks if the server environment is Folia.
     *
     * @return True if running on a Folia-based server.
     */
    public static boolean isFolia() {
        return isFolia;
    }

    /**
     * Runs a task asynchronously without blocking any tick loops.
     *
     * @param plugin   Plugin instance.
     * @param runnable Code to execute.
     */
    public static void runAsync(Plugin plugin, Runnable runnable) {
        if (isFolia) {
            Bukkit.getAsyncScheduler().runNow(plugin, task -> runnable.run());
        } else {
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(plugin, runnable);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Runs a delayed task asynchronously.
     *
     * @param plugin   Plugin instance.
     * @param runnable Code to execute.
     * @param delayMs  Delay in milliseconds.
     */
    public static void runAsyncLater(Plugin plugin, Runnable runnable, long delayMs) {
        if (isFolia) {
            Bukkit.getAsyncScheduler().runDelayed(plugin, task -> runnable.run(), delayMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        } else {
            long ticks = Math.max(1, delayMs / 50);
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLaterAsynchronously(plugin, runnable, ticks);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Runs a repeating task asynchronously.
     *
     * @param plugin      Plugin instance.
     * @param runnable    Code to execute.
     * @param initialMs   Initial delay in milliseconds.
     * @param intervalMs  Interval in milliseconds.
     */
    public static void runAsyncTimer(Plugin plugin, Runnable runnable, long initialMs, long intervalMs) {
        if (isFolia) {
            Bukkit.getAsyncScheduler().runAtFixedRate(plugin, task -> runnable.run(), initialMs, intervalMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        } else {
            long initialTicks = Math.max(1, initialMs / 50);
            long intervalTicks = Math.max(1, intervalMs / 50);
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimerAsynchronously(plugin, runnable, initialTicks, intervalTicks);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Executes code safely on the entity's thread (region thread in Folia or main thread in Paper).
     *
     * @param plugin   Plugin instance.
     * @param entity   Target entity.
     * @param runnable Code to execute on entity thread.
     */
    public static void runEntity(Plugin plugin, Entity entity, Runnable runnable) {
        if (isFolia) {
            entity.getScheduler().run(plugin, task -> runnable.run(), null);
        } else {
            if (Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()) {
                runnable.run();
            } else {
                Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, runnable);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Executes delayed code safely on the entity's thread (region thread in Folia or main thread in Paper).
     *
     * @param plugin   Plugin instance.
     * @param entity   Target entity.
     * @param runnable Code to execute.
     * @param ticks    Delay in server ticks.
     */
    public static void runEntityLater(Plugin plugin, Entity entity, Runnable runnable, long ticks) {
        if (isFolia) {
            entity.getScheduler().runDelayed(plugin, task -> runnable.run(), null, ticks);
        } else {
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(plugin, runnable, ticks);
        }
    }
}
```

```yml
  log: "<prefix><dark_gray>[DEBUG:<yellow><check></yellow>]</dark_gray> <gray><player>: <white><message></white></gray>"


```

`src/test/java/com/nexora/anticheat/ClickTimingTest.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat;

import com.nexora.anticheat.data.PlayerData;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.UUID;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class ClickTimingTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should track clicks and report CPS within temporal window")
    void testCpsCalculation() {
        PlayerData data = new PlayerData(UUID.randomUUID(), "TestPlayer");

        assertEquals(0.0, data.getCps(), 0.001);

        for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
            data.registerLeftClick();
        }

        assertEquals(15.0, data.getCps(), 0.001);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should detect macro click consistency with low variance")
    void testClickVariance() {
        PlayerData data = new PlayerData(UUID.randomUUID(), "MacroPlayer");

        // Simulate identical interval clicks (macro behavior)
        for (int i = 0; i < 25; i++) {
            data.registerLeftClick();
        }

        // Extremely fast repeated calls in the same millisecond have ~0 variance
        double variance = data.getClickIntervalVariance();
        assertTrue(variance < 5.0, "Expected low variance for rapid uniform clicks, got " + variance);
    }
}

```

`src/test/java/com/nexora/anticheat/DetectionLogicTest.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat;

import com.nexora.anticheat.util.MathUtil;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class DetectionLogicTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should detect excessive horizontal speed displacement")
    void testSpeedDisplacement() {
        double maxLegitSpeed = 0.36;

        double normalDelta = MathUtil.distanceHorizontal(10.0, 10.0, 10.25, 10.15);
        assertTrue(normalDelta <= maxLegitSpeed, "Normal speed should not exceed threshold");

        double speedHackDelta = MathUtil.distanceHorizontal(10.0, 10.0, 10.65, 10.55);
        assertTrue(speedHackDelta > maxLegitSpeed, "Speed hack delta should exceed threshold");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should test angular offset vectors for KillAura checks")
    void testVectorMath() {
        // Dot product of identical unit vectors yields 1.0 (0 degrees)
        double dotSame = 1.0;
        double angleSame = Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(dotSame));
        assertEquals(0.0, angleSame, 0.001);

        // Dot product of orthogonal vectors yields 0.0 (90 degrees)
        double dotOrthogonal = 0.0;
        double angleOrthogonal = Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(dotOrthogonal));
        assertEquals(90.0, angleOrthogonal, 0.001);

        // Dot product of opposite vectors yields -1.0 (180 degrees)
        double dotOpposite = -1.0;
        double angleOpposite = Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(dotOpposite));
        assertEquals(180.0, angleOpposite, 0.001);
    }
}

```

`src/test/java/com/nexora/anticheat/MathUtilTest.java`:

```java
package com.nexora.anticheat;

import com.nexora.anticheat.util.MathUtil;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class MathUtilTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should normalize angles into [-180, 180] degrees correctly")
    void testNormalizeAngle() {
        assertEquals(0.0F, MathUtil.normalizeAngle(0.0F), 0.001F);
        assertEquals(180.0F, MathUtil.normalizeAngle(-180.0F), 0.001F);
        assertEquals(-90.0F, MathUtil.normalizeAngle(270.0F), 0.001F);
        assertEquals(45.0F, MathUtil.normalizeAngle(405.0F), 0.001F);
        assertEquals(-45.0F, MathUtil.normalizeAngle(-405.0F), 0.001F);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should compute minimal angular distance between yaw values")
    void testGetAngleDistance() {
        assertEquals(10.0F, MathUtil.getAngleDistance(10.0F, 20.0F), 0.001F);
        assertEquals(20.0F, MathUtil.getAngleDistance(350.0F, 10.0F), 0.001F);
        assertEquals(180.0F, MathUtil.getAngleDistance(0.0F, 180.0F), 0.001F);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should calculate 2D Euclidean horizontal distance accurately")
    void testDistanceHorizontal() {
        assertEquals(5.0, MathUtil.distanceHorizontal(0.0, 0.0, 3.0, 4.0), 0.001);
        assertEquals(0.0, MathUtil.distanceHorizontal(10.5, -5.2, 10.5, -5.2), 0.001);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should calculate statistical variance and standard deviation correctly")
    void testVarianceAndStandardDeviation() {
        List<Double> identicalSamples = List.of(50.0, 50.0, 50.0, 50.0, 50.0);
        assertEquals(0.0, MathUtil.getVariance(identicalSamples), 0.001);
        assertEquals(0.0, MathUtil.getStandardDeviation(identicalSamples), 0.001);

        List<Double> variableSamples = List.of(10.0, 20.0, 30.0, 40.0, 50.0);
        // Variance for 10,20,30,40,50 is 250.0, stdev is sqrt(250) ≈ 15.811
        assertEquals(250.0, MathUtil.getVariance(variableSamples), 0.001);
        assertEquals(Math.sqrt(250.0), MathUtil.getStandardDeviation(variableSamples), 0.001);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should clamp values accurately within limits")
    void testClamp() {
        assertEquals(10.0, MathUtil.clamp(5.0, 10.0, 20.0), 0.001);
        assertEquals(15.0, MathUtil.clamp(15.0, 10.0, 20.0), 0.001);
        assertEquals(20.0, MathUtil.clamp(25.0, 10.0, 20.0), 0.001);
    }
}

```

`supabase_schema.sql`:

```sql
-- ==========================================================
--     NEXORA ANTICHEAT - SUPABASE POSTGRESQL SCHEMA
-- ==========================================================
-- You can run this script directly in your Supabase SQL Editor:
-- Dashboard -> SQL Editor -> New Query -> Paste & Run!
--
-- Note: The Nexora Panel backend also auto-creates these tables
-- automatically when connected via DATABASE_URL!
-- ==========================================================

-- 1. Licenses Table (cryptographic keys, tiers, owner, bound IP)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_licenses (
    key VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    owner VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(255),
    tier VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'LIFETIME',
    status VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    bound_ip VARCHAR(64),
    max_servers INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at BIGINT NOT NULL,
    expires_at BIGINT,
    ban_reason TEXT
);

-- 2. Registered Users Table (Auth with Username, Password Hash, Salt & License Key)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_users (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(64) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    salt VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    license_key VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES nexora_licenses(key) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    role VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER',
    created_at BIGINT NOT NULL,
    last_login BIGINT
);

-- 3. Minecraft Servers Fleet Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_servers (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    license_key VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES nexora_licenses(key) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    server_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    ip VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    port INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 25565,
    mc_version VARCHAR(128),
    plugin_version VARCHAR(64),
    online_players INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tps DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL DEFAULT 20.0,
    status VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ONLINE',
    last_heartbeat BIGINT NOT NULL,
    first_seen BIGINT NOT NULL
);

-- 4. User Controlled Player Bans Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_player_bans (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    license_key VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES nexora_licenses(key) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    server_name VARCHAR(255),
    player_name VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    duration VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PERMANENT',
    banned_by VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    ip_banned BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    banned_at BIGINT NOT NULL,
    expires_at BIGINT,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
);

-- 5. Telemetry & Cheat Violations Log Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS nexora_violations (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    license_key VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES nexora_licenses(key) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    server_name VARCHAR(255),
    player_name VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    check_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    vl DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL DEFAULT 1.0,
    details TEXT,
    timestamp BIGINT NOT NULL
);

-- Performance Indexes
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_username ON nexora_users(username);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_license ON nexora_users(license_key);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_servers_license ON nexora_servers(license_key);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_servers_status ON nexora_servers(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_player_bans_license ON nexora_player_bans(license_key);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_violations_license ON nexora_violations(license_key);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_violations_timestamp ON nexora_violations(timestamp DESC);

```
